Nhanganyaya

'Bhavishya Malika' muunganidzwa wemazana emabhuku akanyorwa makore anenge 600 apfuura nePanchasakhas (shamwari shanu/vabatsiri) vaIshe Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Mabhuku aya akaramba akavanzika kwemazana emakore, uye panguva iyoyo, vanhu vazhinji vakaramba vasingazivi nezvawo. Zvisinei, dutu guru rakarova Odisha muna 1999 rakakwezva pfungwa dzevanhu vazhinji ku 'Bhavishya Malika' nekuti zvainzi zviporofita zvine chekuita nedutu iroro zvakanga zvatonyorwa kare mumabhuku iwayo.

Zvisinei, sezvo yakanyorwa mumutauro weOdia, Bhavishya Malika yakaramba yakaganhurirwa kuOdisha panguva iyoyo. Hapana munhu kunze kweOdisha aiva neruzivo rwemabhuku aya. Asi zvishoma nezvishoma, nguva dzakachinja. Mugore ra2020, denda reCOVID-19 rakakwezva zvakare pfungwa dzevanhu kuBhavishya Malika. Kwakanga kuri mushure medenda iri chete apo kusimudzirwa nekuparadzirwa kwemabhuku eMalika kwakatanga kunze kweOdisha, uye maitiro aya akaramba achienderera mberi kubva ipapo.

Nekuda kweizvi, vanhu vasingaverengeki vakatanga kudzidza nezveBhavishya Malika mumakore mashoma apfuura. Zvisinei, sezvinowanzoitika, pamwe chete nekuparadzirwa kwakanaka uku, kududzira zvisizvo uye ruzivo rusiri rwechokwadi nezvemamwe mavhesi eMalika zvakatangawo kupararira. Semuenzaniso, vamwe vanhu vakatanga kuparadzira runyerekupe rwekuti zera reKaliyuga makore 10,000; kuti zita remambo wekupedzisira wePuri richava Mukunda Dev; kuti zviitiko zvikuru zveMalika nezviito zvinoparadza zvaMwari (Sanhaar-Leela) zvaIshe Kalki zvichaitika panguva inotevera yeMeena-Shani Yoga (nguva iri pakati pa2054-57); kuti dunhu reKhandagiri harisi muBhubaneswar asi riri kumwe; uye kuti kune makore mazhinji asara kusati kwatanga Satyayuga, nezvimwe.

Ruzivo rusiri rwechokwadi rwakadaro rwakatanga kugadzira kusahadzika kwakawanda mupfungwa dzevateveri vatsvene. Nekuda kwekushaikwa kwemhinduro chaiyo kune kusahadzika uku, kutenda kwavo muMalika kwakatanga kudzikira. Chinangwa chikuru chechinyorwa chino hachisi chekungopa vaverengi vatsva ruzivo rwechokwadi uye rwakakwana nezveBhavishya Malika asiwo kugadzirisa kusahadzika kunomuka mupfungwa dzevateveri vatsvene. Zvinotarisirwa kuti nekuwana ruzivo rwechokwadi maererano nezvinhu izvi zvakakosha, vakatendeka vachakwanisa kusimbisa kutenda kwavo muMalika.

1 Bhavishya Malika – Nhanganyaya Pfupi

1.1 Nhanganyaya

Pakati pezana ramakore rechi15 nerechi16, vatsvene vashanu vakuru vakaberekerwa muOdisha (Shishu Ananta Das, Yashobanta Das, Achyutananda Das, Balarama Das, naJagannatha Das) vakaumba mazana ezvinyorwa zvakadaro umo makanyorwa huprofita husingaverengeki. Zvinyorwa izvi zvakazozivikanwa se 'Bhavishya Malika'. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu, akaberekwa muna 1486 uye achionekwa sekuuya kaviri kwaBhagawan Sri Krishna naJaganmata Radharani, aive nehukama hwakadzama nevatsvene ava vashanu. Sri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu akavadaidza vashanu ava kuti 'Panchasakha' (Shamwari Shanu). Panchasakhas ava vakaumba zvinyorwa zveMalika nemurairo waMahaprabhu pachake. Kunze kwePanchasakhas, vamwe vatsvene vakuru vakati wandei vakaenderera mberi netsika yekunyora Malika. Semuenzaniso, mutendi Salabega akaberekwa muzana ramakore rechi17, Mutsvene Arkhit Das naMutsvene Hadidas vakaberekwa muzana ramakore rechi18, Mutsvene Bhima Bhoi wezana ramakore rechi19, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa wezana ramakore rechi20, nezvimwe.

Huprofita hwakadzama hwakanyorwa muzvinyorwa izvi zveMalika pamisoro yakadai sekupera kweKaliyuga, kuberekwa kwaIshe Kalki, kupambwa kwevakaipa, kudzivirirwa kwevatsvene, kusimbiswa kweDharma, nekusvika kweSatyayuga.

1.2 Ndiani aive Panchasakhas?

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, akanyora mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake zveMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ବଳବନ୍ତ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମେ ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Balabanta Yaśhobanta Ananta Achyuta,
Jagannātha Dāsa Pañchasakhā nāme khyāta.

Zvinoreva:
Balarama Das, Yashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, naJagannatha Das vakazozivikanwa sePanchasakha.

Zvinofanira kucherechedzwa kuti nhoroondo yePanchasakhas yakare kare. Pakupera kwenguva yega yega, Panchasakhas vanoburukira Pasi, vakaberekwa kubva muchikamu chaIshe, kupa mupiro wavo usingakosheki kubasa raIshe rekusimbisa Dharma zvakare.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora muzvinyorwa zvake zveMalika ‘Udaya Kahani’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ପଞ୍ଚକୁଳରେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଆଜ୍ଞାରେ।
ଯୁଗ ଯୁଗରେ ଯେ ଜନମ ହେଉ,
କୃଷ୍ଣଲୀଳାମାନ ମୁଖରେ ଗାଉ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ambhe Pañchasakhā pañchakulare,
janama hoilu Kṛṣhṇa ājñāre.
yuga yugare je janama heu,
Kṛṣhṇalīḷāmāna mukhare gau.

Zvinoreva:
Mundima iyi, Mutsvene Achyutananda Das anotaura: "Isu, shamwari shanu, tinoberekwa mumhuri shanu dzakasiyana nemurairo waIshe Krishna. Munguva yega yega, tinoberekwa tichimba zviito zvitsvene (Leelas) zvaKrishna kubva mumiromo yedu."

1.3 Kuzviratidza kwePanchasakhas Mumakore Mana

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, muzvinyorwa zvake zveMalika ‘Udaya Kahani’, anotsanangura mafomu akatorwa nevatsvene vashanu vakuru mune imwe neimwe yeYugas ina:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଦିଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ଅନ୍ତରେ, ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରବେଶ ହୋଇଲା ମହୀରେ।
ଅସୁର ବିନାଶ କରିବା ପାଇଁ, ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିଲେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣେ ଯାଇଁ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଋଷିତନୁ ବହିଲେ, କୃପାଜଳ ଋଷି ଗାଗର୍ବ ହେଲେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଯେ ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ନାରଦ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ କହିଲି ଭେଦ।
ରାମ ଜନମ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଗୁଣ କହିଲେ ନ ସରେ।
ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନଳ ନୀଳ ସୁଷେଣ, ଜାମ୍ବବ ହନୁ ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନ।
ତ୍ରେତୟା ଅନ୍ତେ ଦ୍ୱାପର ପ୍ରବେଶ, ଗୋପାଳ ମୂରତି ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବେଶ।
ପଞ୍ଚସଖାମାନେ ହେଲେ ଜନମ, ସୁବଳ ସୁବାହୁ ସୁଦାମ ଦାମ।
ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଘେନି ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ହେଲେ, ଦ୍ୱାପର ଲୀଳାମାନ ସାଙ୍ଗ କଲେ।
ଅବନୀ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ନାମ ସ୍ଥାପିଲେ, ହରିନାମରେ ଜଗତ ମୋହିଲେ।
ସ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଶୂଦ୍ର ଆଦି ପାଷାଣ୍ଡ ଯେତେ, ହରିନାମ ଶୁଣି ହେଲେ ମୁକତେ।
ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନମ ହେଲୁ, ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନାମ ବହିଲୁ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୋଇଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ମେଳ ବଳରାମରେ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ନାମରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ, କହିଦେଲୁ ବାବୁ ତୋର ଆଗେଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Adi Ananta Yugaṭi Antare, Satya Prabesha Hoilā Mahire.
Asura Bināsha Karibā Pāiñ, Prabhu Janamile Brāhmaṇe Jāiñ.
Pañchasakhā Ṛuṣi Tanu Bahile, Kṛupājala Ṛuṣi Gāgarba Hele.
Mārkaṇḍa Je Swayambhū Nārada, Pañchasakhā Janama Kahili Bheda.
Rāma Janama Tretayā Yugare, Tretayā Guṇa Kahile Na Sare.
Tretayāre Naḷa Nīḷa Suṣeṇa, Jāmbaba Hanu Pañchasakhāmāna.
Tretayā Ante Dwāpara Prabesha, Gopāḷa Mūrati Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Besha.
Pañchasakhāmāne Hele Janama, Subaḷa Subāhu Sudāma Dāma.
Shrībatsa Gheni Pañchasakhā Hele, Dwāpara Līḷāmāna Sāṅga Kale.
Abanī Madhyare Nāma Sthāpile, Harināmare Jagata Mohile.
Strī Shūdra Ādi Pāṣāṇḍa Jete, Harināma Shuṇi Hele Mukate.
Ambhe Pañchasakhā Janama Helu, Ananta Achyuta Nāma Bahilu.
Jagannātha Jashobanta Hoile, Pañchasakhā Meḷa Baḷarāmare.
Emanta Nāmare Ambhe Janama, Kahidelu bābu tora āgeṇa.

Zvinoreva:

Kureva kuti, munguva dzekare, pakupera kweAnanta Yuga apo Satyayuga yakatanga, Ishe Mukuru akaberekwa muchimiro cheBrahmin kuti aparadze madhimoni. Panguva iyoyo, vatsvene vashanu vakazviratidza kubva mumuviri waIshe pachake seVachenjeri: Kripajala, Garga (Gagarba), Markandeya, Swayambhu, naNarada.

Mushure meSatyayuga, muTretayuga, apo Ishe akaita mitambo yetsvene isingaverengeki mukuzvarwa Kwake saRama, shamwari idzi shanu dzakaburukira Pasi muchimiro chaNala, Nila, Sushena, Jambavan (Jambeba), naHanuman.

Pakupera kweTretayuga, Dwaparyuga yakasvika, uye Ishe akaberekwa muchimiro chemukomana wemombe, Shri Krishna. Munguva iyi, Subala, Subahu, Sudama, Dama, naShribatsa vakava Panchasakhas uye vakatora chikamu mumitambo yetsvene naShri Krishna.

Uyezve, muKaliyuga, apo Ishe akazviratidza mukuzvarwa Kwake saChaitanya, shamwari idzi shanu dzakaberekwa kuti dziparadze zita dzvene (Hari-naam), vachisimbisa nzira iri nyore uye yakapfava yeruponeso kune vose. MuKaliyuga, vakazozivikanwa seShishu Ananta Das, Achyutananda Das, Jagannatha Das, Yashobanta Das, naBalarama Das.

1.4 Mutsara Wakadzama wePanchasakhas Mumakore

Apo mudzidzi Ramadas akabvunza mazita chaiwo ePanchasakhas mumakore mana, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akapa tsanangudzo iyi yakadzama muzvinyorwa zvake zveMalika ‘Udaya Kahani’.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ଋଷି କୃପାଜଳ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ସଖାଟି ଅଟଇ ନଳ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁଦାମ ସଖାଟି ହୋଇ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲାଇ।
ଗାର୍ଗବ ଋଷି ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ହେଲେ, ତ୍ରେତୟା ଜାମ୍ବବ ନାମ ବହିଲେ।
ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ସୁବାହୁ ବ୍ରଜ ଅଟନ୍ତି, ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ନାମ କଳିରେ ପାନ୍ତି।
ସ୍ୱୟଂଭୂ ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସେ ଜାଣେ, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ଅଟେ ସେହି ସୁଷେଣ।
ଗୋପରେ ଶ୍ରୀବତ୍ସ ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ଜାତ, କଳି ଯୁଗରେ ନାମ ଶିଶୁ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗରେ ସଖା ନାରଦ ଋଷି, ତ୍ରେତୟାରେ ନୀଳ ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶି।
ଦାମ ଗୋପାଳ ଦ୍ୱାପର ଯୁଗରେ, ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ନାମ କଳିରେ।
ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଋଷି ଅଟେ ହନୁମାନ, ସୁବଳ ସଖାଟି ଦ୍ୱାପରେ ପୁଣ।
ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ନାମ କଳିରେ ହେଲେ, ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ନାମ ପ୍ରକାଶ କଲେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Satya Yugare Ṛuṣi Kṛupājala, Tretayā Sakhāṭi Aṭai Naḷa.
Dwāpare Sudāma Sakhāṭi Hoi, Kaḷi Yugare Achyuta Bolāi.
Gārgaba Ṛuṣi Satya Yugare Hele, Tretayā Jāmbaba Nāma Bahile.
Dwāpare Subāhu Braja Aṭanti, Jashobanta Nāma Kaḷire Pānti.
Swayambhū Satya Yugare Se Jāṇe, Tretayāre Aṭe Sehi Suṣeṇa.
Gopare Shrībatsa Dwāpare Jāta, Kaḷi Yugare Nāma Shishu Ananta.
Satya Yugare Sakhā Nārada Ṛuṣi, Tretayāre Nīḷa Nāma Prakāshi.
Dāma Gopāḷa Dwāpara Yugare, Baḷarāma Dāsa Nāma Kaḷire.
Mārkaṇḍa Ṛuṣi Aṭe Hanumāna, Subaḷa Sakhāṭi Dwāpare Puṇa.
Jagannātha Nāma Kaḷire Hele, Pañchasakhā Nāma Prakāsha Kale.

Kushandurwa kweZviziviso:
  • Achyutananda Das: Aive Muchenjeri Kripajala muSatyayuga, Nala muTretayuga, naSudama muDwaparayuga.
  • Yashobanta Das: Aive Muchenjeri Garga muSatyayuga, Jambavan muTretayuga, naSubahu muDwaparayuga.
  • Shishu Ananta Das: Aive Muchenjeri Swayambhu muSatyayuga, Sushena muTretayuga, naShribatsa muDwaparayuga.
  • Balarama Das: Aive Muchenjeri Narada muSatyayuga, Nila muTretayuga, naDama muDwaparayuga.
  • Jagannatha Das: Aive Muchenjeri Markandeya muSatyayuga, Hanuman muTretayuga, naSubala muDwaparayuga.

1.5 Masimba Akakosha neUnyanzvi hwePanchasakhas

Panchasakhas vaive vatsvene vanoziva zvose, vakazviziva, uye vakaziva Mwari. Vaive maYogis makuru, vatsvaki, nevatsvene, mumwe nemumwe achipfuura mune imwe nzvimbo yesainzi yemweya. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anotsanangura izvi muMalika yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଆଗମ୍ୟ ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ,
ଗାର କଟା ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଜାଣେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ଆଗତ ଅନାଗତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭଣେ,
ବଳରାମ ଦାସ ତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ବଖାଣେ।
ଭକ୍ତିର ଭାବ ଜାଣେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମହନ୍ତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Āgamya Bhāba Jāṇe Jashobanta,
Gāra Kaṭā Jantra Jāṇe Ananta.
Āgata Anāgata Achyuta Bhaṇe,
Baḷarāma Dāsa Tattwa Bakhāṇe.
Bhaktira Bhāba Jāṇe Jagannātha,
Pañchasakhā E Oḍiśā Mahanta.

Zvinoreva:
Pakati pePanchasakhas vakazvarwa muOdisha:
  • Yashobanta Das aive muzivi wezvakavanzika zvakadzama zvezvakare nezveramangwana (Agam-Nigam).
  • Shishu Ananta Das aive nyanzvi musainzi yemifananidzo yezvakavanzika nemichina (Yantra-Vigyan).
  • Achyutananda Das aive muoni wezvakare, zvazvino, nezveramangwana (Trikal-Darshi).
  • Balarama Das aive nyanzvi yakadzama yechokwadi chekupedzisira neuzivi (Tattwa-Vigyan).
  • Jagannatha Das aive asingaenzaniswi mumafungiro ekuzvipira kwakachena (Bhakti-Bhaav).

1.6 Nzvimbo Tsvene (Ashrams) dzePanchasakhas

Panchasakhas vakavamba Peethas dzavo nenzvimbo dzekuzviparadzanisa nadzo munzvimbo shanu dzakasiyana muOdisha. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anotsanangura izvi mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅଢ଼ଙ୍ଗେ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ, ବାଲିପାଟଣାରେ ରହିଲେ ଅନନ୍ତ।
ସାତ ଲହଡ଼ିରେ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ରହି, କ୍ଷେତ୍ରବରେ ବଳବନ୍ତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Nemāḷe Achyuta Aḍhaṅge Jashobanta, Bālipāṭaṇāre rahiḷe Ananta.
Sāta Lahaḍire Jagannātha rahi, Khetrabare Baḷabanta.

Zvinoreva:
  • Achyutananda Das akavamba chigaro chake kuNemalo (dunhu reCuttack).
  • Yashobanta Das akavamba Peetha yake kuAdhanga (dunhu reJagatsinghpur).
  • Shishu Ananta Das (Ananta) akavamba nzvimbo yake yevamonaki muBalipatna (dunhu reKhurda).
  • Jagannatha Das akavamba Ashram yake muSata Lahadi.
  • Balarama Das (Balabanta) akaita Shrikhetra (Puri) nzvimbo yake yekugara.

1.7 Panchasakhas – Kuzvarwa Kwoumwari kwaIshe

Panchasakhas vakataura kakawanda mumagwaro avo eMalika kuti ivo kuzvarwa kwakaparadzana (Anshavatar) kwaIshe Mukuru pachake. Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das anonyora mumagwaro ake 'Chumbak Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲିଣ କଳିଯୁଗେ ନାମ ସୁଦାମ ସଖା ଅଟନ୍ତି,
ଶ୍ରୀଅଙ୍ଗରୁ ଜାତ କଟିରୁ ଭକତ ଶୁଣ ହୋ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଚେତି।
ମୁଖୁଁ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ହୃଦୁଁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜାତ ବାହୁ ମୂଁଳୁ ବଳରାମ,
ରାଧାଙ୍କ ହାସ୍ୟରୁ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ଦାସ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରେ ଘେନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Achyuta boliṇa Kaḷiyuge nāma Sudāma sakhā aṭanti,
Shrī-aṅgaru jāta kaṭiru bhakata shuṇa ho Bāraṅga cheti.
Mukhuṃ Jashobanta hṛuduṃ ambhe jāta bāhu mūṃḷu Baḷarāma,
Rādhāṅka hāsyaru Jagannātha Dāsa Bāraṅga heture ghena.

Zvinoreva:
MuKaliyuga, Achyutananda Das ishamwari Sudama kubva kuDwaparayuga uye akazvarwa kubva muchiuno (Kati) chaIshe. Yashobanta Das akazvarwa kubva pamuromo mutsvene (Shri-mukh) waMahaprabhu. Shishu Ananta Das akabuda kubva pamoyo waIshe (Hriday), naBalarama Das akazvarwa kubva pamaoko aIshe (Bahu). Uyezve, Jagannatha Das akaburuka kubva pakuseka (Haasya) kwaAmai Radharani.

Kwete Shishu Ananta Das ji chete, asi Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyorawo nezveizvi mumagwaro ake eMalika, 'Chakada Madana'. Mahapurush anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ନିରାକାର ଦେହୁ ପାଞ୍ଚଟି ଜ୍ୟୋତି,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଅନନ୍ତ ପଞ୍ଚକ ମୂରତି।
ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ ଜଗନ୍ନାଥ ବଳରାମ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇଲୁ ଏ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ଧାମ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Nirākāra dehu pāṃchaṭi jyoti,
Achyuta Anaṃta paṃchaka mūrati.
Jashobaṃta Jagannātha Balarāma,
janama hoilu e martya dhāma.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kubva pamuviri wemwenje waIshe Asina Chimiro, isu vashanu Panchasakhas takaburuka, uye takazvarwa panyika ino pasi pemazita aAchyutananda, Shishu Ananta, Jashobanta, Jagannatha, na Balarama.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumagwaro eMalika, 'Barnatika':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଶରୀର ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତରୁ,
ପଞ୍ଚସଖା ଜନ୍ମ କହିଛି ଗୁରୁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shrī Kṛiṣhṇa sharīra paṃchabhūtaru,
paṃchasakhā janma kahichi guru.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kuzvarwa kwe Panchasakhas kwakaitika kubva kuzvinhu zvishanu zvemuviri waIshe Shri Krishna.

1.8 Zviprofita NeMurairo Woumwari

Ndima dzeMalika dzinoonekwa semashoko aMahaprabhu pachake. Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୁଁ କହୁଛି ବୋଲି ମୋର କଥା ନୁହେଁ ଆଦ୍ୟୁଁ ଖଞ୍ଜିଛନ୍ତି ଧାତା,
କର୍ମବାମ ହେଲେ କଳ୍ପନା ଗ୍ରାସିବ ପ୍ରକୃତି କାଟିବେ ଚିତା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Muṃ kahuchi boli mora kathā nuheṃ ādyuṃ khañjichanti dhātā,
Karmabāma hele kalpanā grāsiba prakṛuti kāṭibe chitā.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush anoti: "Chero chandinonyora, aya haasi mashoko angu. Izvi zvose zvakatarwa naIshe kubva pakutanga chaipo."

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das akaudzawo mudzidzi wake mu‘Patta Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ହେତୁରଖ ଯାକୁସାର,
ଭାବବିନୋଦିଆ ହରିଙ୍କ ଏ ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ସିନା ଅଗୋଚର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Āhuri kathāe shuṇare Bāraṅga heturakha jākusāra,
Bhābabinodiā Hariṅka e bāṇī aṭe sinā agochara.

Zvinoreva:
"Haiwa mudzidzi Baranga, teerera! Aya haasi angu, asi mashoko aIshe Shri Hari."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika, 'Anakara Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

ଛତିଶ ସଂହିତା ଅଠସ୍ତରୀ ଗୀତା ବଂଶାନୁ ସପ୍ତବିଂଶରେ,
ଉପବଂଶାନୁ ଦ୍ୱାଦଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ବେନି ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଶତ ଖଣ୍ଡ ରେ।
ପଦ ପଦ୍ୟାବଳି ଲକ୍ଷେକ ଯେ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ସବୁ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ମହିମା,
ତୋ ଆଗେ କହିଲୁଁ ବ୍ରଜ କୁମର ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ସାରସ୍ୱତ ସୀମା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chatisha saṃhitā aṭhastarī gītā baṃshānu saptabiṃshare,
upabaṃshānu dwādasha khaṃḍa beni bhabiṣhya shata khaṃḍa re.
Pada padyābali lakṣheka je graṃtha sabu Shrīkṛiṣhṇa mahimā,
to āge kahilu braja kumara brahma sāraswata sīmā.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anoti magwaro aakanyora anosanganisira 36 Samhitas, 78 Gitas, 27 Banshanucharitras, 24 Upabanshanucharitras, 100 magwaro eMalika, nemazana ezviuru zvemavhesi (padas). Mahapurush akataurawo kuti magwaro aya ose akanyorwa nekubwinya kana nyasha dzaIshe Shri Krishna.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀରଙ୍ଗା ଚରଣ ବିନୁ ନ ଜାଣଇ ନୀଳାଚଳେ ମନ ମୋର,
ତାଙ୍କ କଥାମାନ ଉଲ୍ଲେଖ କରଇ ନ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଛାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shrī raṃgācharaṇa binu na jāṇai nīlāchale mana mora,
tāṃka kathāmāna ullekha karai na kahe Achyuta chāra.

Zvinoreva:
Mune iyi sloka, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anoti pfungwa dzake hadzina kubatana nechinhu chero chipi zvacho kunze kwetsoka dzaIshe Mukuru. Pfungwa dzake dzinoramba dzakanyura muNilachala Dhama, Puri, kureva kuti, mukuzvipira kwerudo kuna Ishe. Anotaurawo kuti chero chaanonyora mu magwaro eMalika, ose mashoko chaiwo akataurwa naIshe; haana kunyora chero chinhu chekuzvidira kwake.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyorazve mumagwaro ake eMalika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଲା ଯେପରି,
ଆମ୍ଭେ ଲେଖିଲୁ ପୋଥି ସେହିପରି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Prabhunkara ājñā helā jepari,
āmbhe lekhilu pothi sehipari.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, sezvazvakanyatso kurairwa nekutemerwa naIshe Mukuru, ndakanyora magwaro eMalika nenzira iyoyo chaiyo.

Achyutanandaji akanyora mu 'Agata Bhabishya Prabandha Malika' yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ମୁଖରୁ ଯାହା ମୋତେ ଆଜ୍ଞା ହେଉଅଛି,
ସେହି ପଦମାନ ସବୁ ପୁରାଣେ ଲେଖୁଛି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shrī mukharu jāhā mote ājñā heuachi,
sehi padamāna sabu purāṇe lekhuchi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nemurairo wa Ishe Mukuru, nekutungamira Kwake chaiko, ndiri kunyora Malika. Chero chabva kuna Ishe, ndiri kutsanangura masloka iwayo muMalika Purana.

1.9 Malika Igwaro Rakavanzika uye Zviprofita Zvaro Zvakavanzwa

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mu‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋଗୀମାନେ ଯୋଗ ଅନ୍ତ ନ ପାଇବେ ଆଉ କେବା ସାମରଥା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Yogīmāne yoga anta na pāibe āu kebā sāmarathā.

Zvinoreva:
Kunyange maYogis makuru nevarume vakachenjera havagoni kuwana chakavanzika chezvinhu izvi zvakavanzwa kuburikidza neYoga, tisingatauri vamwe vanhu.

Achyutananda Das anonyorawo nezvechimiro chakavanzika chemagwaro:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଲଟ ପାଲଟ କକ୍ଷା ଏହି ଅଙ୍କ, କର ହୃଦେ ତୁହି ଠାବ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଲୀଳା ଶେଷ ଯେ ହୋଇବ, ମସିହାରେ ନାହିଁ ଠାବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Olaṭa pālaṭa kakshā ehi aṅka, kara hṛude tuhi ṭhāba.
Kaḷiyuga līḷā shesha je hoiba, masihāre nāhiṃ ṭhāba.

Zvinoreva:
Zviprofita zvakanyorwa zvichibva pakuverenga kwenhamba, uye nhamba idzi dzakavanzwa nekudzipindura. Zviitiko zvichaitika asi kwete zvichitevera makore ekarenda akajairika; zvinotevera zvakavanzika zviyero zvekufananidzira.

Nezve kuzvarwa kwaKalki, anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହେବୁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସନ୍ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବୁ।
ଗୁପତ ଭକତ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଯାହା,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ଏ ଅଟେ ଏହା।
ଗୁପତରେ କେତେ ଖେଳ କରିବୁ,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କକୁ ଚଳାଇ ଥିବୁ।
ମୋର ଭକତଟି ଜାଣିବେ ସେହି,
ଗୁପତ ଅଙ୍କ ବୋଲିଟି ଯେ ଏହି।
ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ,
କହୁଅଛନ୍ତି ସେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Gupatare ambhe janama hebu,
gupatare san sāla chālibu.
Gupata bhakata jāṇanti jāhā,
gupata aṅka e aṭe ehā.
Gupatare kete kheḷa karibu,
gupata aṅkaku chaḷāi thibu.
Mora bhakataṭi jāṇibe sehi,
gupata aṅka boliṭi ye ehi.
Gupata gupata ati gupata,
kahuachanti se dīna Achyuta.

Zvinoreva:
Kuzvarwa kwaIshe kuchava muchivande. Nguva nemakore zvichaenderera mberi nenzira yakavanzika. Vatsvene vakavanzika chete ndivo vachaziva izvi nekuti nhamba dzakavanzwa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika, 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଆନେ ନ କହିବୁ ତୁହି,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଗୀତରେ କହଇ ମୋ ପାଦକୁ ଥାଅ ଛୁଇଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata āne na kahibu tuhi,
dīna Achyuta je gītare kahai mo pādaku thāa chuiṃ.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, aya haasi mashoko akavanzika chete, asi mashoko akavanzika zvikuru ayo Achyutananda Das ji akanyora achirangarira Ishe nekufungisisa pamakumbo avo akaita selotus.

Mune rimwe gwaro, Mahapurush anoudza mudzidzi wake Ramadasa:

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେଲେ ତୋହ ମୋହ ଦେଖା,
ତେରଜଣ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଥିବୁ ଆମ୍ଭେ ପଞ୍ଚସଖା।
ଗୁପତ ଅତି ଗୁପତ ଅଟଇ ହେ ରାମ,
କହୁଅଛି ବ୍ରଜ ବାଳ ହେତୁରେ ତୁ ଘେନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kalki prakāsha hele toha moha dekhā,
terajaṇa madhye thibu āmbhe pañchasakhā.
Gupata ati gupata aṭai he Rāma,
kahuachi braja bāla heture tu ghena.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe Kalki vachazviratidza, kuratidzika kwavo kwoumwari kuchaitika, uye isu Panchasakhas tichawana mukana wakanaka wekushumira nekugara takazvipira pamakumbo avo. Asi mitambo iyi youmwari haingaitiki muchivande chete, asi nenzira yakavanzika zvikuru.

Mahapurush anonyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ଗୁପତ ଗୁପତ ଏ ମହା ଗୁପତ ଶୁଣ ତୁ ରେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର,
ବ୍ରହ୍ମା ଶଙ୍କରକୁ ଭେଦ ତ ନଥିବ ନ ଜାଣିବେ ଇନ୍ଦ୍ର ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Gupata gupata e mahā gupata shuṇa tu re Rāmachandra,
Brahmā Shaṅkaraku bheda ta nathiba na jāṇibe Indra Chandra.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, O mudzidzi Ramachandra, mashoko eMalika haasi akavanzika chete, asi akavanzika zvikuru. Kunyange Prajapati Brahma naIshe Shankar havazokwanisi kunzwisisa zvakavanzika zvavo, tisingatauri Ishe Indra naIshe Chandra.

Sant Bhima Bhoi akanyorawo pamusoro penyaya iyi mumagwaro ake eMalika, 'Stuti Chintamani':

Odia Sloka:

ସଂସାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ କେମନ୍ତେ ଜାଣିବେ ନର ଅଙ୍ଗେ ଦେହ ବହି,
ଗତ ଆଗତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଜଣାନାହିଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Saṃsāra madhyare kemaṃte jāṇibe nara aṅge deha bahi,
gata āgata yugara byabasthā samastaṃku jaṇānāhiṃ.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, varume nevakadzi vasingaverengeki munyika havazokwanisi kuwana ruzivo pamusoro pekurongeka kwenguva, kupera kweKali Yuga, Malika, nezviporofita zvayo. Avo chete vanopihwa nyasha naIshe, avo vaiva vateveri vaIshe munguva dzakapfuura, ndivo vachakwanisa kuziva nezvezviporofita izvi zvakadzama zvikuru.

Zvino mubvunzo unomuka, ndivanaani vateveri vaIshe ava vekare avo Ishe vachapa nyasha dzavo uye vachazotenda mumashoko eMalika, vachakwanisa kuziva nezvawo, uye vachava neruzivo rwakakwana pamusoro pekupera kweKali Yuga neKalki avatar!

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akapa mhinduro kune izvi mumagwaro ake eMalika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta'. Mahapurush anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

କୋଟିକେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଏ ରସ ତିନି ସସ୍ରେ ଗଣାସେହି,
ମହିମା ପ୍ରକାଶ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ସେହି ଦାସ ଆନେ ମୁଁ କହନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Koṭike goṭie jāṇaṃti e rasa tini sasre gaṇāसेhi,
mahimā prakāsha nishche sehi dāsa āne muṃ kahaṃti nāhiṃ.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, munhu mumwe chete mu crore (mamiriyoni gumi) ndiye achakwanisa kuziva nezvezviporofita izvi, uye huwandu hwese hwevanhu vakadaro munyika yose huchava 3,000 chete.

1.10 Zviporofita zveMalika hazvizokundikani

Bhavishya Malika inoumbwa nemashoko aIshe pachavo, saka hapana mubvunzo wekuti zvingakundikana. Mumagwaro eMalika ‘Chakada Madana’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ପୃଥିବୀ ଦୁଇଫାଳ ହେଲେ ହୋଇବ, ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମେ ଯିବ।
ପର୍ବତ ଶିଖରେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ମିଥ୍ୟା ନୁହଇଁ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତର ବାଣୀ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ କ୍ଷମତା କାହାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pṛuthibī duiphāḷa hele hoiba, pūrba sūrya abā pashchime jiba.
Parbata shikhare phuṭiba kaiñ, Achyuta bachana mithyā nuhaiñ.
Achyutara bāṇī aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ khyamatā kāhāra.

Zvinoreva:
"Nyika ingaparadzana kuita zvikamu zviviri, zuva rinobuda kumabvazuva ringabuda kumadokero, ruva relotus ringatumbuka pamusoro pegomo—asi mashoko aAchyutananda haagoni kunyepa. Akaita semutsara wakanyorwa padombo, uye hapana ane simba rekuaratidza kuti manyepo."

Mu ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’, anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ସୀତା ଠାକୁରାଣୀ ଅସତୀ ହୋଇବେ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ତିଳେ ନ ଟଳିବ ପାଷାଣର ଗାର ଏହି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Sītā ṭhākurāṇī asatī hoibe parbate phuṭiba kaiñ,
Achyuta bachana tiḷe na taḷiba pāṣāṇara gāra ehi.

Zvinoreva:
MuKaliyuga, vanhu vangatoshevedza Amai Sita kuti havana kuchena, uye ruva relotus ringatumbuka pamusoro pegomo, asi mashoko aAchyutananda Das haazokundikani, nokuti akaita semutsara wakavezwa padombo.

1.11 Mu ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, achitaura mashoko aIshe asina chimiro (Nirakar), anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଥା ଯେବେ ନ ହୋଇବ, ଆମ୍ଭ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ମିଥ୍ୟା ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

E kathā jebe na hoiba, ambha Shrīmukha mithyā heba.

Zvinoreva:
Mundima iyi, Ishe asina chimiro pachavo vanoti: "Kana mashoko eMalika akasaitika, ipapo muromo wangu mutsvene pachangu uchazoratidzwa kuti manyepo." Nokuti aya mashoko anobva pamuromo waIshe pachavo, haagoni kukundikana.

Mahapurush akanyora zvakare mu 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ବାଣୀ ପଥରର ଗାର ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ପୂର୍ବ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଅବା ପଶ୍ଚିମକୁ ଯିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ ଏହି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shrī Achyuta bāṇī patharara gāra parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
pūrba sūrya abā pashchimaku jiba mo bachana satya ehi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, zuva ringabuda kumadokero panzvimbo pekumabvazuva, ruva relotus ringatumbuka pamusoro pegomo, asi mashoko aAchyutananda haagoni kuchinjwa. Mashoko aAchyutananda akanyorwa padombo; ndiwo chokwadi chakakwana.

Kuzviratidza kwaAchyutananda Das ji, Sant Hadi Das, uyo akaberekwa muzana remakore rechi18 pedyo neChatia mudunhu reJajpur muOdisha, akanyorawo magwaro mazhinji eMalika.

Mumagwaro ake eMalika 'Kali Chautisha', akanyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳିବ ନାହିଁଟି ଏ କଥା ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହୋଇବ ଦିନେ,
ଟାକି ରହିଛନ୍ତି ଭରସା କରି ଭକତମାନେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ṭaliba nāhiṃṭi e kathā nishche hoiba dine,
ṭāki rahichaṃti bharasā kari bhakatamāne.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, zvinhu zvakanyorwa mumagwaro eMalika hazvizochinjwi zvachose – zvirokwazvo zvichaitika rimwe zuva. Vateveri vose vakamirira nekutenda zviitiko izvi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare mumagwaro ake eMalika, 'Agata Bhabishyat Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ ବାରମ୍ବାର,
ବୁଝାଏ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଧୀର।
ନମଣ ଏହାକୁ ଯେ ଆନ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ କରେ ଧ୍ୟାନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶଯିବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା,
ସାରିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକାଡୋଳା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Achyuta dāsa bārambāra,
bujhāe bhakataṃku dhīra.
Namaṇa ehāku je āna,
Achyuta dāsa kare dhyāna.
Kali kalmaṣha nāshajiba,
Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Anaṃta Achyutanka līlā,
sāribe prabhu chakāḍolā.

Zvinoreva:
Anonyora kuti ndiri kudzokorora kutsanangurira vateveri nemoyo murefu. Vateveri havafaniri kufunga kuti mashoko aya manyepo, nokuti Achyuta akanyora magwaro aya ari mukufungisisa. Zvivi nematambudziko eKali Yuga zvichaparadzwa, uye mitambo youmwari yaIshe Kalki yakasimbiswa. Mahapurush anonyora zvakare kuti mashoko ake haazochinjwi zvachose.

Mahapurush akanyora zvakare mune rimwe remagwaro ake eMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ଅମୃତ ଭକ୍ଷି ଯାଇପାରେ ପ୍ରାଣ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ନୋହିବ ଆନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Amṛita bhakṣhi jāipāre prāṇa,
Achyuta bachana nohiba āna.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kunyange chinhu chisingagoneki sekurasikirwa neupenyu panzvimbo pekuwana kusafa nekunwa mvura youpenyu (Amrit) chingaitika, asi mashoko aAchyutananda Das haagoni kunyepa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mu 'Udaya Kahani' yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଆନ ନୋହିବ,
କହିଲେ ଅପ୍ରାଧ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପାଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Achyuta bachana āna nohiba,
kahile aparādha nishche pāiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mashoko aAchyutananda Das haazonyepi. Chero ani zvake anoti mashoko aya manyepo zvirokwazvo achava mugoveri wechivi ichocho.

Mahapurush anonyora mu 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ମୋ ଶ୍ରୀମୁଖ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ପର୍ବତେ ଫୁଟିବ କଇଁ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗାଇଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହଇ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mo shrīmukha bāṇī anyathā nohiba parbate phuṭiba kaiṃ,
Nemāla baṭaku laya lagāiṇa dīna Achyutī kahai.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mashoko angu haazochinjwi, kunyange dai ruva relotus rakatumbuka pagomo! Mashoko aya akanyorwa ari mukufungisisa pasi pemuti weNemal banyan (Nemal Vata).

Mumagwaro eMalika 'Chakada Madana' pachayo, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀଫାଟି ଜଳୁପଛେ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଅନଳ,
ଯାହା କହିଛନ୍ତି ତାହା ସର୍ବଦା ଅଚଳ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mahīphāṭi jalupache pralaya anala,
jāhā kahichaṃti tāhā sarbadā achala.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, moto unobuda mushure mekunge nyika yapatsanuka ungaunza kuparadzwa (Pralaya) kunyika yose, asi mashoko angu haagoni kunyepa. Mashoko angu asingarambiki uye asingachinjiki.

2 Chinangwa cheMalika – Ruponeso rweVateveri Vatsvene

Kazhinji, vanhu vazhinji vanotya mushure mekuverenga zviporofita zveMalika vachizviramba se"nzira dzekutyisidzira." Zvisinei, kufunga uku kwakakanganisa zvachose. Chinangwa chikuru kudzivirirwa kwemweya nekutungamirirwa kwevateveri kuburikidza nekuchinja kwenguva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ହେତୁ ରସାଇବା ପାଇଁକି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶାହାସ୍ତ୍ର ପୁରାଣ କଲା,
କଳିକାଳଠାରୁ ବଳି କାଳ ଯହିଁ ହକ କଥାଟା ଲେଖିଲା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Hetu rasāibā pāiṃki Achyuta shāhāstra purāṇa kalā,
kalikālaṭhāru bali kāla jahiṃ haka kathāṭā lekhilā.

Zvinoreva:
Pakupera kweKaliyuga, kumutsa kuziva kwakavata kwevateveri, ari kunyora magwaro aya eMalika. Chinangwa ndechekuti muKaliyuga yakadzama, apo rima rekusaziva rinopararira kwose kwose, vateveri vaIshe vanogona kusvika kuna Mahaprabhu Kalki kuburikidza neMalika, zvichibvumira kubatana kwemuteveri naMwari. Nechikonzero ichi, ari kunyora zviporofita zvikuru mumagwaro eMalika kubva kuKaliyuga kusvika kuSangam Yuga (Nguva Yekuchinja) uye mberi muAnanta Yuga iri kuuya (Adi Satyayuga).

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ଅବନୀକୁ ଚେତେଇବା ପାଇଁ ଚେତାବନୀ,
ଅବନୀ ହିତେ ଲେଖା ଏ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Abanīku cheteibā pāiñ chetābanī,
Abanī hite lekhā e bhabishya bāṇī.

Zvinoreva:
Chinangwa chezviporofita zvakanyorwa neni mumagwaro eMalika kupa yambiro kunyika. Anotsanangura kuti zviporofita izvi zvakanyorwa kuti zvibatsire nyika.

Mahapurush anonyora zvakare mu ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୂର୍ବର ବାସନା ମୂଳେ ଜ୍ଞାନ ଉଦୟ ହେଲା,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହିତରେ ମାଳିକା ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଲେଖି ରଖିଲା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pūrbara bāsanā mūḷe jñāna udaya helā,
Bhaktanka hitare Māḷikā grantha lekhi rakhilā.

Zvinoreva:
Nekuda kwe'vasana' yekare (zvinyorwa), kureva kuzvipira kuna Ishe kubva pakuberekwa kwakapfuura, ruzivo rwakandivhenekera pakuberekwa uku, uye ipapo ndakanyora magwaro eMalika kuti zvibatsire nekugara zvakanaka kwevateveri.

Mahapurush anonyora zvakare mu ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ସତର୍କ ଘଣ୍ଟି ଯେ ଚେତାବନୀ ଏହି ଜାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa satarka ghaṇṭi je chetābanī ehi jāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Mashoko emabhuku eMalika ndeekuyambira; azive kuti ari muchimiro chenyevero.

Mahapurush akanyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଏହି ଚେତାବନୀ ପୁଣ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ଭକ୍ତ ହିତେ ପୁଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Sāraswata grantha ehi chetābanī puṇa,
bāraṇa beḷe prakāsha bhakta hite puṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Ndima dzeMalika dziri muchimiro chenyevero kuvanhu. Dzanyorwa kuti dzibatsire vatendi pakupera kweKaliyuga. Naizvozvo, zvinofanira kunge zvakajeka kwatiri kuti Malika haiparidzi kutya, asi inogadzira nzira yeruponeso. Chinangwa chega cheBhavishya Malika ndicho chakanakira nyika.

2.1 Malika haisi yevanhu vose

Tinofanira kuziva zvakanaka kuti Malika haisi yemunhu wose. VaPanchasakha Mahapurushas vakarambidza vadzidzi vavo mumabhuku eMalika kuti vasaparidze mashoko aya pamberi pemapenzi. Mahapurush akati izvi hazvisi zvemunhu wose; havasi vose vachakwanisa kuzvinzwisisa. Vatendi vaIshe chete ndivo vachakwanisa kuzvitenda.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ କଳିପୁଣି ଆଗତ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବାଣୀ,
ଭକତ ହିତରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲି ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଯେ ଭଣି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Lakshe pañchāashī grantha kaḷi puṇi āgata bhabishyabāṇī,
Bhakata hitare prakāsha karili dīna Achyuta je bhaṇi.

Zvinoreva:
Ndakanyora mabhuku anosvika 185,000 uye kunze kwaizvozvo, ndakanyora zviprofita zvakawanda zviri kuuya. Ndiri kuburitsa zvese izvi kuti zvibatsire vatendi.

Achyutananda Das anobva ati kumudzidzi wake Ramadas mubuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଏ ପରମତତ୍ତ୍ୱ ମୂର୍ଖକୁ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ତତେ ସପ୍ତବାର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି କହଇ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ପଞ୍ଚାଅଶୀ ତିନି ଅଠର ମାଳିକା କଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ରଖିଛି ଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଜନଙ୍କ ପୀୟୂଷ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra e paramatattwa mūrkhaku na kara prakāsha,
tate saptabāra niyama deuchi kahai dīna Achyuta.
Lakshe pañchāashī tini aṭhara mālikā kali prakāsha,
bāraṇa beḷaku e grantha rakhichi jñānī janaṅka pīyūṣa.

Zvinoreva:
Teerera, mudzidzi Ramadas, aya mashoko akavanzika. Usaataure pamberi pemapenzi, zvikasadaro uchatadza. Mabhuku aya ari muchimiro chenyevero kuvanhu ayo achange akaita senectar kuvatendi pakupera kweKaliyuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mu‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ହୋଇବ ଡାକିନୀ ଗୋଳ ପଡ଼ିବ ଚହଳ,
ଅବନୀ କମ୍ପିବ ସଦା ହୋଇ ଟଳଟଳ।
ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ମାନ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Hoiba ḍākinī goḷa paḍiba chahaḷa,
Abanī kampiba sadā hoi taḷataḷa.
Nānā utpāta māna antarikṣe heba,
Bhakta binā anya kehi jāṇi na pāriba.

Zvinoreva:
Pakupera kweKaliyuga, panguva yekugadzwa kweDharma, hondo inotyisa ichaitika, uye pachava nemhirizhonga Pasi. Pasi richadedera. Kusagadzikana kwakawanda kuchaitika mumhepo. Asi kunze kwevatendi, hapana mumwe achakwanisa kunzwisisa kuti aya ndiwo zviito zvaIshe zvinoparadza.

Mahapurush anoti kumudzidzi Ramadas mubuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାବୁରାମ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ କହିବୁ ନାହିଁ,
ଘୋର କଳିକାଳ ଟାହି ଯେ ଟାପରା ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା ହେବ ତହିଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shuṇa Bāburāma Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhanku kahibu nāhiñ,
Ghora Kaḷikāḷa ṭāhi je ṭāparā bānā nindā heba tahiñ.

Zvinoreva:
Haiwa mudzidzi Ramadas, usaudze mashoko ebhuku iri re‘Chakada Madana’ pamberi pemapenzi. MuKaliyuga yakadzika, mapenzi achavaseka, izvo zvichakonzera kushorwa kwaIshe.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare mubuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କୁ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇଟି ବିଷ ଜ୍ଞାନୀଙ୍କୁ ଅଟେ ପିୟୂଷ,
ଅଜ୍ଞାନୀ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଲେ ମୋ ଅମୃତେ ହେବ ବିଷ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mūrkhanku e grantha aṭaiṭi biṣa jñānīnku aṭe piyūṣa,
Ajñānī āgare prakāsha karile mo amṛute heba biṣa.

Zvinoreva:
Kumapenzi, mashoko aya eMalika akaita sechepfu, asi kune vakachenjera akaita se'Piyush' kana nectar. Kuudza mashoko aya kune vasingazivi kwakafanana nekusanganisa chepfu munectar.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare mu‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କହୁଛି ଏହି ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଅଟଇ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଏହା ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଗୁପତରେ ଖେଳ ହୋଇବ ତୋତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଗଣ୍ଠିଆ ଭକତ ଅଟଇ ଗଣ୍ଠି କଥାରେ ମନ,
ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ ପୁରାଣ।
ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ହୋଇବ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକତ,
ଶୁଣ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଦାସରେ ମୋର ବଚନ ସତ୍ୟ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shuṇa Rāmachandra kahuchi ehi niṣṭhā bachana,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa aṭai bhaktaṅka gaṇṭhidhana.
Bāraṇa beḷaku rakhichi ehā bhaktaṅka pāiṃ,
Gupatare kheḷa hoiba tote deli chetei.
Gaṇṭhiā bhakata aṭai gaṇṭhi kathāre mana,
Sātasha bāstarī parārddha bhabishyata purāṇa.
Dharma saṃsthāpana hoiba bhakta hebe mukata,
Shuṇa Rāmachandra dāsare mora bachana satya.

Zvinoreva:
Teerera mudzidzi Ramadas, mashoko aya angu eMalika akaita se'Ganthidhana' kana pfuma inokosha kuvatendi. Ishe achagadzira Dharma uye vatendi vachasunungurwa. Zviito izvi zvichaitika muchivande zvikuru. Ndinonyora mabhuku aya eMalika chete kuti vatendi vaponeswe. Achavabatsira mu‘Barana Bela’ kana pakupera kweKaliyuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare mubuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ନେମାଳ ବଟକୁ ଲୟ ଲଗେଇ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଖେଦା ଲେଖଇ।
ସାରସ୍ୱତ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଟଇ ଏହି,
ବେଳକୁ ରଖିଛି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ।
ଉତ୍ତମ ଶିଷ୍ୟରେ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ବାନା ନିନ୍ଦା କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Nemāḷa baṭaku laya lagei,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa khedā lekhai.
Sāraswata grantha aṭai ehi,
Beḷaku rakhichi bhaktaṅka pāiñ.
Uttama shiṣyare kara prakāsha,
Bānā nindā kahe Achyuta Dāsa.

Zvinoreva:
Pasi pemuti weBanyan weNemalo, ndiri kunyora bhuku iri reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’. Richabatsira vatendi pakupera kweKaliyuga. Ribudise chete pakati pevatendi vakanakisisa; zvikasadaro, pachava nekushorwa kwemashoko aya nekwaIshe.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora zvakare mu‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗି ଅଲଗାରେ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଭକ୍ତ ଗଣ୍ଠିଧନ ଜାଣ,
ଭକତ ଜାଣିବେ ଏହାର ଯେ ଭେଦ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଅଛି ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ଏ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ପ୍ରକାଶ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନେମାଳ ବଟ ଯେ ସାଧୁ ପଦାଶ୍ରିତ ଭଣେ ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Lāgi alagāre Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa bhakta gaṇṭhidhana jāṇa,
bhakata jāṇibe ehāra je bheda shūnyare achi pramāṇa.
Bāraṇa beḷare e grantha prakāsha mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
Nemāḷa baṭa je sādhu padāshrita bhaṇe pāmara Achyuta.

Zvinoreva:
Chakada Madana ipfuma kuvatendi. Pakupera kweKaliyuga, vatendi chete ndivo vachakwanisa kunzwisisa zvinhu zvayo nezvakavanzika zvayo. Usaibudise pamberi pemapenzi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mu‘Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚେତୁଆ ଭକତମାନଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ତିନି ଅଠର ଖଣ୍ଡ,
ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଲେଖିଲା ପାମର ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ଜାଣନ୍ତି ଦୁରୁଦଣ୍ଡ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chetuā bhaktamānaṅka nimante tini aṭhara khaṇḍa,
Bhabishya lekhilā pāmara Achyuta na jāṇanti durudaṇḍa.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush akanyora mabhuku eMalika ezviprofita anosvika 318 chete kuti abatsire vatendi vakamuka. Mapenzi nevatadzi havazokwanisi kuziva chakavanzika chemabhuku aya.

Akanyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ବିନାଶି ପ୍ରଭୁ ମେଦିନୀ ରଖିବେ,
ସନାତନ ଧର୍ମ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ପ୍ରଚାର କରିବେ।
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନରହରି,
ନାମ ଭଜଏ ଘୋର କଳିରେ ଯିବ ତରି।
ବିପଦ କାଳରେ ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ରକ୍ଷା ପାଇଁ,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଗ୍ରନ୍ଥ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ରଚଇ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mlechha bināshi Prabhu medinī rakhibe,
Sanātana Dharma nishche prachāra karibe.
Kaḷiyuga sheshare prakāsha Narahari,
Nāma bhajae ghora kaḷire jiba tari.
Bipada kāḷare bhaktaṅka rakṣā pāiñ,
Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa grantha Achyuta rachai.

Zvinoreva:
Pakupera kweKaliyuga, Ishe achazviratidza. Achaparadza vakaipa nevatadzi kuti adzivirire Pasi uye achagadzirazve Sanatana Dharma. Mahapurush akanyora kuti panguva iyoyo, kuti atungamirire vatendi uye kuti avadzivirire, ari kunyora mabhuku eMalika. Mundima iyi, Mahapurush akanyora zvakajeka kuti chinangwa chekunyorwa kweMalika ndechekudzivirira vatendi munguva yedambudziko uye kugadzira nzira yeruponeso rwavo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora zvakare mubuku rake reMalika, 'Chakada Madana':

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମ ସଂସ୍ଥାପନ ପାପ ନାଶନ,
ଖେଦା ଚେତାବନୀ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ଜ୍ଞାନ।
ସବୁ ଲେଖିଗଲି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ପାଇଁ,
ଭକ୍ତ ମୋହର ଯେ ସୋଦର ଭାଇ।
ଭକତ ମୋ ପଞ୍ଚ ପ୍ରାଣଟି ଜାଣ,
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ନିମନ୍ତେ ଖେଦା ଲିଖନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dharma saṃsthāpana pāpa nāshana,
khedā chetābanī bhabishya jñāna.
Sabu lekhigali bhaktanka pāiṃ,
bhakta mohara je sodara bhāī.
Bhakata mo pañcha prāṇaṭi jāṇa,
bhaktanka nimaṃte khedā likhana.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, zviprofita zvandakanyora mumabhuku angu eMalika maererano nekupera kwezvivi nekugadzwa kweDharma pakupera kweKaliyuga, zvese ndezvevatendi nekuti vatendi vakaita sevanun'una vangu. Vatendi ndivo mweya yangu mishanu (Pancha-prana), uye ndakanyora mabhuku ese aya eMalika chete nekuda kwavo.

Mahapurush akanyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ସାତଶ ବାସ୍ତରୀ ପରାର୍ଦ୍ଧ ଅଟଇ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ଜାଣ,
ଘୋର କଳିଯୁଗ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନ ଛନ୍ତି ଡର ଲାଗୁଅଛି ପୁଣ।
ମୂର୍ଖଙ୍କ ଆଗରେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ନ କର ନିୟମ ଦେଉଛି ମୁହିଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Sātasha bāstarī parārddha aṭai Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa jāṇa,
ghora kaliyuga duṣhṭajana chaṃti ḍara lāguachi puṇa.
Mūrkhanka āgare prakāsha na kara niyama deuchi muṃhi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mudzidzi Rama, mune 772 Parardhas kana zvitsauko mubuku iri reMalika rinonzi 'Chakada Madana'. Usaazivise pamberi pemapenzi. Kune vanhu vakaipa vakawanda muKaliyuga inotyisa; zviise kure navo vose.

Mu'Chakada Madana' pachayo, Mahapurush anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ମୂର୍ଖେ ନ କର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ନୋହିଲା ନୋହିଲା ପଡୁଥିବ ହୁରି ଭକ୍ତ ଜାଣିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shuṇa Rāmadāsa Chakaḍā Maḍāṇa mūrkhe na kara prakāsha,
nohilā nohilā paḍuthiba huri bhakta jāṇiba abashya.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Haiwa mudzidzi Ramadasa, usaazivise zviprofita izvi zveMalika pamberi pemapenzi. Vachati mashoko aya manyepo, kuti zviitiko izvi hazvizoitiki. Naizvozvo, zviudze chete kuvatendi; ivo chete ndivo vachakwanisa kuzvitenda.

3 Zviprofita zvitatu zveMalika Zvakaitika

Bhavishya Malika ine mashoko aMahaprabhu pachake, uye naizvozvo hazvishamisi kuti zviprofita zvisingaverengeki muMalika zvakaitika kusvika zvino. Tichakurukura zvimwe zviprofita zvakadaro newe zvakaratidza kuva zvechokwadi 100%.

3.1 Chiprofita Maererano naAtal Bihari Vajpayee Ji

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumabhuku ake eMalika 'Kalikalpa Gita' ne'Achyuta Thara':

Odia Sloka:

ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ ଯେବେ, କଳ୍କୀଙ୍କ ଲୀଳା ଲାଗିବ ତେବେ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ରାଜା, ଫେରିବ କିଛିତାହାର ତରିଜା।
କିଛି ଦିନ ଭୋଗ କରିବ ମହୀ, ତେର ଦିନ ସେହି ଛତ୍ର ଉଡ଼ାଇ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ହୋଇଲେ ଯେବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଯେ ରାଜା, ତାଙ୍କ ସମୟରେ କିଛି ହୋଇଲା ତରିଜା।
ତେରଦିନ ଛତ୍ର ସେହି ଉଡ଼େଇଣ ଦେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ମହୀ ଭୋଗ କଲେ।
ସେ ଅଟନ୍ତି ଅଟଳ ବିହାରୀ ବାଜପେୟୀ, ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ରାଜ ସିଂହାସନେ ବସିଲେକ ତହିଁ।
ପ୍ରଥମେ ତେର ଦିନ ପ୍ରଧାନ ମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ହେଲେ, ତାପରେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ବରଷ ଶାସନ କରିଲେ।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ପଥରର ଗାର, ଆନ କରିବାକୁ ନାହିଁ ଶକତି କାହାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Tera dina pakṣa hoiba jebe, Kaḷkīṅka līḷā lāgiba tebe.
Khetratre hoiba Brāhmaṇa rājā, pheriba kichhitāhāra tarijā.
Kichhi dina bhoga kariba mahī, tera dina sehi chhatra uḍāi.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Khetratre hoile jebe Brāhmaṇa je rājā, tāṅka samayare kichhi hoilā tarijā.
Teradina chhatra sehi uḍeiṇa dele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa mahī bhoga kale.
Se aṭanti Aṭala Bihārī Bājapeyī, Dillī rāja siṃhāsane basileka tahiñ.
Prathame tera dina pradhāna mantrī hele, tāpare pāñcha baraṣa shāsana karile.
Achyuta bachana aṭe patharara gāra, āna karibāku nāhiñ shakati kāhāra.

Zvinoreva:
Mazana emakore apfuura, Mahapurush akanyora nezvemutungamiriri wezvematongerwo enyika anokosha weIndia yakazvimirira. Akataura kuti apo muBrahmin achitonga India, shanduko dzakawanda dzichaonekwa munyika. Kutanga, achatonga kwemazuva 13, uye gare gare achabata simba kwemakore mashanu. Mahapurush akanyora kuti zita remunhu uyu anotonga kubva kuDelhi richange riri "Atal Bihari Vajpayee". Sezvinoratidzwa nenhoroondo, Shri Vajpayee akashanda sePM kwemazuva 13 muna 1996 uye ipapo kwenguva yakazara yemakore 5 kubva muna 1999 kusvika 2004.

3.2 Chiprofita Maererano naMahatma Gandhi

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, makore 600 apfuura mubuku rake rine mukurumbira 'Shiva-kalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta', akaita chiprofita chakakura maererano naMahatma Gandhi.

Odia Sloka:

ଜନମ ହୋଇ ଗୁଜରାଟରେ, ମୋହନ ମୋହିବ ରାମ ନାମରେ।
ଅହିଂସାକୁ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର କରିବ ଜାଣ, ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ ହଟିବେ ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣ।
ତା ଅନ୍ତେ ଧର୍ମ ହୋଇବ ହାଣି, ମୋହନ ରାଜୁତି ହୋଇବ ହାଣି।
ଗୁଳି ଚୋଟେ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଯିବ, ଗଡସେ କଳିମଞ୍ଜି ପୋତିବ।
ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବେ ସଂସାର ପ୍ରାଣୀ, ଟେକି କହୁଥିବେ କଥା ନ ଜାଣି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Janama hoi Gujarāṭare, Mohana mohiba Rāma nāmare.
Ahinsāku astra kariba jāṇa, phiriṅgī haṭibe se kāḷe jāṇa.
Tā ante dharma hoiba hāṇi, Mohana rājuti hoiba hāṇi.
Guḷi choṭe tāra prāṇa jiba, Gaḍse kaḷimañji potiba.
Jāṇi na pāribe saṃsāra prāṇī, ṭeki kahuthibe kathā na jāṇi.

Zvinoreva:
"Achazvarirwa mudunhu reGujarat. Zita rake richange riri 'Mohan' uye achagara achidana zita raRama. 'Ahimsa' (Kusaita Chisimba) ichave chombo chake, uye nekuda kwekuedza kwake, maBritish (Firingis) vachamanikidzwa kusiya India."

Mahapurush akawedzera kunyora kuti Mohan haaizobata simba iye pachake, uye mushure mekufa kwake, paizoita kuderera kweDharma. Akanyatsocherechedza kuti munhu anonzi "Godse" aizoita kuti afe ne "bhurakisi" (Guli), uye nokuita izvozvo, aizoisa mbeu dzeKali munyika. Akatofanotaura kuti vanhu vazhinji vaizotadza kunzwisisa Gandhi zvakanaka uye vaizoruramisa chiito chemhondi nekusaziva. Sezvatinoziva, shoko rega rega rechiporofita ichi pamusoro peBaba veNyika rakazadzika.

3.3 Chiporofita cheDutuvhuta Guru ra1999 uye Kuputika kweKalpavata

Murugwaro rwake 'Jai Birachit Chautisha', Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akafanotaura dutuvhuta ra1999 makore 600 pamberi.

Odia Sloka:

ସମୁଦ୍ର ଉଠି ଆସିବ ବତାସ ବହିବ, ଶ୍ରୀ କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଭାଙ୍ଗି ପକାଇବ।
ସତ୍ୟଭାବ ହେ, ସାଲ ଅନେଶତ କର ଠାବ ହେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Samudra uṭhi āsiba batāsa bahiba, Shrī Kaḷpabaṭa mora bhāṅgi pakāiba.
Satyabhāba he, sāla aneshata kara ṭhāba he.

Zvinoreva:
"Gungwa richakwira uye mhepo ine simba ichavhuvhuta. Bazi reKalpavata tsvene (muti weBanyan wekare mukati menzvimbo yeTemberi yeJagannath) richatyoka." Mahapurush akataura kuti chiitiko ichi chaizoitika mugore "Anesata" (99).

Musi waGumiguru 29, 1999, Dutuvhuta Guru rakarova Odisha nemhepo ye 250 km/h. Rakakonzera kuparadzwa kukuru, richitora hupenyu hwevanhu vanopfuura 10,000. Zvichokwadi kuchiporofita, bazi guru remuti weKalpavata wekare mukati metemberi yePuri rakatyoka panguva yedutu iri.

3.4 Chiporofita cheKuzvitonga kweIndia (1947)

Murugwaro 'Garuda Kalpa', Mutsvene Achyutananda akafanotaura kuti India yaizosununguka kubva kuhutongi hweBritish muna 1947 achishandisa kodhi yenhamba.

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ରହ୍ମ ଭକ୍ତି ବେଦ ବାରକୁ ଥୋଇ, ଏ ଠାରେ ବିଦେଶୀ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Brahma bhakti beda bāraku thoi, e ṭhāre bideshī jibe paḷāi.

Kududzira Nhamba:
Mahapurush akashandisa mazwi ekufananidzira enhamba:
  • Brahma: Inomiririrwa ne 1 (Sezviri mu "Ekam Brahma").
  • Bhakti: Inomiririrwa ne 9 (Mhando pfumbamwe dzeNavadha Bhakti).
  • Veda: Inomiririrwa ne 4 (MaVeda mana).
  • Baar: Inomiririrwa ne 7 (Mazuva manomwe evhiki).

Kana zvaiswa pamwe chete (1-9-4-7), zvinoumba 1947. Chiporofita chinotaura kuti mugore rino, vatorwa (British) vaizotiza kubva munyika ino.

3.5 Chiporofita Nezve Kuuraya Vana Vasikana Vari Mudumbu

Mu 'Jaiphula Malika', Mahapurush akafanotaura zvakaipa zvemagariro zvekupera kweKaliyuga:

Odia Sloka:

କନ୍ୟା ରତ୍ନକୁ ଗର୍ଭର, ନଷ୍ଟ କରିବ ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ହୃଦୟେ ନ କରି ପାପ ବିଚାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kanyā ratnaku garbhara, naṣṭa kariba tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
hṛudaye na kari pāpa bichāra.

Zvinoreva:
"Vanhu vachaparadza mwana musikana (Kanya Ratna) mukati medumbu (Garbha) kakawanda vasina kana pfungwa yechivi mumoyo yavo." Izvi zvinonyatso taridza nguva yerima yekuuraya vana vasikana vari mudumbu yakatambudza nzanga mumakumi emakore achangopfuura.

3.6 Chiporofita cheDenda ra2020 (COVID-19)

Mahapurush Hadidas, kuzvarwa patsva kwePanchasakhas akazvarwa muzana ramakore rechi 18, akanyora mu 'Agata Bhavishyat Chautisha':

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ବିଂଶ ଯେବେ ବର୍ଷ ବୋଲାଇବ, ବହୁ ବିତିପାତ ହେବ।
ଅଜଣା ବ୍ୟାଧିରେ ବହୁ ନାଶ ଯିବେ, ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dui biṃsha jebe barsha bolāiba, bahu bitipāta heba.
Ajaṇā byādhire bahu nāsha jibe, hāhākāra paḍijiba.

Zvinoreva:
"Kana gore 'Dui-Binsha' (Makumi maviri-Maviri / 2020) rasvika, pachava nenyonganyonga huru. Vanhu vachafa nechirwere chisingazivikanwe (Ajana Vyadhi) uye pachava nekuchema kwekuora mwoyo (Haahakaar) kwese kwese." Iyi itsanangudzo inoshamisa uye yakarurama yedenda reCOVID-19 rakapararira pasi rose muna 2020.

3.7 Chiporofita cheDhamu reHirakud

Murugwaro 'Chakada Madana' uye 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa', Vatsvene Achyutananda naArkhit Das vakafanotaura kuvakwa kwedhamu guru muOdisha.

Odia Sloka:

ଛବିଶ ଠାବରେ ହୀରାକୁଦ ଉଛୁଳିବ, ଏ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଅର୍ଦ୍ଧଭାଗ ଜଳମଗ୍ନ ହେବ।
ଧନଜନ ନଷ୍ଟ ହେବ କହିଲି ବୁଝାଇ, ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ବେଶୀ ବେଳ ନାହିଁ।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲିଣଟି ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକାକାର ହେବ।
ସେହି ବେଳେ ଗଙ୍ଗା ପୁଣି ଆସିବେଟି ମାଡ଼ି, ବୈତରଣୀ, ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣୀ ଯେ ଦେବେ ଘୋର ରଡ଼ି।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ବଡ଼ ବନ୍ଧ ତିଆରି ଯେ ଥିବ, ସେଇବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାଙ୍ଗିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ।
ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ଛଅ ଜିଲ୍ଲା ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇ, ଯିବାପାଇଁ ବାଟ ନ ପାଇବେ ପୁଣି କେହି।
ହୀରାକୁଦ ବୋଲି ଗ୍ରାମର ଯେ ନାମ ଥିବ, ବନ୍ଧବାଡ଼ ଭାଙ୍ଗି ସବୁ ଏକ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chabiṣha ṭhābare Hīrākuda uchuḷiba, e Oḍiśhā arddhabhāga jaḷamagna heba.
Dhanajana naṣhṭa heba kahili bujhāi, dīna Achyuta kahai beśhī beḷa nāhiñ.
Hīrākuda boliṇaṭi grāma nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu ekākāra heba.
Sehi beḷe Gaṅgā puṇi āsibeṭi māḍi, Baitaraṇī, Brāhmaṇī je debe ghora raḍi.
Oḍiśhāre baḍa bandha tiāri je thiba, seibeḷe śhatrudwārā bhāṅgiṇa je jiba.
Oḍiśhāre chha-a jillā jaḷārṇnaba hoi, jibāpāiñ bāṭa na pāibe puṇi kehi.
Hīrākuda boli grāmara je nāma thiba, bandhabāḍa bhāṅgi sabu eka je hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Achyutananda Das ji murugwaro rwake rweMalika 'Chakada Madana' uye mutsvene Brahma Avadhut Arakhita Das, akazvarwa muchikamu chechipiri chezana ramakore rechi 18 uye akaita sadhana yakakwana mubako reOlashuni, murugwaro rwake rweMalika 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa' akanyora kuti dhamu guru richavakwa munyika yeOdisha rine zita richanzi "Hirakud". Akawedzera kutaura kuti munguva yekupedzisira yeKaliyuga, nekurwiswa kwevavengi, dhamu iroro richatyoka, izvo zvichaita kuti matunhu matanhatu enyika yeOdisha anyure, zvifambiso zvichamira, uye pachava nekurashikirwa kukuru kweupfumi nevanhu. MaMahapurusha akati panguva imwe cheteyo Ganga icharatidza chimiro chayo chine hasha uye nzizi dzeBaitarani, Brahmani, nezvimwe zvichaitawo kuparadzwa kukuru.

Sezvinonyatsozivikanwa, mugore ra 1948 munyika yeOdisha yeIndia, basa rekuvaka dhamu rainzi Hirakud rakatanga, rakapera mugore ra 1953. Mumwedzi waNdira wegore ra 1957, neMutungamiriri weNyika panguva iyoyo Pandit Jawaharlal Nehru, kuvhurwa kwepamutemo kwedhamu iri kwakaitwa. Nehurefu hwese hwe 26 km, rakavakwa pamusoro peRwizi Mahanadi mudunhu reSambalpur reOdisha, dhamu iri nhasi ndiro dhamu refu kwazvo rakavakwa nevanhu pasi rose. Nenzira iyi, kururama kwechikamu chekutanga chechiporofita ichi kwakaratidzwa.

Pasina nguva nyika ichaona chikamu chechipiri chechiporofita chataurwa chichizadzikawo kana, panguva yehondo yeIndia neChina, nekurwiswa kwemissile kweChina, dhamu iri richatyoka uye nyika yeOdisha ichatambura nekukukura kwemvura kunotyisa.

3.8 Chiporofita cheKuzvarwa kwePakistan Mazana Emakore Pamberi

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das nevamwe vakataura nezvekusikwa nezita rePakistan makore 600 apfuura murugwaro rwakadai se 'Gupta Kheda Malika' uye 'Bhavishyat Malika'. Saizvozvowo, mutsvene wezana ramakore rechi 18 Arkhit Das akazvitaura mu 'Mahagupta Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ପାକିସ୍ତାନର ଦଶା ଯାହା ହେବ ସହସା, ମନ କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦଇ ଥରେ ଶୁଣ ସେ ଭାଷା।
ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ତାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ, ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପ୍ରାଚ୍ୟ ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ଗଣ ଏହିପରି ମିଳିଣ, ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ଲାଗି ସର୍ବେ କରିବେ ରଣ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟଗୋଟି ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ହାହାକାର ଶୁଭୁଣ ଯେ ଥିବ।
ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ, ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ପାକିସ୍ତାନ ରାଜ୍ୟ ଗୋଟା ଛାରଖାର ହେବ, ପଛ ବେଳକୁ ତ ପୁଣି ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଯେ ସ୍ଫୁରିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pākistānara dashā jāhā heba sahasā, mana karṇṇa dei thare shuṇa se bhāṣā.
Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākistānaṭi raṅge, mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Prāchya pāshchātya gaṇa ehipari miḷiṇa, Pākistāna lāgi sarbe karibe raṇa.
Pākistāna rājyagoṭi chhārakhāra heba, chāriāḍe hāhākāra shubhuṇa je thiba.
Chīna Pākistāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa, Ruṣiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmana Jāpāna.
Pākistāna rājya goṭā chhārakhāra heba, pachha beḷaku ta puṇi buddhi je sphuriba.

Zvinoreva:
"Nyika inonzi Pakistan ichangoerekana yavapo. Munguva dzekupedzisira, India ichatarisana nehondo ine utsinye nePakistan. Masimba eMiddle East neWestern achaungana; Nyika dzechiMuslim, nyika dzeWestern, China, neAmerica zvichatsigira Pakistan, ukuwo Russia, Germany, neJapan zvichabatsira India. Pakistan ichaparadzwa zvachose (Chara-khara) muhondo iyi, uye pakati pekuora mwoyo kwepasi rose, ichazoziva zvikanganiso zvayo."

Zvakakosha kucherechedza kuti izwi rekuti 'Pakistan' rakangogadzirwa muna 1933, asi vatsvene ava vakaripa zita mazana emakore apfuura.

3.9 Zviporofita Nezve Guta reBhubaneswar

Hwaro hweBhubaneswar yemazuva ano hwakaiswa muna 1948 naPM Nehru. Zvisinei, kutaura kwayo seguta guru kunoonekwa murugwaro rweMalika rwakadai se 'Chakada Madana' uye 'Kali Agata Bhabishyant'.

Odia Sloka:

ଭୈରବୀ ଡାକିବ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର, ଚୈତ୍ର ମାସ ଯେ ମଙ୍ଗଳବାର।
ପୃଥିବୀରେ ଗୋଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଆରମ୍ଭିବ ଯେବେ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସୈନ୍ୟ ମାଡ଼ି ଯେ ଆସିବେ।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ, ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ତ ପୁଣି ରାଜଧାନୀ ଜାଣ, ବିଦେଶୀ ଶତ୍ରୁଙ୍କ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି ପଡ଼ିବଟି ପୁଣ।
ଭୈରବ ଡାକ ହେବ ଜାଣ, ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରଟି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhairabī ḍākiba Bhubaneshwar, Chaitra māsa je Maṅgaḷabāra.
Pṛuthibīre goḷa yuddha ārambhiba jebe, Bhubaneshwaraku sainya māḍi je āsibe.
Bhubaneshwarare lāgiba goḷa, Ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Bhubaneshwar ta puṇi rājadhānī jāṇa, bideshī shatruṅka dṛuṣṭi paḍibaṭi puṇa.
Bhairaba ḍāka heba jāṇa, Bhubaneshwarati pramāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Vatsvene vakafanotaura kuti neChipiri mumwedzi waChaitra, ruzha rune simba (kuomba kwaBhairavi) hwaizonzwika muBhubaneswar. Panguva yeHondo Yenyika Yetatu, mauto evavengi achasvika kuBhubaneswar (Ekamra Bana). Zvinyorwa zvakanyatso taura kuti Bhubaneswar yaizova guta guru uye chinangwa chevavengi vekunze.

3.10 Chiporofita Nezve Guru reMweya Rine Mukurumbira Shri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, muzvinyorwa zvake zveMalika zvakanyorwa makore anoda kusvika 600 apfuura, akafanotaura kuzvarwa kwe "Yugapurusha" (Murume weNguva) aizoita mitambo mizhinji yeumwari uye ane mukurumbira waizopararira pasi rose.

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳାମାନ ହେବ କୀର୍ତ୍ତି ରହି ଯିବ, ଅନୁକୂଳ ନାମ ଧରି।
ଦେଶ ବିଦେଶରୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିବେ, ସତସଙ୍ଗ ଆଶ୍ରା କରି।
ସତନାମ ଗୋଟି ଉଚ୍ଚାରି ବୋଲିବେ, ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ ହେବ ମେଳ।
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ମାର୍ଗେ ରହି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦେଖିବ, ଯୁଗପୁରୁଷଙ୍କ ଖେଳ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Līḷāmāna heba kīrtti rahi jiba, Anukūḷa nāma dhari.
Desha bidesharu bhakata āsibe, sat-saṅga āshrā kari.
Satanāma goṭi uchchāri bolibe, bhaktaṅka heba meḷa.
Shūnya mārge rahi Achyuta dekhiba, Yugapuruṣaṅka kheḷa.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush akanyora kuti mutsvene ainzi "Anukul" aizoonekwa, uye vatsvene vanobva kuIndia nekune dzimwe nyika vaizouya kuzotsvaga pokugara mu "Satsanga" yake. Zita dzvene (Satnaam) raizoimbwa, uye pachava nekuungana kukuru kwevatsvene. Achyutananda Das akawedzera kutaura kuti iye pachake, achigara munzvimbo isingaonekwi/isina chinhu (Shunya Maarga), aizoona mutambo weumwari weYugapurusha uyu.

Kuzadzisa chiporofita ichi, Anukulchandra Chakravarty akaberekwa musi waGunyana 14, 1888, mudunhu rePabna reBengal isina kupatsanurwa (yava Bangladesh). Achizivikanwa pasi rose saShri Shri Thakur Anukulchandra, akavamba 'Satsang Ashram' muDeoghar, Jharkhand, muna 1946. Mamiriyoni evateveri pasi rose akabatana nebasa rake, vachimuti 'Yuga Purushottam.'

3.11 Kutaurwa kweBhomba reAtomu muMalika Texts

Kunyange bhomba reatomu rakagadzirwa pakati pezana ramakore rechi20, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das (mu'Gupta Kheda Malika' ne'Bhavishyat Malika') naBalarama Das (mu'Kali Tandaba Gita') vakaritaura mazana emakore apfuura.

Odia Sloka:

ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା କରିଦେବେଟି ଜମା, ପୋଡ଼ିଜାଳି ଦେବା ପାଇଁ ଭାରତ ସୀମା।
ପରମାଣୁ ଯେ ବୋମା ଯାହା ଲାଗି ଗାରିମା, ଦେଖାଇ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ଆଜି ପାଶ୍ଚାତ୍ୟ ସେନା।
ତାହା ଫୁଟିବ ନାହିଁ କେଣେ ଯିବ ମିଳାଇ, ଏହା ଦେଖି ବିଦେଶୀଏ ଯିବେ ପଳାଇ।
ଅଗ୍ନିର ଦାହିକା ଶକ୍ତି ଲୋ ବଉଳ ପ୍ରଭୁ କରିବେ ହରଣ, ପରମାଣୁ ବୋମା ଯାହାଲାଗି ଗାରିମା ଶୀତଳ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Paramāṇu je bomā karidebeṭi jamā, poḍijāḷi debā pāiñ Bhārata sīmā.
Paramāṇu je bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā, dekhāi huanti āji pāshchātya senā.
Tāhā phuṭiba nāhiñ keṇe jiba miḷāi, ehā dekhi bideshīe jibe paḷāi.
Agnira dāhikā shakti lo bauḷa Prabhu karibe haraṇa, paramāṇu bomā jāhā lāgi gārimā shītaḷa hoiba jāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Vatsvene vakanyora kuti panguva yeHondo Yenyika Yetatu, vavengi veIndia vaizoronga kuparadza miganhu yeIndia vachishandisa Mabhomba eAtomu (Paramanu Bomba). Zvisinei, hurongwa hwavo huchakundikana nekuti Ishe Kalki vachatora simba rinopisa/rinoparadza (Dahika Shakti) remabhomba aya. Zvombo zvacho zvinozvirumbidza mauto ekuMadokero neevavengi zvichava "zvinotonhora" (zvisina simba) uye hazvizove nemhedzisiro panyika yeIndia. Vachiona chiitiko ichi chisingagoneki, vavengi vekunze vachatiza vachitya. Magwaro anoyambira kuti hondo yenyukireya inotyisa (Anu-Paramanu Yuddha) ichazoitika, ichiita kuti Pasi rive risina kugara, izvo vateveri vachapupura.

3.12 Kutaurwa kwaMadan Mohan Malaviya, Mahatma Gandhi, naMotilal Nehru

Akaberekwa muna 1850, mutsvene mukuru Bhima Bhoi (muteveri wetsika yePanchasakhas) akapa ruzivo nezvevatungamiri vebato rekusunungura India mugwaro rake 'Padmakalpa'.

Odia Sloka:

ମଦନମାଲବ୍ୟ ନାମ ବୋଲିକରି ଏକ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବ ଜନ୍ମି,
ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ସହିତରେ ଅହିଂସା ଧର୍ମରେ ନିରନ୍ତରେ ଥିବେ ପୁଣି।
ଚୁଡ଼ଙ୍ଗ ବଂଶରେ ଉତପତ୍ତି ହୋଇ ମୋତିଲାଲ ଯେ ନେହେରୁ,
ମହାପ୍ରତାପୀ ଅଟଇ ସେହୁ ଭକ୍ତ ଆସିଛନ୍ତି ଶୂନ୍ୟପୁରୁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Madanamālabya nāma bolikari eka bhakta thiba janmi,
Gāndhī sahitare ahinsā dharmare nirantare thibe puṇi.
Chuḍaṅga baṃshare utpatti hoi Motilāl je Neheru,
mahāpratāpī aṭai sehu bhakta āsichanti Shūnyapuru.

Zvinoreva:
Gwaro rakaprofita kuberekwa kwemuteveri ainzi "Madan Mohan Malaviya" uyo, pamwe na"Mahatma Gandhi," aizotungamira bato rakavakirwa pachitendero chekusashandisa chisimba (Ahimsa). Uyezve, raitaura nezvekuberekwa kwemuteveri ane simba guru uye ane pesvedzero "Motilal Nehru." Vose vatatu vakava vatungamiri vakakosha veIndian National Congress uye kurwira rusununguko.

3.13 Chiporofita chekuurayiwa kwaMahatma Gandhi naNathuram Godse

Mu'Jaiphula Malika', Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akafanotaura rufu rweMahatma.

Odia Sloka:

ମୋହନ ଗାନ୍ଧୀ ମରଣ, ନାଥୁରାମ ହସ୍ତେ ହୋଇବ ଜାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏ କଥା ସିଦ୍ଧ ଫଳ ରେ ନିପୁଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mohana Gāndhī maraṇa, Nāthurāma haste hoiba jāṇa lo jāiphula,
e kathā siddha phaḷa re nipuṇa.

Zvinoreva:
"Rufu rwaMohan Gandhi ruchaitika mumaoko aNathuram." Sezvakanyorwa nhoroondo, musi waNdira 30, 1948, Nathuram Vinayak Godse akauraya Mahatma Gandhi.

3.14 Kuzvitonga kweIndia pasi pehutungamiri hwaMahatma Gandhi

Mu'Kali Bhagabata', Mahapurush Abhiram Paramahansa (mutsvene wezana ramakore rechi20 mutsika yeMalika) akatsanangura shanduko yezvematongerwo enyika.

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟାଗ୍ରହୀ ହୋଇଲେ ମେଳି ।
ଅନ୍ତର ହୋଇଯିବ କଳି ।।
ଇଉରୋପିଆନ ଯିବେ ଛାଡ଼ି ।
ମୋହନ ସେନା ଦେବେ କାଢ଼ି ।।
ପ୍ରଥମ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ସେନାପତି ।
ହେବେ ମୋହନ ସୈନ୍ୟ ଘେତି ।।
ମୋହନ ବୋଲି ଯାଙ୍କୁ କହି ।
ମହତ ଆତ୍ମା ଅଟେ ସେହି ।।
ସେହି ନାରଦ ମହାମୁନ୍ୟ ।
ତାଙ୍କୁ କରନ୍ତି ସର୍ବେ ମାନ୍ୟ ।।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Satyāgrahī hoile meḷi,
antara hoijiba kaḷi.
Iuropiāna jibe chhāḍi,
Mohana senā debe kāḍhi.
Prathama yuddhe senāpati,
hebe Mohana sainya gheti.
Mohana boli jāṅku kahi,
mahata ātmā aṭe sehi.
Sehi Nārada mahāmunya,
tāṅku karanti sarbe mānya.

Zvinoreva:
Mutsvene akafanotaura kuti kuburikidza nekuungana kweSatyagrahis, maEurope aizoomanikidzwa kuenda. Akaziva "Mohan" (Gandhi) semukuru/mutungamiri wekurwira rusununguko kukuru kwekutanga uku. Akatsanangurawo Mahatma Gandhi semweya mukuru (Mahatma) uye kuzvarwa patsva kwaNarada, ane mazwi aizoitwa rukudzo nevose. Izvi zvakazadziswa sezvo hutungamiri hwaGandhi hwakava simba guru rakaita kuti maBritish abve panyika yeIndia.

4 Zera reKaliyuga

Zvinoenderana nemagwaro eSanatan, zera rose reKaliyuga makore 432,000. Bhavishya Malika inosimbisawo nguva iyi yose, asi inowedzera chiporofita chakakosha: nekuda kwezvivi zvakanyanya zvevanhu, Kaliyuga haizopedzi nguva yayo yose.

4.1 Murairo weMagwaro wemakore 432,000

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mu'Adi Samhita':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିରେ ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯାଏ, ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନିର୍ଣ୍ଣୟ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷire chāri lakṣa jāe, batiśa sahasra nirṇaya.

Zvinoreva:
"MuKaliyuga, zviuru zvina zvemakumi ezviuru nemakumi matatu nezviviri (432,000) zvemakore zvakatarwa seupenyu hwayo."

Zvadaro Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mugwaro rake, 'Dashapatala Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଭୋଗ ପ୍ରମାଣ ସାର,
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ହଜାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaliyuga bhoga pramāṇa sāra,
chārilakṣha je batisha hajāra.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, upenyu hwakatarwa hweKaliyuga makore zviuru zvina zvemakumi ezviuru nemakumi matatu nezviviri.

Anonyora mugwaro rake reMalika, 'Parardha Jataka':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶି ସହସ୍ର କଳି,
କରିଲି ତିନି ଯୁଗଠାରୁ ବଳି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chārilakṣha batishi sahasra kali,
karili tini yugaṭhāru bali.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, upenyu hweKaliyuga makore zviuru zvina zvemakumi ezviuru nemakumi matatu nezviviri.

Zvisinei, maMahapurusha haana kungomira pakutaura izvi chete; vakataurawo mumagwaro avo eMalika kuti nekuda kwezvivi zvakasiyana-siyana zvakaitwa nevanhu, Kaliyuga haizokwanisi kupedza nguva yayo yose.

4.2 Nei Kaliyuga ichapera kare

MaPanchasakhas anojekesa kuti kunyange zera rethiyori riri makore 432,000, "Bhoga" (nguva chaiyo inonzwiwa) ichava pfupi zvikuru nekuda kwehuremu hwezvivi. Mu'Dashapatala Malika', zvakanyorwa kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର, ଭୋଗ ନୋହିବ କହ ପୀତବାସ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra, bhoga nohiba kaha Pītabāsa.

Zvinoreva:
"Haiwa Ishe Pitabasa (Jagannath)! Tiudzei kuti Kaliyuga haizokwanisi sei kunzwa nguva yayo yose yemakore 432,000."

Mahapurush anonyora mugwaro rake reMalika, 'Kalikalpa Gita':

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ବର୍ଷ ଆଉ,
ଭୋଗ ନହେବ ପାପଭାରା ଯହୁଁ।
ଆପଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଉଦିତ,
ପାପୀମାନଙ୍କୁ କରିବେ ମୁକତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chāri lakṣha batisha sasra barṣha āu,
bhoga naheba pāpabhārā jahuṃ.
Āpaṇe prabhu hoibe udita,
pāpīmānaṃku karibe mukata.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nekuda kwehuremu hwakanyanya hwezvivi muKaliyuga, Kaliyuga haizokwanisi kunzwa upenyu hwayo hwemakore 4,32,000. Kunyange nguva iyi isati yasvika, Ishe vachazvarwa patsva, vatadzi vachaparadzwa, uye Dharma ichasimbiswa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyorazve mune rimwe remagwaro ake eMalika:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ,
ପାପ ଯୋଗୁଁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kali chāri lakṣha batisha sasra nohiba bhoga,
pāpa joguṃ kṣhaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Kaliyuga haizokwanisi kunzwa nguva yayo yakatarwa yemakore 4,32,000. Nekuda kwezvivi, upenyu hwayo huchaderera, kureva kuti, haizokwanisi kunzwiwa zvachose.

4.3 Kana zvisiri makore 4,32,000, ipapo upenyu hunonzwiwa hweKaliyuga huri papi?

Mazuva ano, vamwe vanhu vari kuita basa rekuparadzira, vachitaura Malika, kuti upenyu hunonzwiwa hweKaliyuga makore 10,000. Ngazvizivikanwe kuti izvi zvakakanganisa zvachose.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mugwaro rake reMalika, 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta':

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶ ସହସ୍ରରେ କଳିନାଶ ହେବ ଏ ଅଟେ ବିଷମ ବାଣୀ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dasha sahasrare kalināsha heba e aṭe biṣhama bāṇī.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Kaliyuga ichapera mumakore 10,000 – ichi chirevo chisingawirirani kana chenhema.

MaPanchasakha Mahapurushas akanyora munzvimbo dzakawanda mumagwaro avo eMalika kuti upenyu hunonzwiwa hweKaliyuga huchava makore 5,000, mushure mezvo Ishe Kalki vachazvarwa patsva uye Satya-Kali kana Ananta Yuga kana Adya Satya Yuga ichasvika.

Huyai, ngatiwane ruzivo rwakawanda pamusoro penyaya iyi imwe neimwe kuburikidza neslokas dzeMalika.

Chekutanga pane zvose, ngatizivei zvakataurwa naIshe Shri Krishna pachavo nezveupenyu hweKaliyuga.

4.4 Nguva Chaiyo: Makore 5,000

Kusiyana neruzivo rusiri rwechokwadi runoparadzirwa nevamwe kuti Kaliyuga inogara makore 10,000, zvinyorwa zveMalika zvinodzokorora kutaura kuti kutonga chaiko kwaKali kunopera mushure memakore 5,000, kuchiteverwa nenguva yekuchinja.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anotaura mashoko aIshe Krishna kuna Garuda mubhuku rake 'Agata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭୋଗ, ପାପ ଯୋଗୁ କ୍ଷୟ ହୋଇବ ଅଳ୍ପ ପଡ଼ିବ ଭାଗ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେ ପଚାଶ ଭୋଗ ହେବ ନିକର, ଏଥୁ କିଛି ଉଣା ହୋଇବ ଶୁଣ ଗରୁଡ଼ ବୀର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷi chāri lakṣa batiśa sahasra nohiba bhoga, pāpa jogu kṣaya hoiba alpa paḍiba bhāga.
Pāñcha sahasra je pachāsha bhoga heba nikara, ethu kichi uṇā hoiba shuṇa Garuḍa bīra.

Zvinoreva:
"Iwe Garuda ane ushingi! Teerera, Kaliyuga haizooni makore 432,000. Nekuda kwechivi, nguva yayo ichaderera. Ichapera pedyo nemakore 5,050.

4.5 Mufananidzo waRavana

Odia Sloka:

ରାମଦାସ ବୋଲଇ ହୋ ଶୁଣ ଗୁରୁଦେବ, କଳିଯୁଗ କେତେ ପୁଣ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।
ଚାରିଲକ୍ଷ ବତିଶ ଯେ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ, କେମନ୍ତେ ଯେ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ହୋଇବଟି ଶେଷ।
ଏଡ଼େ ବଡ଼ ଯୁଗ ପୁଣ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ସଂଶୟ ଫେଡ଼ିଣ ମୋତେ କହ ଗୁରୁଦେବ।
ରାମଦାସ ବାଣୀ ଶୁଣି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହନ୍ତି, ତୋତେ କହୁଅଛି ରାମ ଶୁଣ ଦେଇ ମତି।
ରାବଣ ଯେ ତ୍ରେତୟା ଯୁଗରେ ତପ କଲା, ଛପନ ଗଣ୍ଡା ଯୁଗକୁ ବର ସେ ପାଇଲା।
ପାପେ ଗଲା ତାର ତିନି ଭାଗ ଯେ ବିନାଶ, ଏକଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସ୍ୱର୍ଗେ ପଶି ଅପସରୀ ମାନଙ୍କୁ ହରିଲା, ପର ଦ୍ରବ୍ୟ ହରି ଯେବେ ପାଦେ ଖଟାଇଲା।
ଆପେ ଜଗତମାତା ଯେ ଜନକ ନନ୍ଦିନୀ, ତାଙ୍କୁ ହରି ନେଲା ଗର୍ବେ ମୂଢ଼ ବିଂଶପାଣି।
ସୁରାପାନ କଲା ସେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିଙ୍କୁ ବଧିଲା, ବ୍ରହ୍ମହତ୍ୟା ଆଦିକରି କେତେ ପାପ କଲା।
ପାପରୁ ଯେ ତିନି ଭାଗ ହୋଇଲା ବିନାଶ, ଏକ ଭାଗ ଭୋଗ କଲା ବିଶ୍ରବାର ଶିଷ୍ୟ।
ସେହିପରି କଳିଯୁଗ ବିନାଶ ହୋଇବ, ପାଞ୍ଚସସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅନ୍ୟ ପାପେ ଯିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Rāmadāsa bolai ho shuṇa gurudeba, kaliyuga kete puṇa bhoga je hoiba.
Chārilakṣha batisha je sahasra baraṣha, kemaṃte je yuga puṇa hoibaṭi sheṣha.
Eḍe baḍa yuga puṇa bināsha hoiba, saṃshaya pheriṇa mote kaha gurudeba.
Rāmadāsa bāṇī shuṇi Achyuta kahaṃti, tote kahuachi Rāma shuṇa dei mati.
Rābaṇa je tretayā yugare tapa kalā, chapana gaṇḍā yugaku bara se pāilā.
Pāpe galā tāra tini bhāga je vināsha, ekabhāga bhoga kalā Bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Swarge pashi apasarī mānaṃku harilā, para drabya hari jebe pāde khaṭāilā.
Āpe jagatamātā je Janaka naṃdinī, tāṃku hari nelā garve mūḍha biṃshapāṇi.
Surāpāna kalā se kṣhatriṃṃku badhilā, brahmahatyā ādi kari kete pāpa kalā.
Pāparu je tini bhāga hoilā bināsha, eka bhāga bhoga kalā bishrabāra shiṣhya.
Sehipari kaliyuga bināsha hoiba, pāṃchasasra bhoga heba anya pāpe jiba.

Mubhuku rake 'Kalpatika', Achyutananda Das anotsanangura chiitiko ichi achishandisa muenzaniso waRavana. Ravana akanga aita kutendeuka kukuru uye akawana hupenyu hwe "56 Ganda" (nguva huru yekudenga). Zvisinei, nekuti akapamba Amai Sita, akatuka vanasikana vekudenga, uye akaita zvivi zvinotyisa zvakadai seBrahman-hatya, akafara chete chikamu chimwe chechipo chake, apo zvimwe zvikamu zvitatu zvakaparadzwa nezvivi zvake.

Saizvozvowo, nguva yeKaliyuga yemakore 432,000 iri "kupedzwa" nekuwedzera nekukurumidza kwechivi chepasi rose, zvichikonzera kupera kwayo nekukurumidza mushure memakore 5,000 enguva yakararama, ichigadzira nzira yeAnanta Yuga kana Satya Yuga.

4.6 Zera reKaliyuga: Ongororo Yakadzama

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake zveMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ଯେ ବତିଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗର ଅଟଇ ଆୟୁଷ।
ପାପଭାରରେ ଆୟୁ କଟିଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chāri lakṣa je batiśa sahasra,
Kaḷiyugara aṭai āyuṣa.
Pāpabhārare āyu kaṭijiba,
Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi bhoga hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, zera reKaliyuga makore mazana mana nemakumi matatu nemaviri ezviuru (432,000), asi nekuda kwesimba rezvivi, zera iri richapfupiswa uye richapera mumakore zviuru zvishanu (5,000) chete.

Kuzvarwa patsva kwaMahapurush Achyutananda Das, akazvarwa muzana ramakore rechi18 mudunhu reJajpur muOdisha, Mutsvene Mahapurush Hadi Das akakurukura nezvezera reKaliyuga mubhuku rake reMalika rainzi ‘Kali Chautisha’. Akanyora kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ସଂବତ୍ସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଯୁଗ ଆଦ୍ୟ ହୋଇବ ଶୁଭଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରବେଶ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Saṃbatsara pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hoiba sheṣa,
Satya yuga ādya hoiba shubhajoge prabesha.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, mu "Sambatsara Paancha Sahasra" (makore 5,000), Kaliyuga ichapera, uye mushure mezvo, Adya Satya Yuga ichatanga nenguva yakanaka.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mune rimwe bhuku reMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ଯେ ହଜାର ଥାରୁ ମହାଭୟ ହେବ,
ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସମ୍ରାଟକୁ ଆସି ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଗାନ୍ଧାର ସେନାଟି ଗୋଳ କରିବ ଅବଶ୍ୟ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର ପଚାଶ କଳିର ଆୟୁଷ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pāñcha je hajāra thāru mahābhaya heba,
Dillī samrāṭaku āsi bipada paḍiba.
Gāndhāra senāṭi goḷa kariba abashya,
Pāñcha hajāra pachāsha Kaḷira āyuṣa.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush anonyorawo pano kuti mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga, pachava nemamiriro ezvinhu ekutya kukuru; njodzi dzichawira Mambo weDelhi kana Mutungamiriri weNyika. Hondo yeGandhara (Taliban) ichanyatsoita hondo neIndia. Mahapurush anonyorazve kuti izvi zvose zvichaitika mushure mekupera kweKaliyuga, kureva, mushure memakore 5,050 eKaliyuga apfuura.

Achyutananda Das ji akanyorawo ndima mubhuku rake reMalika kwaasina kutaura zera reKaliyuga zvakananga semakore 5,000 kana 5,050, asi akazvitaura zvisina kunanga kuburikidza nezviratidzo:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶେ ମସିହା ହୋଇବ ଶେଷ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କରିବେ କଳି ବିନାଶ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Biṃshe masiha hoiba sheṣa,
Ananta karibe Kaḷi bināsha.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, mumakore '20s'—kubva mugore zviuru zviviri (2000) kusvika zviuru zviviri nemakumi mapfumbamwe nemapfumbamwe (2099)—pane imwe nguva panguva iyi, Kaliyuga ichapera. Ishe Ananta vachaparadza Kaliyuga Pasi.

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, akatsanangura zera reKaliyuga zvakadzama mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Aagata Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବୋଲଇ ଶୁଣିମା ଗୋସାଇଁ କୁହ ଭବିଷ୍ୟ ବିଚାର,
କେତେବେଳେ କଳ୍କୀ ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ଶୁଣଇଁ ମୁଖୁ ତୁମ୍ଭର।
ଶିଶୁ ବୋଲନ୍ତି ହେ ଶୁଣିମା ବାରଙ୍ଗ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ତୁହି,
ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଯେବେ ଯିବ ଭୋଗ ହୋଇ।
ଜେସେନେକେ ନିଶି ପାହିଲେ ପ୍ରଭାତ ଯୁଗଶନ୍ଧି ଏହା ଜାଣ,
ଯେମନ୍ତ ସମୟେ କଳଙ୍କି ସ୍ୱରୂପ ହୋଇବେ ସେ ନାରାୟଣ।
ସମକ୍ଷର ବତା ଶୁଣି ଆଣିକରି ପ୍ରମାଣ ଏହାକୁ କର,
ସବୁ ଏକଠାବେ ମିଶାଇ କରିଣ କରିବୁ ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାର।
ଏହି ସମୟକୁ ତଳେ କରିଥିବୁ କହଇ ହୋ ବାବୁ ତୋତେ,
ଠିକ ରେ ଏ କଥା ଦେଖାଇ କହିଲୁ ରଖିଥିବୁ ହୃଦଗତେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bāraṅga bolai shuṇimā gosāiṃ kuha bhabiṣya bichāra,
ketebeḷe Kalkī swarūpa hoibe shuṇaiṃ mukhu tumbhara.
Shishu bolanti he shuṇimā Bāraṅga Kaḷaṅki swarūpa tuhi,
yugashandhi pāñcha sahasra baraṣa jebe jiba bhoga hoi.
Jeseneke nishi pāhile prabhāta yugashandhi ehā jāṇa,
jemanta samaye Kaḷaṅki swarūpa hoibe se Nārāyaṇa.
Samakṣara batā shuṇi āṇikari pramāṇa ehāku kara,
sabu ekaṭhābe mishāi kariṇa karibu pāñcha hajāra.
Ehi samayaku taḷe karithibu kahai ho bābu tote,
Ṭhika re e kathā dekhāi kahilu rakhithibu hṛudagate.

Zvinoreva:
Mudzidzi Baranga Das anoti kuna Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das—"Iwe Guru Dev, ndiudze nezveramangwana. Ndinoda kunzwa kubva pamuromo wenyu kuti Ishe vachazviratidza riini muchimiro chavo cheKalki."

Pakunzwa izvi, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das anoudza mudzidzi wake mukuru Baranga kuti kana 'Yuga-Sandhi' (kuchinja pakati pemazera) ikaitika, ipapo chete ndipo Ishe vachatora kuzvarwa kwavo kweKalki. Achitsanangura zvakare, anoti Yuga-Sandhi iyi ichaitika kana Kaliyuga yave nemakore 5,000. Sezvo mangwanani achiuya mushure meusiku, rimwe zera rinouya mushure mezera rimwe. Chikamu chepakati pakati pemazera maviri chinonzi Yuga-Sandhi. Uye muYuga-Sandhi iyi, Narayana vachatora chimiro chavo cheKalki. Yuga-Sandhi iyi ichauya mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura.

Achyutananda ji, achitaura zera reKaliyuga mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kaliyuga Malika’, anoti:

Odia Sloka:

ଚହଟିବ ଲୀଳା ତୁ ଚାରିରେ ମିଶା ଏକ,
ଚଢ଼ା ତିନି ଶୂନ ତହିଁ ଯେତେ ହେଲା ଠିକ।
ଚଳିଯିବ ଘୋର କଳି ଦଳି ଦେବେ ମିଳି,
ଚେତାଇ ଗୀତ ଛନ୍ଦଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ସେ ଭାଳି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

chahaṭiba līḷā tu chārire mishā eka,
chaḍhā tini shūna tahiñ jete helā ṭhika.
Chalijiba ghora Kaḷi daḷi debe miḷi,
chetāi gīta chhandai Achyuta se bhāḷi.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, wedzera chimwe kunhamba ina (4+1=5) uye isa mazero matatu padivi pehuwandu; nhamba inowanikwa inomiririra nguva iyo nguva yeKaliyuga inotyisa ichapera. Mundima iri pamusoro, Mahapurush ari kutaura zera reKaliyuga nenzira yekufananidzira. Anoti 4+1=5, uye kuisa mazero matatu padivi pe5 zvinoreva kuti mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura, zera iroro richapera.

Mune rimwe bhuku reMalika ‘Bhavishya Chautisha’, anoti kumudzidzi wake Ramachandra:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅମରପୁର,
ଠାକୁର ତହିଁରୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ, ଠାରି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଧର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ṭhikaṇā Amarapura,
Ṭhākura tahiñru hebe bāhāra, Rāmachandra he,
ṭhāri pāñcha sahasra dhara.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, Iwe mudzidzi Ramachandra, kuzvarwa patsva kwaIshe Kalki kuchaitika muAmarapur (dunhu risingafi—riripo kunyange Satya Yuga isati yavapo, kureva, Jajpur), uye izvi zvichaitika mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura.

Zvadaro, mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kali Bayalisha’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anotaura nezvezera reKaliyuga nezviratidzo. Mahapurush anoti:

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶରଥ ସୁତ ମୁଣ୍ଡରେ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କ ଚଢ଼ିବ,
ବଜ୍ର ଅଙ୍କ ଦେଇ ଏଗାର ଅଙ୍କ ତହୁଁ ଫେରିବ।
ଶେଷ ଡାହାଣ ଭାଗ ଯାହା ହେଲା ଗାଣ୍ଡିବ ଗୁଣ,
କଳି ଶେଷ ଭୋଗ ଏହିଟି କହିଲି ଅରଜୁନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dasharatha suta muṇḍare tini aṅka chaḍhiba,
bajra aṅka dei egāra aṅka tahuṃ pheriba.
Sheṣa ḍāhāṇa bhāga jāhā helā gāṇḍiba guṇa,
kaḷi sheṣa bhoga ehiṭi kahili Arajuna.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, nyora nhamba 3 pamusoro penhamba yevanakomana vaMambo Dasharatha (kureva, 4^3 = 64). Mahapurush anobva ati chichinja nhamba yacho uye bvisa 11 kubva panhamba iyoyo (Kuchinja 64 kunopa 46, uye 46 - 11 = 35). Mahapurush anoti wedzera nhamba iri kurudyi rwenhamba iyi yakawanikwa ne 'Gandiva' (kureva, 1000) uye anonyora kuti Kaliyuga ichapera munhamba yakawanikwa kana makore 5,000.
Cherechedza: Pano, ne 'Gandiva', Mahapurush ari kureva huwandu hwayo hwekurwisa, hwaive 1,000 Yojanas.

Achyutananda Das ji anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Adi Sanhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ ଯେ ସୁବାହୁ ଯୁଗ କଳି,
କ୍ଷୀଣ ଆୟୁଷ ମହାବଳି।
ପାପେ ସକଳ କ୍ଷୟ ଯିବ,
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଭୋଗ ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

E je subāhu yuga kaḷi,
kṣīṇa āyuṣa mahābaḷi.
pāpe sakaḷa kṣaya jiba,
pāñcha sahasra bhoga heba.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, zera remakore 432,000 eKaliyuga richapfupiswa kusvika kumakore 5,000 chete nekuda kwezvivi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚସହସ୍ର ବରଷ ଭୋଗ ହେବ,
ସେହି ଦିନୁ ସତ୍ୟକଳି ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷi pāñchasahasra baraṣa bhoga heba,
sehi dinu Satyakali prakāsha pāiba.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, chiitiko cheKaliyuga chichava chemakore 5,000. Mushure mezvo, 'Satyakali'—chiedza cheAdya Satya Yuga mukati meKaliyuga—chichazviratidza.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ହଜାରରେ ଗଙ୍ଗାଦେବୀ ମେଳ କଟକ ଠାରେ,
ଭକ୍ତ ଦେଖିବେ ନେତ୍ରରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷi pāñchahajārare Gaṅgādebī meḷa Kaṭaka ṭhāre,
bhakta dekhibe netrare.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura, Mwarikadzi Ganga vachapfuura nepakati peCuttack; zvinoreva kuti, zvose kubva kuKolkata kusvika kuCuttack zvichanyura; pachava nemafashamo anotyisa.

Mahapurush akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର କଳି ହଟେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ,
ମୋର ତଥ୍ୟମାନ ସେତେବେଳେଟି ହୋଇବ।
ପୂର୍ବ ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଆବର ଦକ୍ଷିଣ,
ପ୍ରକାଶ ହେବେ କଳଙ୍କି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବଚନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pāñcha sahasra Kaḷi hāte satya upujiba,
mora tathyamāna setebeḷeṭi hoiba.
pūrba pashchima uttara ābara dakṣiṇa,
prakāsha hebe Kaḷaṅki Achyuta bachana.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura, Kaliyuga ichapera uye Chokwadi (Satya) chichasvika. Ipapo, chiedza chaIshe Kalki chichazviratidza munzira dzose—Kumabvazuva, Kumadokero, Kuchamhembe, uye Kuzasi.

Mutsvene Achyutananda Das anonyorazve mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kaliyuga Bhavishya Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚାରି ଲକ୍ଷ ସପ୍ତ ବିଂଶ ସହସ୍ର,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଆୟୁଷ ହେବ ବିନାଶ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରଟି ଭୋଗ ହୋଇବ,
ସତ୍ୟଯୁଗ ପୁଣି ଉଦୟା ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chāri lakṣa sapta biṃsha sahasra,
Kaḷiyuga āyuṣa heba bināsha.
pāñcha sahasraṭi bhoga hoiba,
Satyayuga puṇi udayā heba.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, makore 427,000 achaparadzwa kubva pazera remakore 432,000 eKaliyuga; makore 5,000 chete achazorarama, mushure mezvo Satya Yuga ichatanga.

Kwete Mahapurush Achyutananda Das chete, asi mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, akakurukurawo nezvezera reKaliyuga muzvinyorwa zvake zvakasiyana-siyana zveMalika. Achitsanangurira mudzidzi wake Baranga mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chumbaka Malika’, akanyora kuti:

Odia Sloka:

ଏବେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ଠିକ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ବାରଙ୍ଗ ବିଚାର ଚିତ୍ତରେ ଘେନ।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ebe pāñcha ṭhika kahibā shuṇa,
Bāraṅga bichāra chittare ghena.
pāñcha sahasra jetebeḷe heba,
sampūrṇa līḷā prakāsha hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, teerera zvakanaka mudzidzi Baranga, bata nhamba 5. Kana makore 5,000 apfuura, ipapo chete ndipo zviito zvose zvaMwari zvaIshe zvichazviratidza.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das akanyorazve mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chumbaka Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ର ଗଲେ,
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଜନମ ଯେ ହୋଇବେ ଭଲେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷiyuga pāñcha sahasra gale,
Viṣṇu janama je hoibe bhale.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura, kuzvarwa patsva kwaIshe Vishnu kuchaitika.

Achitsanangura kuti Kaliyuga ichapera mushure memakore zviuru zvishanu apfuura uye mitambo yaIshe ichaitika, Mahapurush Balarama Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’:

Odia Sloka:

ହସ୍ତିନା କଟକ ଯେବଣ ଭୂଇଁ,
ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ ଦିଲ୍ଲୀ ସହର ସେହି।
ପୂର୍ବର ଚରିତ କହିବା ଶୁଣ,
ଦେବାପି ଅଛନ୍ତି ତପସ୍ୟାରେଣ।
ଉତ୍ତର ଖଣ୍ଡେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରନ୍ତି,
ତପସ୍ୟା ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେ ହୋଇ ନାହାନ୍ତି।
ପଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରେ ବରଷ ପୁଣ,
ତପସ୍ୟା ତାଙ୍କର ହୋଇବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ।
ବାରୁଣା କୁଳେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଭେଟିବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଶ୍ରୀହସ୍ତେ ସିରୀପା ବାନ୍ଧିବେ।
ଶ୍ରୀପଦ୍ମ ହସ୍ତ ତା ମୁଣ୍ଡେ ଲଦିବେ,
ହସ୍ତିନା ରାଜ୍ୟ ନଗ୍ରରେ ସ୍ଥାପିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Hastinā kaṭaka jebaṇa bhūiṃ,
bartamāna Dillī sahara sehi.
Pūrbara charita kahibā shuṇa,
Debāpi achhanti tapasyāreṇa.
Uttara khaṇḍe tapasyā karanti,
tapasyā pūrṇa he hoi nāhānti.
Pañcha sahasre baraṣa puṇa,
tapasyā tāṅkara hoiba pūrṇa.
Bāruṇā kuḷe prabhuṅku bheṭibe,
Prabhu shrīhaste sirīpā bāndhibe.
Shrīpadma hasta tā muṇḍe ladibe,
Hastinā rājya nagrare sthāpibe.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, nyika yaive Hastinapur Cuttack ndiyo guta reDelhi ranhasi. Teerera nyaya yekare—Devaapi ari kuita kutendeuka. Ari kuita kutendeuka mumusha weKalapa mudunhu reUttarakhand muIndia, kuchapedzwa mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura. Mushure mekunge kutendeuka kwapera, achasangana naIshe. Ishe vachaisa korona paari nemaoko avo emaruva uye vachamuita Mambo weHastinapur kana Delhi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kali Kalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦାରୁ ଚଢ଼ି ଦାରୁରୂପ ତେଜିକରି କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହୋଇବ,
ନିଷ୍ଠା ବଚନ ମୋହର ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳିରେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଘଟିବ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ କଳି ପାଞ୍ଚ ସହସ୍ରରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dāru chaḍhi dārurūpa tejikari Kalkī rūpa hoiba,
niṣṭhā bachana mohara Rāmachandra Kaḷire nishche ghaṭiba.
āhuri kathāe shuṇa Rāmadāsa Kaḷi pāñcha sahasrare.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura, Ishe Jagannatha achasiya 'Daaru-rupa' yake kana chimiro chaJagannatha otora chimiro chaKalki.

Kubva mundima dzose dziri pamusoro, zviri pachena kuti zera reKaliyuga rakaonekwa ingori makore 5,000 chete. Haasi makore 432,000 kana makore 10,000.

Asi mubvunzo unokosha unomuka pano— Sezvatinoziva, makore 5,000 eKaliyuga akatopfuura uye parizvino gore rechi 5,127 riri kuenderera. Zvinoenderana nekuverenga uku, Kaliyuga yaifanira kunge yapera panguva ino uye 'Adya Satya Yuga' yaifanira kunge ichitanga! Asi simba reKaliyuga richiri kuoneka kwese kwese. Chivi nekusarurama zvakapararira kwese kwese. Nei paine kupesana uku?

Mhinduro ndeiyi— Chekutanga, makore 5,000 ishoko rakajairika, kwete chairo chaizvo. Makore 5,000 zvinoreva anenge makore 5,000. Ichi ndicho chikonzero nei munzvimbo dzakawanda, makore 5,050 achinyorwawo.

Chechipiri, zvinoenderana neMalika, nguva yeKaliyuga yakatopera chaizvo uye parizvino, 'Sandhikala' (nguva yekuchinja) yeKaliyuga neSatya Yuga iri kuenderera. Zvinoenderana neMalika, panguva iyi yeSandhikala ndipo pachaitika zviitiko zvekuzvarwa kwaIshe Kalki nekugadzwa kweDharma Naye—kureva, kuparadzwa kwevakaipa neruponeso rwevatsvene. Ichi ndicho chikonzero nei chivi nekusarurama zviri pamusoro-soro kunyangwe nguva yeKaliyuga yapera. Zvinofanira kuzivikanwa kuti kana kugadzwa kweDharma kwapera, simba reKaliyuga richapera zvachose uye Chokwadi, Dharma, neRuramisiro zvichagadzwa patsva.

Achyutananda Das akanyorawo mumabhuku ake eMalika nezve kuti kugadzwa kweDharma uku kuchaitika riini pasi pehutungamiriri hwaIshe Kalki.

4.7 Kubatana kweMeena-Shani

Mahapurush akanyora mubhuku rake ‘Aagata Bhavishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଠିକଣା ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବୋଲେ,
ଠ’ ତିନି ବାମେ ପାଞ୍ଚ ରଖିଲେ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ହେ,
ଠକିଯିବ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭଲେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ṭhikaṇā Achyuta bole,
ṭha’ tini bāme pāñcha rakhile Rāmachandra he,
ṭhakijiba mīna shani bhale.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, kana ukaisa mazero matatu nenhamba 5 pamberi pawo (5,000), zvinoreva kuti mushure memakore 5,000 eKaliyuga apfuura, kubatana kweMeena-Shani (Saturn muPisces) kuchaitika, kuchave kwakakosha zvikuru.

5 Meena-Shani Yoga; Zvinoenderana neMalika

5.1 Chii chinonzi Meena-Shani?

Muzvinyorwa zvenyeredzi, Saturn (Shani) inoonekwa seplaneti inofamba zvishoma-zvishoma, ichigara muchiratidzo chimwe cheZodiac kwemakore anenge 2.5. Kune zviratidzo zveZodiac zvakazara gumi nembiri (12) mudenderedzwa reZodiac, uye Saturn inogara mune chimwe nechimwe kwemakore anenge 2.5. Izvi zvinoreva kuti Saturn inotora makore anenge 12 × 2.5 = 30 kuti ipfuure nedenderedzwa rose reZodiac.

Ngatitii Saturn yapinda muchiratidzo cheZodiac nhasi; inodzokera kuchiratidzo chimwe chete mushure memakore anenge 30 mushure mekupedza kufamba kwese. Meena-Shani zvinoreva kufamba kweSaturn muchiratidzo cheZodiac chePisces (Meena).

Nguva yekupedzisira Saturn yakapfuura nePisces yaive muna 1995. Sezvo Saturn ichitora makore anenge 30 kuti idzokere kuchiratidzo chimwe chete, 1995 + 30 = 2025. Naizvozvo, mugore ra2025, Saturn inopinda muPisces zvakare. Mushure ma2025, kufamba kunotevera kuchave 2025 + 30, inova 2054–2055.

Panchasakha Mahapurushas vakatsanangura zvakajeka mumabhuku avo eMalika kuti kuparadzwa kwevakaipa, kuchengetedzwa kwevatsvene, nekugadzwa kweDharma naIshe Kalki zvichanyanya kuitika panguva yeMeena-Shani.

Kuti tinzwisise izvi zviri nani, ngationei dzimwe ndima dzakataurwa muMalika netsanangudzo dzadzo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Prachi Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ମହୀମଣ୍ଡଳରେ ଚମକ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ନ ଜାଣିବେ ହୀନ ଜନ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀରେ ସଂହାର ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ର ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mahīmaṇḍaḷare chamaka paḍiba,
na jāṇibe hīna jana.
Mlechcha māruṇīre saṃhāra hoiba,
mīna shani ra pramāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pachava nekuchema panyika; pachava nemhirizhonga kwese kwese. Hapana mumwe kunze kwevatsvene achakwanisa kunzwisisa izvi. Muzviitiko izvi, vanhu vakaipa nevane chivi vasingaverengeki vachaparadzwa. Mahapurush akati zviitiko izvi zvichaitika panguva yemakore 2.5 yeMeena-Shani Yoga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦା ପ୍ରମାଣ, ଗୁପ୍ତରେ ଲାଗିବ ସେ ମାୟା ରଣ।
