ଥୋକେ ଯିବେ କାମଚଣ୍ଡୀ ମୁଖରେ, ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛ ମାରୁଣୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଗୁପ୍ତରେ।
ତରକ ତେରୁଆ ଯେତେ ଭକତ, ଯୋବରା କାଳିଆବୋଦାରେ ନାଶ।
ଛିନ୍ନମସ୍ତା ଦେବୀ ଖର୍ପର ପାତି, କାଠମାଳିଆଙ୍କୁ ଖାଇବ ବାଛି।
ଘୋର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ସେବେଳେ ହେବ, ସବୁ ବୁଦ୍ଧି ଜ୍ଞାନ ହଜିଣ ଯିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ନ ମିଳିବ ଶସ୍ୟ ନିଅଣ୍ଟି, ମୀନ ଶନିଆସି ଦେବଟି ଘାଣ୍ଟି।
ଲକ୍ଷେ ଚୁଲି ସେହିଦିନ ଫିଟିବ, ହାଡ଼ିଆ କମାର ପରଣୁ ଥିବ।
ସେଦିନ ଯେ ଗୁରୁବାରଟି ଜାଣ, କାଳିଆ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଅଟେ ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jobarā Kāḷiābodā pramāṇa,
guptare lāgiba se māyā raṇa.
Thoke jibe Kāmachaṇḍī mukhare, Mlechcha māruṇī je heba guptare.
Taraka teruā jete bhakata, Jobarā Kāḷiābodāre nāsha.
Chhinnamastā debī kharpara pāti, kāṭhamāḷiāṅku khāiba bāchi.
Ghora andhakāra sebeḷe heba, sabu buddhi jñāna hajiṇa jiba.
Anna na miḷiba shasya niaṇṭi, mīna shaniāsi debaṭi ghāṇṭi.
Lakṣe chuli sehidina phiṭiba, hāḍiā kamāra paraṇu thiba.
Sedina je gurubāraṭi jāṇa, Kāḷiā parīkṣā aṭe pramāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora kuti kana Saturn iri muPisces, panguva iyoyo, miedzo yevanyengeri vose nevasiri vatsvene ichaitika muJobra neKaliaboda yeCuttack. Vanaamai Vatsvene (Vamwarikadzi) vachaparadza vose vasiri vatsvene vakadaro. Panguva iyoyo, pachavawo nerima kwemazuva manomwe neusiku hunomwe. Njere dzemunhu wese dzichamira kushanda. Panguva iyoyo, vatadzi havazokwanisi kuwana kana chikafu.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଅଙ୍କ,
ଭାରତଶ୍ୱରୀ ହେବ ଉତପାତ।
ହେତୁ ରଖିଥାଅ ବ୍ରଜକୁମର,
ସେକାଳେ ତେଜିବି ମୁଁ କଳେବର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna shani heba Mukunda aṅka,
Bhāratashwarī heba utapāta.
Hetu rakhithāa Brajakumara,
sekāḷe tejibi muñ kaḷebara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana Saturn iri muPisces, pachava nemhirizhonga muIndia. Panguva iyoyo, Ishe Mukuru achasiya chimiro chake chevanhuwo zvavo otora chimiro chaKalki chine simba guru.

Mune rimwe bhuku reMalika, Mahapurush akanyora:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନିର ସଂଯୋଗ ହେବାର ସମୟ,
ପୃଥବୀରେ ହେବ ପୁଣି ଘୋର ଯେ ପ୍ରଳୟ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna shanira saṃyoga hebāra samaya,
pṛthabīre heba puṇi ghora je pralaya.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva yekubatana kweMeena-Shani, pachava zvakare nekuparadzwa kunotyisa (Pralaya) Panyika.

Achyutananda Das ji anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ୋଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧୃବ।
ଅଢ଼ୋଇ ବରଷ ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ ଛବିଶ ଅଙ୍କ କୁଅନା,
ଶ୍ମଶାନ ପରାୟ ଏ ସାରା ସଂସାର ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ସିନା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna shani āsi deuthiba ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhōi heba,
dasha aṇā jiba chha' aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.
aḍhāi baraṣa ghora utpāta chhabbisha aṅka kuanā,
shmashāna parāya e sārā saṃsāra uchhanna hoiba sinā.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva iyo Saturn inopfuura nePisces, Hondo Yenyika Yetatu ichatanga Panyika, kusanganisira India. Kwemakore maviri nehafu, kuparadzwa kunotyisa kuchaonekwa Panyika. Nyika yose ichaparadzwa yotora chimiro chenzvimbo yekupisira zvitunha. Panguva iyoyo, vanhu vasingaverengeki vachafa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mune rimwe bhuku reMalika ‘Bhabishyata Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ସାବଧାନ ହୁଅ ତୁହି,
ଠା ଠିକଣା କିଛି ରହିବ ନାହିଁ।
ହେ ଭକ୍ତଗଣ! ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସିଗଲେ ଧାଇଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Sābadhāna hua tuhi,
ṭhā ṭhikaṇā kichhi rahiba nāhiñ.
He bhaktagaṇa! Mīna shani āsigale dhāiñ.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, imi vanhu, chenjerai. Meena-Shani Yoga iri kusvika. Panguva iyoyo, njodzi dzakawanda dzichauya, uye kuparadzwa kuchakanganisa anenge munhu wese; hapana achaponeswa. Njere dzevanhu dzichamira kushanda. Mhirizhonga ichapararira kwese kwese.

Akazvarwa muzana ramakore rechi19, muporofita-mudetembi Bhima Bhoi akanyora pamusoro penyaya iyi mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Padmakalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଖିବ ଜଳ ଅବିଳମ୍ବେ,
ମୀନ ଶନିର ମଧ୍ୟଭାଗେ।
ମୋଗଲ ପଠାଣର ଧାଡ଼ି,
ମଧ୍ୟ ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଥିବ ମାଡ଼ି।
ସିଂହ ମଙ୍ଗଳ ଗ୍ରହ ଦୃଷ୍ଟି,
କିଛି ମେଦିନୀ ଯିବ ଫାଟି।
ଗିରି କନ୍ଦର ଗୁମ୍ଫାମାନ,
ମୋ ଭକ୍ତେ ରହିବେଟି ପୁଣ।
ଆସିବେ ଚଣ୍ଡୀକାଇଗଣ,
ଭକ୍ଷିବେ ନଗ୍ରରେ ପଶିଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shukhiba jaḷa abiḷambe,
mīna shanira madhyabhāge.
Mogala paṭhāṇara dhāḍi,
madhya Oḍishā thiba māḍi.
Siṃha maṅgaḷa graha dṛṣṭi,
kichhi medinī jiba phāṭi.
Giri kandara gumphāmāna,
mo bhakte rahibeṭi puṇa.
Āsibe chaṇḍīkāigaṇa,
bhakṣibe nagrare pashiṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mushure mechikamu chepakati cheMeena-Shani Yoga, nzizi nehova zvichatanga kuoma pakarepo. Mauto enyika dzinovenga achapinda muOdisha orwa. Pakati peMeena-Shani Yoga iyoyo, kana Mars iri muLeo, kudengenyeka kwenyika kuchaitikawo. Ishe achachengeta vatsvene vake vakachengeteka nekungwarira mumapako emakomo. Chandi neYoginis vachaparadza vakaipa nevane chivi.

Mutsvene-mudetembi Narada Das akataurawo nezvemhirizhonga dzinoitika panguva yeMeena-Shani Yoga mubhuku rake reMalika. Akanyora:

Odia Sloka:

ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀଏ ନାଶ ଯିବ ରଙ୍କରେ।
ବତିଶ ତେତିଶ ପୁଣି ଚଉତିରିଶ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ ହେବ ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ।
ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ ଉଲ୍ଲୋଳ,
ଯୋଗିନୀ ହେବେ ପ୍ରବଳ।
ପଡ଼ି ହାଡ଼ କଙ୍କାଳ,
ଗଡ଼ିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ ମାଳ।
କମ୍ପିବ ଭୈରବ ଡାକରେ,
ମନ ତର୍କିଥିବୁ ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare,
prāṇīe nāsha jiba raṅkare.
Batisha tetisha puṇi chautirisha,
mīna shani bhoga heba aḍhāi baraṣa.
Pṛthabī heba ulloḷa,
yoginī hebe prabaḷa.
Paḍi hāḍa kaṅkāḷa,
gaḍiba muṇḍa māḷa.
Kampiba bhairaba ḍākare,
mana tarkithibu tetisha aṅkare.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Meena-Shani Yoga ichagara kwemakore maviri nehafu uye panguva iyi, vanhu vasingaverengeki vachafa. Pachava nekuchema Panyika. Yoginis vachaita chiito chitsvene chekuparadza. Panguva ino, zvitunha zvichapararira munyika yose. Nyika ichadedera nekuomba kwaBhairavi.

Achitaura nhaurirano pakati paIshe Krishna naAmai Rukmini panguva yeDvapara Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Rukmini Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ମେଳ ଗୋ ହୋଇବ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ସେବେଳେ ସମର ହେବ ଭାରତ ମଣ୍ଡଳେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna shani meḷa go hoiba jetebeḷe,
sebeḷe samara heba Bhārata maṇḍaḷe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva iyo Saturn inopfuura muPisces, hondo huru ichaitika muIndia.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ପଡ଼ିଲେଟି ଘୋର ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ମନେ ରଖିଥିବୁ ରାମ ତୁହି ବ୍ରଜସୁତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna shani paḍileṭi ghora utpāta,
mane rakhithibu Rāma tuhi Brajasuta.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana Meena-Shani Yoga ikaitika, mhirizhonga inotyisa ichaonekwa Panyika ino.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku reMalika rinonzi ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ତେରଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହୋଇବ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଢ଼ାଇ ବରଷ ରାଜନ,
ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଗଗନ।
ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ କଷଣ ପଡ଼ିବ,
ଚଉଦ୍ୱାରେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ।
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନିକ ଯନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଚଳ,
ନିକଟରେ ଗୋଳ ଚହଳ।
ତପ ସଙ୍ଗେ ତୋପ ପରୀକ୍ଷା,
ସତ୍ୟ ଅନନ୍ତଟି ଦେଖା।
ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗଟି ହୋଇବ,
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Teradina pakṣa hoiba,
mīna shani yoga paḍiba.
Aḍhāi baraṣa rājana,
dibasare tārā gagana.
Bhaktaṅku kaṣaṇa paḍiba,
Chaudwāre yuddha hoiba.
Baijñānika yantra achaḷa,
nikaṭare goḷa chahaḷa.
Tapa saṅge topa parīkṣā,
satya Anantaṭi dekhā.
Ananta yugaṭi hoiba,
Achyutī kāmanā pūriba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKaliyuga, panguva yekugadzwa kweDharma, vhiki mbiri (Paksha) dzemazuva gumi nematatu dzichaitika. Pachava neMeena-Shani Yoga ichagara kwemakore maviri nehafu. Panguva iyoyo, nyeredzi dzichaonekwa masikati. Munguva iyoyo, vatsvene vachasangana nematambudziko mazhinji. Hondo ichaitika muChoudwar yeCuttack; midziyo yesainzi nemagetsi ichamira kushanda. Hondo huru ichaitika. Misiri dzemazuva ano nemakanoni enyika dzinovenga zvichapikisana nesimba rekuzvipira (Tapa) revatsvene vakakwana veIndia nevarume vakachenjera. Ishe Kalki vachabuda; vatsvene vachagamuchira chiono (Darshan) chaIshe. Zera Risingaperi (Ananta Yuga) richasvika. Chishuwo chaAchyutananda chichazadzikiswa.

Akanyora zvakare mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବସରେ ତାରା ଆକାଶୁ ଖସିବ ମୀନ ଶନିର ରାଜତ୍ୱ,
ବାଡ଼ି ବେମାରିରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dibasare tārā ākāśu khasiba mīna shanira rājatva,
bāḍi bemārire aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nyenyedzi dzichadonha kubva kudenga masikati; vanhu vasingaverengeki vachafa nekuda kwezvirwere nemadenda. Panguva iyoyo, Meena-Shani Yoga ichange ichienderera.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି କରିବ ରାଜତ୍ୱ ମାନବ ମାରୁଣୀ ହେବ,
ପରାଧୀନ ହେବ ଏ ଭାରତବର୍ଷ ଶାସନ କଳ ଲାଗିବ।
ଅରାଜକ ହେବ ଏହି ଜମ୍ବୁଦ୍ବୀପ ଭକ୍ତ ହୋଇବେ ଛାନିଆଁ,
ଅନହୁତି ଅଗ୍ନି ପ୍ରଜ୍ୱଳିତ ହେବ ଉଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ ସାରା ଦୁନିଆ।
ଛିନିଛତ୍ର ହେବ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳ ଥୟ ନ ପଡ଼ିବେ କେହି,
ଚକଡ଼ା ମଡ଼ାଣ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିଣ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କୁହଇ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna shani āsi kariba rājatwa mānaba māruṇī heba,
parādhīna heba e Bhāratabarṣa shāsana kaḷa lāgiba.
Arājaka heba ehi Jambudwīpa bhakta hoibe chhāniāñ,
anahuti agni prajwaḷita heba uchhanna sārā duniā.
Chhinichhatra heba Oḍishā maṇḍaḷa thaya na paḍibe kehi,
Chakaḍā maḍāṇa prakāsha kariṇa dīna Achyuta kuhai.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana Saturn yapinda muPisces, nguva yekuparadzwa kwevanhu ichatanga. Hurongwa hwedemocracy huchaparara, uye India icharasikirwa nehutongi hwayo—inogona kutotonga pasi peumwe munhu. Nyonganyonga ichatora hurongwa hwekutonga, uye nyika ichanyura munyonganiso. Vatsvene vachatyiswa vachiona zviitiko izvi. Moto wekuparadza haungoparadzi Odisha neIndia chete, asi nyika yose.

Achyutananda ji anonyora muKalikalpa Gita yake’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକୁ ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଗମନ ହେଲେ ଏମନ୍ତ ଭିଆଣ,
ଭାରା ନିବାରଣେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଦେବରାୟ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେ ଉହ୍ଲେଇବେ ଜାଣ।
କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna rāshiku Shanishchara gamana hele emanta bhiāṇa,
bhārā nibāraṇe prabhu debarāya martye uhleibe jāṇa.
Kalkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣatrīmānaṅku nikṣatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kuti asunungure Pasi kubva muchivi, Ishe Mahavishnu vachatora chimiro pasi pano. Munguva yeMeena-Shani Yoga, vachitora chimiro cheKalki, Ishe Mukuru vachafamba munyika uye nekuparadza vakaipa nevatadzi, Vachasunungura Amai Pasi.

Mahapurush anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଭାବ ବିନୋଦିଆ ମୂର୍ତ୍ତିଙ୍କୁ ଭାବ,
ଭାବରେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ ଚୁମ୍ବିବ।
ବାଇଆ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଗୀର,
ବାରହାତ ଖଣ୍ଡା ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବିରାଟ ଗାଦୀ ସଂହାର।
ଭକତ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଦାସ,
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହୋଇ ପୃଥବୀ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ ବିଶେଷ।
ମାୟାରେ ବୁଲେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ,
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଅଣାକାର ହୃଦରେ ଘୋଷ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହେବ ଦୃଶ୍ୟ।
ଯୋବରା ଘାଟର ଲୀଳା,
ଜଗିଛି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ କରି ହେଳା,
କଟିବ ପାପୀଙ୍କ ଗଳା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
bhāba binodiā mūrttiṅku bhāba,
bhābare Achyutī dāsa chumbiba.
Bāiā Achyutī gīra,
bārahāta khaṇḍā heba bāhara,
birāṭa gādī saṃhāra.
Bhakata Achyutī dāsa,
bhūmikampa hoi pṛthabī ushwāsa,
bhakta rahibe bisheṣa.
Māyāre bule Achyuta,
mantra aṇākāra hṛdare ghoṣa,
mīna shani heba dṛshya.
Jobarā ghāṭara līḷā,
jagichhi Achyuta na kari heḷā,
kaṭiba pāpīṅka gaḷā.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichaitika. Hondo huru ichaitika muVirat Gadi kana Choudwar yeCuttack, uye bakatwa remaoko gumi nemaviri richauraya vatadzi. Kudengenyeka kwenyika kukuru kuchaitika kuchaparadza vakaipa. Miedzo yevanyengeri vose ichaitika paJobra Ghat yeCuttack, uye Amai Vatsvene vachanwa ropa ravo. Munguva iyi, vatsvene vachadzivirirwa. Mahapurush akanyora kuti zviitiko izvi zvichaitika muMeena-Shani Yoga.

Achyutananda Das ji anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Tattvabodhini’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ରାଶିକି ଶନିଶ୍ଚର ଯିବ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ନଷ୍ଟ ଭ୍ରଷ୍ଟ କରିଦେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna rāshiki Shanishchara jiba,
Oḍishā naṣṭa bhraṣṭa karideba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mushure mekupinda kwaSaturn muPisces, nyika yeOdisha ichakuvadzwa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyorazve mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗ ଦେଶକୁ ପଡ଼ିବ ତେର ଦିନ ପକ୍ଷ ହେବ,
ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ସେଠାବରେ ହେବ ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ମନେ ଭାବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna shani yoga deshaku paḍiba tera dina pakṣa heba,
gupata māruṇī seṭhābare heba Rāmachandra mane bhāba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Meena-Shani Yoga ichaitika, uye pachava nevhiki mbiri dzemazuva gumi nematatu. Panguva imwe chete iyoyo, vanhu vachafa muchivande, uye Dharma ichasimbiswa.

Zvakare mu‘Kalikalpa Gita’, anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଚଳିଯିବ କଳି ଗୋଳ ଉପୁଜିବ ବାବୁ,
ମୀନ ଶନି ହୋଇଥିବ ଗୁପତ ରଖିବୁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chaḷi jiba kaḷi goḷa upujiba bābu,
mīna shani hoithiba gupata rakhibu.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, munguva yeMeena-Shani Yoga, Kaliyuga ichapera uye Dharma ichasimbiswa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyorazve mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛାରଖାର ହେବ ଏ ସୁନା ସଂସାର ଭଇରବୀ ଦେବ ଡାକ,
ଅକାଳ ମରଣ ଗୁପତ ମାରୁଣୀ ମୀନ ଶନିଯୋଗ ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chārakhāra heba e sunā saṃsāra bhairabī deba ḍāka,
akāḷa maraṇa gupata māruṇī mīna shaniyoga heba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nyika ino icha"paradzwa" kana kuparadzwa zvachose. Amai Bhairavi vachabudisa ruzha rukuru; vatadzi vazhinji vachasangana nerufu rusingatarisirwi, uye vazhinji vachafa muchivande. Zviito izvi zvese zvekuparadza zvichaitika munguva yeMeena-Shani Yoga, kureva kana Saturn iripo muchiratidzo chePisces.

Kubva mundima dzeMalika dzapiwa pamusoro, zviri pachena kuti munguva yeMeena-Shani Yoga, zviito zvekuparadza zvaIshe Kalki zvichange zvichinyanya kushanda. Munguva iyi, Pasi rose richapupura zviitiko zvinoparadza zvakakura uye zvisati zvamboitika. Kungochema, hondo, nyonganyonga, kusagadzikana, uye njodzi dzinongoitika dzoga zvichaonekwa kwese kwese. Munguva iyi, vakaipa nevasina kururama vachaparadzwa kubva paPasi rose.

5.2 Mune Meena-Shani ipi chiito chitsvene chaIshe Kalki chichaitika?

Mushure mekuziva kubva mundima dziri pamusoro kuti zviito zvitsvene zvinoparadza zvaKalki zvichaitika munguva yeMeena-Shani, mubvunzo mukuru unomuka: munguva ipi chaiyo yeMeena-Shani zviitiko izvi zvichaitika?

Meena-Shani Yoga inoitika kamwe chete pamakore 30. Naizvozvo, zvakakosha kuziva kuti ndeipi Meena-Shani, maererano neMalika, ichapupura zviitiko zvekugadzwa kweDharma.

Kana tichinyatsoongorora, tinoona kuti Saturn yaive muPisces muna 1995; parizvino, muna 2025, Saturn yapinda muPisces zvakare; ipapo muna 2054, uye anenge makore 30 mushure mezvo, muna 2084, kufamba kwaSaturn muPisces kuchaitika zvakare.

Ndeipi Meena-Shani?

Zvakanyorwa zvakajeka muBhavishya Malika kuti kana zviitiko izvi zvaitika mukati memakore maviri nehafu eMeena-Shani, zita raMambo wePuri, Odisha, panguva iyoyo richava Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chandra Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଥିବ ସେହି ବେଳର,
ପୃଥବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ଘନ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ପଞ୍ଚଭୂତର ଏ ପ୍ରଳୟ ରାଶି,
ଭୂଭାରାକୁ ରାମ ଯିବ ରେ ଗ୍ରାସୀ।
ସେକାଳେ ମୀନେ ଶନି ଥିବ ଚଲି,
ନାଶିବ କାଳ ହେବ ଜଳ ଖେଳି।
ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ କହଇ ଗୁରୁ ସ୍ମରିଣ,
ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର କଳ୍ପରେ ମୁଁ କଲି ବଖାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dibyasiṃha thiba sehi beḷara,
pṛthabī hoijiba ghana andhāra.
pañchabhūtara e pralaya rāśi,
bhūbhārāku rāma jiba re grāsī.
sekāḷe mīne śani thiba chali,
nāśiba kāḷa heba jaḷa kheḷi.
Achyuta kahai guru smariṇa,
chandra kalpare muñ kali bakhāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana Saturn iri muPisces, panguva iyoyo, rima guru richafukidza Pasi kwemazuva 7 neusiku 7. Kuparara kunokonzerwa nezvinhu zvishanu (Pasi, Mvura, Moto, Mhepo, uye Ether) kuchaparadza Pasi rose. Mahapurush akanyora kuti munguva iyoyo, zita raMambo wePuri richava ‘Divyasingha’.

Zvino pane zvimwe zvakakosha kucherechedza pano. Mambo wePuri wazvino, Shri Divyasingha Deva (IV), parizvino ane makore anenge 72. Mune inotevera Meena-Shani—kureva, makore 30 kubva zvino—zera rake richava 100+ uye pane mukana usina kana diki wekuti achange achiri pachigaro chePuri panguva iyoyo!

Zvino vamwe vanhu, vanotaura nguva yezviitiko seMeena-Shani inotevera (kureva, gore ra2054–2057), vanofunga kuti zviitiko izvi zvinogona kuitika panguva yekutonga kwemumwe mambo anonzi Divyasingha! Asi regai ndikuudzei kuti izvi hazvigoneke. Mambo wePuri wazvino ndiye Mambo wechi19, uye maererano neMalika, Mambo wechi19 achava Mambo wekupedzisira weKaliyuga. Kureva kuti, maererano neMalika, mushure maMambo wazvino Shri Divyasingha Deva, hapazovi nemamwe Madzimambo ePuri.

Hurukuro iri pamusoro inoratidza kuti zviitiko zvese zvinoparadza zvichaitika munguva yeMeena-Shani Yoga pakati pa2025–2028, panguva yekutonga kwaShri Divyasingha Deva IV.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyorazve mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ବହୁତ ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ପାପର ଭାରା,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ହୋଇବେ ଧରା।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରେ ପାଣି ପଶିବ,
ସିଂହ ଦ୍ୱାରେ ରକ୍ତଧାର ବହିବ।
ଟିକରା ଗୋହିରୀ ହୋଇବ ରଡ଼ି,
ଧାନ ଥିବ ଯିବ କଣ୍ଠା ଅଗାଡ଼ି।
ସେବେଳକୁ ଏକା ଅକ୍ଷର ସାର,
ସପତ ଦିନ ଯେ ହେବ ଅନ୍ଧାର।
ଗାଲୁଆ ମାଲୁଆ ଭକତ ଛନ୍ତି,
ବଗୁଲିଆ କର୍ମେ ପେଟ ପୋଷନ୍ତି।
ଏମାନେ ଯେ ଦେବୀ ଖପରରେ ଯିବେ,
ଭକତମାନେ ଆନନ୍ଦରେ ରହିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bahuta anyāya pāpara bhārā,
Dibyasiṃha deba hoibe dharā.
Baḍa deuḷare pāṇi pashiba,
siṃhadvāre raktadhāra bahiba.
ṭikarā gohirī hoiba raḍi,
dhāna thiba jiba kaṇṭhā agāḍi.
sebeḷaku ekā akṣara sāra,
sapata dina je heba andhāra.
gāluā māluā bhakata chanti,
baguliā karme peṭa poṣanti.
emāne je debī khaparare jibe,
bhaktamāne ānandare rahibe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, muchikamu chekupedzisira cheKaliyuga, pachava nekusaruramisira kwakawanda uye mutoro wakawandisa wechivi. Panguva iyoyo, mvura yegungwa ichapinda muTemberi yeJagannath. Hondo ichaputika mukati metemberi. Rwizi rweropa ruchayerera nepagedhi reShumba (Singhadwara). Panguva iyoyo, rima richafukidza Pasi rose kwemazuva manomwe neusiku manomwe. Vose vari vatsvene vekunyepedzera, vanowana raramo yavo kuburikidza nezviito zvisina kururama, vachaparadzwa neVanaamai Vatsvene. Vatsvene vachapedza nguva yavo vachifara. Munguva iyoyo, Mambo wePuri achava Shri Divyasingha Deva, uyo achabatwa kana kupambwa nevamanikidzi.

Zviri pachena kubva mundima iri pamusoro kuti kana zviitiko zvekugadzwa kweDharma zvaitika, uye kana paine rima kwemazuva manomwe neusiku manomwe paPasi, Mambo wePuri panguva iyoyo achava Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mumwe Mahapurush kubva kuOdisha akazvarwa muzana ramakore rechi20 akanyora mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake kuti mushure megore ra2033, chiedza chenguva itsva (Satyayuga) chichabuda; kureva, maererano neMahapurush, zviito zvitsvene zvese zvekuchinja kwenguva zvichapedzwa gore ra2033 risati rasvika. Tichapa ndima dzine chekuita neizvi pamberi penyu muChitsauko 12. Chinangwa ndechekuti zviitiko zvese zvinoparadza zvichaitika 2033 isati yasvika.

Mahapurush Bhima Bhoi (muimbi-mutsvene bofu akazvarwa muSambalpur, Odisha muzana ramakore rechi19) akanyora pamusoro penyaya iyi mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Padma Kalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ନିଶ୍ଚୟ କଳଙ୍କି ହୋଇବ, ଯେ ଦିନ ମୀନ ଶନି ଭୋଗ।
ପଞ୍ଚଗ୍ରହ ଯେ ଯୁକ୍ତ ହେବେ, ଆଗାମୀ ଅଢ଼ାଇର ଯୋଗେ।
ତଦନ୍ତେ ଷଡ଼ ଗ୍ରହ ଆସି, ଶନି କୁମ୍ଭରୁ ଯିବେ ଖସି।
ପ୍ରବେଶ ମୀନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ, ଟାକିଥାଅ ହେ ସୁଜ୍ଞ ନରେ।
ହୋଇବ ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ ସମର, ଦମନ ହୋଇବ ବାହାର।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ନାଶିବ ଅବହେଳେ, ଜଗତେ ରଖି ବାହୁବଳେ।
ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛଙ୍କୁ ଦମନ ନାଶିବ, ଦୁର୍ଗା ଖପର ପକାଇବ।
ଅସଂଖ୍ୟ କଳି ସେ ଦମନ, ଶୁଣ ହେ ସାଧୁ ସୁଜ୍ଞଜନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Nishchaya Kaḷaṅki hoiba, je dina mīna shani bhoga.
Pañchagraha je yukta hebe, āgāmī aḍhāira joge.
Tadante ṣaḍa graha āsi, shani kumbharu jibe khasi.
Prabesha mīna saṅgatare, ṭākithāa he sujña nare.
Hoiba asaṃkhya samara, damana hoiba bāhāra.
Biṣṇu nāshiba abaheḷe, jagate rakhi bāhubaḷe.
Mlechchaṅku damana nāshiba, Durgā khapara pakāiba.
Asaṃkhya kaḷi se damana, shuṇa he sādhu sujñajana.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana Saturn yapinda muPisces, zviito zvitsvene zvinoparadza zvaIshe Kalki zvichaitika. Nguva iyi ichagara makore maviri nehafu. Munguva iyi, hondo dzisingaverengeki dzicharwiwa pakati penyika dzakasiyana. Ishe Kalki vachadzivirira vatsvene, uye Amai Durga vachaparadza vakaipa nevatadzi.

Mundima iri pamusoro, Mahapurush akataura zvakajeka kuti mune Meena-Shani ipi zviito zvitsvene zvinoparadza zvaKalki zvichaitika. Achitsanangura kuti ndeipi Meena-Shani iyoyo, akati pazuva iro Saturn inopinda muPisces, mapuraneti mashanu anenge atove muPisces. Kureva kuti, pazuva iroro, mapuraneti anosvika 6 anenge ari muPisces—mapuraneti mashanu atovepo uye Saturn imwe, achiita mapuraneti 6.

Kana tichitarisa Meena-Shani ya2025, tinoona kuti chirevo cheMahapurush chiri kuita chokwadi chakakwana. Musi wa29 Kurume 2025, zuva iro Saturn inopinda muPisces, mapuraneti mashanu (Rahu, Venus, Mercury, Zuva, neMwedzi) anenge atove muPisces.

Zvino, kana tichitarisa Meena-Shani ya2054, tinoona kuti musi wa14 Chivabvu 2054, zuva iro Saturn inopinda muPisces, mapuraneti maviri chete (Venus neMars) anenge atove muPisces, uye naSaturn, anoita mapuraneti 3. Asi maererano naMutsvene Bhima Bhoi, panofanira kunge paine mapuraneti anosvika 6 muPisces, kwete 3.

Kana tichitarisa Meena-Shani Yoga yegore ra2084, tinoona kuti musi wa20 Kurume 2084, zuva iro Saturn inopinda muPisces, mapuraneti maviri chete (Mercury neZuva) anenge atove muPisces. Kunyange kusanganisira Saturn, mapuraneti 3. Izvi zvinoreva kuti kufamba kwemapuraneti 6 hakusi kuitika pano futi.

Zviri pachena kuti Mahapurush haasi kutaura nezveMeena-Shani Yoga ya2054 kana 2084. Meena-Shani yaari kutaura nezvayo mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Padma Kalpa’ ndiyo Meena-Shani ya2025.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akataurawo mubhuku rake reMalika kuti ndiani achava Mahant (Musoro) wePadmaban Peeth yake iri muNemalo, Cuttack, kana zviito zvitsvene zvinoparadza izvi zvaitika. Akanyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳରେ,
ନବ୍ୟକ୍ତ କଥା କହିଲି ରାମ ହେତୁ ଚିତ୍ତରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta kaḷi śeṣa beḷare,
Nabyakta kathā kahili rāma hetu chittare.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, O mudzidzi Ramadas, kana Kaliyuga yapera, panguva iyoyo munhu anonzi Gaganānanda achava Musoro wePeeth yangu.

Mahapurush akanyora mune chimwe chinyorwa cheMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଗନାନନ୍ଦ ଯେ ମହନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ ଗଗନେ ଉଡ଼ିବ ଧୂଳି,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶ ଯେ ଚମକି ପଡ଼ିବ ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ହାଲହୁଳି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Gaganānaṃda je mahaṃta hoiba gagane uḍiba dhūḷi,
Oḍishā desha je chamaki paḍiba paḍijiba hālahuḷi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, zviito zvaMwari zvichaitika panguva yaMahant Gaganānanda. Panguva iyoyo, pachava nekupopota munyika yeOdisha.

5.3 Mahant Gaganānanda Goswami

Zvinofanira kucherechedzwa kuti muchizvarwa chechi14 chaMahapurush Achyutananda Das, muzukuru wake uye parizvino ane makore 85–90 Shri Gaganānanda Goswamiji ndiye Musoro wechigaro cheumwari chaMahapurush, Padmaban, Nemalo. Izvi zvinoitawo kuti zvive pachena kuti Mahapurush ari kutaura nezvenguva iripo.

Kubva pamapoinzi mana ari pamusoro, zviri pachena kuti maererano neMalika, Meena-Shani umo zviito zvinoparadza zvaMwari zvaKalki zvichaitika ichauya gore ra2033 risati rasvika, uye panguva iyoyo yeMeena-Shani, Shri Divyasingha Deva achava Mambo wePuri uye Shri Gaganānanda achava Mahant wePadmaban Peeth, Nemalo. Kwete izvi chete, asi pazuva iro Saturn inopinda muPisces, Shad-graha (mapuraneti matanhatu) Yoga ichange ichiumba muPisces.

Zviri pachena kuti Mahapurush ari kunongedza kuMeena-Shani inosvika pakati pa2025–2028 nekuti mapoinzi ese mana ari pamusoro anoshanda chete kuMeena-Shani Yoga iyi uye hapana imwe.

Hazvidi kutaura kuti maererano neMalika, zviitiko zvikuru zvinoparadza zvichaitwa naIshe Kalki zvichaitika pakati pa2025–2028.

Cherechedza: Mazuva ano vamwe vanhu vari kutaura kuti Meena-Shani inotaurwa muMalika haisi Meena-Shani iripo, asi inotevera inosvika muna 2054! Zvingave zvakakodzera kuti vatsvene vese vane kutenda muMalika vasavhiringidzike neizvi, nekuti mashoko akadaro akanyatso kukanganisa.

6 Mambo wePuri pakupera kweKaliyuga

Muchitsauko chakapfuura, takaona kuti zviitiko zvikuru zvese zvekusimbiswa kweDharma zvichaitika panguva yekutonga kwaMambo wePuri, Shri Divyasingha Deva. Muchitsauko chino, tichanzwisisa izvi kuburikidza nemamwe mavhesi eMalika.

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, akanyora muchinyorwa chake cheMalika ‘Kali Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ପୁରୁଷୋତ୍ତମ ଦେବ ରାଜା ଠାରୁ,
ଉନ୍ନବିଂଶ ରାଜା ହେବ ସେଠାରୁ।
ସେଠାରୁ ପୁଣ ରାଜା ନାହିଁ ଆଉ,
ଅକୁଳି ହୋଇବେ କୁଳକୁ ବହୁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Puruṣottama Deba rājā ṭhāru,
unnabiṃsha rājā heba seṭhāru.
Seṭhāru puṇa rājā nāhiṃ āu,
akuḷi hoibe kuḷaku bahu.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kutanga naMambo Purushottama Deva mushure maMambo Indradyumna, huwandu hwemadzimambo 19 huchava Madzimambo eJagannath Puri panguva dzakasiyana. Mahapurush akanyora kuti Mambo wechi19 achange asina mwanakomana uye achava mambo wekupedzisira; kureva kuti, hapana mumwe munhu achava mambo mushure make.

Kana tikacherechedza mamiriro ezvinhu aripo, tinoona kuti Mambo wePuri aripo ndiye Mambo wechi19 uye haanawo mwanakomana. Chiratidzo chakajeka chaMahapurush ndechekuti mitambo yese yaKalki yekusimbiswa kweDharma ichaitika panguva yekutonga kwamambo aripo.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora muchinyorwa chake cheMalika ‘Adi Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନାମେ ରାୟେ,
ଏ ଯୁଗ ପ୍ରାନ୍ତେ ସେ ଉଦୟେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shri Dibyasiṃha nāme rāye,
e juga prānte se udaye.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakuguma kweKaliyuga, Shri Divyasingha achava Mambo wePuri.

Mune chimwe chinyorwa cheMalika, akanyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଅଙ୍କେ ବାବୁ ସରବ ଦେଖିବୁ,
ଛାଡ଼ି ଚକା ଗଲୁ ବୋଲି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣିବୁ,
ନର ବାଳୁତ ରୂପରେ ଆମ୍ଭେ ଜନମିବୁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dibyasiṃha aṅke bābu saraba dekhibu,
chhāḍi chakā galu boli nishchaya jānibu,
nara bāḷuta rūpare āmbhe janamibu.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana kutonga kwaMambo anonzi Shri Divyasingha Deva kuri kuenderera mberi muPuri, panguva iyoyo mitambo yese yeumwari yaIshe Kalki ichaitika. Panguva iyoyo, Ishe achasiya Temberi Yake yeJagannath uye achazvarwa mumuviri wemunhu seKalki Avatar.

Zvadaro muchinyorwa cheMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’, Achyutanandaji anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ବୃଦ୍ଧ ବାଳକ ରାଜା ହୋଇଥିବ,
ଗୁପତରେ ସଣ ସାଲ ଚାଲିବ।
ବାଳବୃଦ୍ଧ ଅଙ୍କେ ବତାସ ହେବ,
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ।
ଜଳ ପବନ ବତାସ ବହିବ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୀନମାନ ବହୁ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bruddha bāḷaka rājā hoithiba,
gupatare saṇa sāla chāliba.
bāḷabruddha aṅke batāsha heba,
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba.
jaḷa pabana batāsha bahiba,
pṛthibī hīnamāna bahu hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, “Baalak-Briddha” achava Mambo wePuri; kureva kuti, munhu achava mambo anokwira chigaro achiri mudiki uye anoramba ari pachigaro ichocho kusvika akwegura. Panguva yekutonga kwake, zviitiko zvakadai semafashamo emvura nemadutu emhepo zvichaitika.

Zvinozivikanwa kuti pakati pemadzimambo akaitwa kusvika zvino, hapana kunze kwaShri Divyasingha Deva akave mambo kubva paudiki kusvika pakukwegura. Mambo aripo Shri Divyasingha Deva akakwira chigaro musi waChikunguru 7, 1970, aine makore 17 chete. Achiri pachigaro ichi, akapinda mukukwegura uye parizvino ane makore anenge 72. Kuburikidza nerwiyo urwu, nemutauro wechiratidzo, Mahapurush akazivisa kuti Divyasingha Deva achava mambo pakuguma kweKaliyuga.

Achyutananda Dasji akanyorazve mu‘Kalikalpa Gita’ yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ଅଙ୍କରେ ଜାଣ,
ଧୂମକେତୁ ହେବ ପୂର୍ବଭାଗେଣ।
ଉଲ୍‌କାପାତ ହେବ ହାଲହୁଳି,
ଶ୍ୱାନ ଶୃଗାଳ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ବୋବାଳି।
ମାତିବେ କାଳୀ ଯେ କରାଳୀଗଣ,
ଲୋକେ ମରିବେ ହୋଇ ରଣଭଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dibyasiṃha Deba aṅkare jāṇa,
dhūmaketu heba pūrbabhāgeṇa.
ulkāpāta heba hālahuḷi,
shwāna shṛgāḷa chhāḍibe bobāḷi.
mātibe kāḷī je karāḷīgaṇa,
loke maribe hoi raṇabhaṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana Shri Divyasingha Deva ari Mambo wePuri, panguva iyoyo nyenyedzi ine muswe ichaonekwa kumabvazuva. Pachava nemvura yenyenyedzi pasi rose. Mhuka dzinodya nyama dzakadai sembwa nemakava dzichasiya masango dzichinanga kunzvimbo dzekugara. Amai Kali nevamwe vavo vechiYogini vachaparadza vatadzi Pasi, zvichikonzera kufa kwevanhu vasingaverengeki.

Mahapurush akazivisa muchidimbu kuburikidza nerwiyo rwuri pamusoro kuti zviitiko zvekusimbiswa kweDharma zvichaitika panguva yekutonga kwaMambo wePuri anonzi Shri Divyasingha Deva pakuguma kweKaliyuga.

Mahapurush akati kumudzidzi wake Ramadas mune chimwe chinyorwa cheMalika:

Odia Sloka:

କେଉଁ କାଳ ବୋଲି ବୋଇଲୁ ଶୁଣ ରାମ ରତନ,
ଥିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ରାଜନ କୋଳେ କନ୍ୟା ରତନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Keuñ kāḷa boli boilu śuṇa Rāma ratana,
thiba Dibyasiṃha rājana koḷe kanyā ratana.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, O mudzidzi Ramadas, teerera kuti ndiri kutaura nezvenguva ipi—panguva ino zviitiko izvi zvichaitika, kutonga kwaMambo anonzi Divyasingha kuchange kuchienderera mberi, uyo asingazove nemwanakomana; vanasikana chete.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake zveMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ବାରଣ ବେଳକୁ କରିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପତି,
ତାଙ୍କ କୁଳେ ପୁତ୍ର ନୋହିବ ଭଣେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bāra bāra beḷaku kariba Dibyasiṃha nṛpati,
tāṅka kuḷe putra nohiba bhaṇe dīna Achyutī.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakuguma kweKaliyuga, pachava nekutonga kwaMambo anonzi Divyasingha uyo asingazove nemwanakomana.

Uprofita uhu hwaMahapurush hunokodzerawo Mambo wePuri aripo, Shri Divyasingha Deva, zvakakwana. Uku zvakare humbowo hwekuti Kaliyuga ichapera panguva yekutonga kwaMambo Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake zveMalika, ‘Kaliyuga Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mashoko akataurwa naIshe Krishna kuna Arjuna muDwaparayuga, umo Ishe Krishna akati:

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shrikshetra rājana mora na sebi payara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakuguma kweKaliyuga, Mambo wePuri haazozadzisi basa rangu rezuva nezuva (muchiitiko chaJagannath).

Achyutananda Dasji akabva anyora:

Odia Sloka:

ନ ଥିବ ରାଜନ ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ମଉଡ଼ ମଣି,
ରାଜ୍ୟ ରାଜକୁଳ କନ୍ୟାକୁ ନ କରିବ ବର୍ଣ୍ଣି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Na thiba rājana Shrīkṣetre kṣetra mauḍa maṇi,
rājya rājakuḷa kanyāku na kariba barṇṇi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Mambo wePuri haazogari muShrikshetra kana Puri, asi achagara kumwe. Haazoroori mwanasikana wamambo wemhuri yehumambo yeOdisha asi acharoora mwanasikana wamambo kubva kune imwe nyika.

Mutsvene mukuru weOdisha akazvarwa muzana ramakore rechi18, Mahapurush Arakshita Das, akanyora muchinyorwa chake cheMalika ‘Dwadasa Chandrika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଆସିବେ ଫେରି,
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରକୁ ନ ଯାଇ ରହିବେ ଜାଣ ସେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି।
ଭେଦ ମୁଁ କାହୁଁ ଜାଣିଥାନ୍ତି ଆଦିମାତା କହିଲେ,
ଭଣେ ଅରକ୍ଷିତ ଅଧମ ତହୁଁ ଶୁଣିଲି ଭଲେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati nishchaya āsibe pheri,
kṣhetraku na jāi rahibe jāṇa se Khaṇḍagiri.
bheda muñ kāhuñ jāṇithānti Ādimātā kahile,
bhaṇe Arakṣhita adhama tahuñ śuṇili bhale.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush anonyora kuti pakuguma kweKaliyuga, Divyasingha Deva achava Mambo wePuri uye haazogari muShrikshetra kana Puri, asi achagara munzvimbo yeKhandagiri kana Bhubaneswar. Mahapurush anonyorazve kuti iye pachake aigona sei kuziva chakavanzika chezvinhu izvi; Amai Vekutanga (Adi Mata) vakamuudza zvinhu izvi.

Ndirege ndizivise vaverengi kuti madzimambo ese asati asvika Mambo aripo aigara muPuri, Odisha, uye achiita basa rezuva nezuva raIshe Jagannath, asi Mambo aripo Shri Divyasingha Deva anoita basa raIshe Jagannath chete pane dzimwe nguva dzakakosha. Haagari muPuri asi anogara muShahid Nagar, Bhubaneswar. Uyezve, Shri Divyasingha Deva haana kuroora mwanasikana wamambo kubva kuOdisha asi akaroora mwanasikana waLieutenant Colonel Anant Singh Charak weJammu.

Uprofita hutatu huri pamusoro hunokodzera Mambo wePuri aripo, Shri Divyasingha Deva, zvakakwana. Uku zvakare humbowo hwekuti Kaliyuga ichapera panguva yekutonga kwaMambo Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake zveMalika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Māgha Saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājattwa karibe ehi Ekāmra Nagara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana Divyasingha Deva ari Mambo wePuri, ipapo kutonga kwaAnanta Keshari, kureva, Ishe Kalki, kuchaitika. Richava zuva reMagha Saptami; Bhubaneswar (Ekambra Nagar) ichava nzvimbo.

Rwiyo urwu runoratidzawo kuti Mambo wePuri panguva yemitambo yeumwari yaKalki haazove mumwe kunze kwaShri Divyasingha Deva.

Mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Mohakalpa’, Achyutanandaji akapa chimwe chiratidzo chichiita kuti zvive pachena kuti Shri Divyasingha Deva aizova mambo wekupedzisira wePuri. Mahapurush anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଧନୁଶର ଧରି ଆଗେ ସେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ନୃପତି ଯେ ଜାଣ ବୈଷ୍ଣବ ଧର୍ମ ଛାଡିବେ।
କ୍ଷତ୍ରି ବୃତ୍ତି ଧରି ପଦାତିଙ୍କ ସହ କରିବେ ବହୁ ସମର,
ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କ ଦେଖି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଡିବେ ପଦ୍ମପାଦର।
ନବଲକ୍ଷ ଧର୍ମ-ହୀନ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ତହିଁ ତୁରିତେ ହେବେ ଛେଦନ,
ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଘାତେ ତହିଁ ମେଦିନୀ ଯେ କମ୍ପମାନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଠାରୁ ଆଦି ଓଡିଶା ରାଜ୍ୟରେ ଯେତେକ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଥିବେ,
ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେଇ ସମସ୍ତେ ତହିଁ ମରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ର ପୁଛିବେ,
ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ହତ୍ୟା ବୋଲି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣମାନଙ୍କୁ କହି ବଧ୍ୟ ନ କରିବେ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପରୀକ୍ଷା କରିବେ ଗାୟତ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ଜାଣ,
ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣଟି ପରୀକ୍ଷା ନ ଦେବ ନାଶିବ ଯେ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସୁଦର୍ଶନ ଚକ୍ର ଶୂନ୍ୟ ଗଗନେ ରଖିବେ,
ଚଉଷଠି ଅକ୍ଷର ପ୍ରମାଣ ନ ପାଇ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଥାଇ ଛେଦିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Lalāṭa Kesharī dhanushara dhari āge se juddha karibe,
Dibyasiṃha Deba nṛpati je jāṇa baiṣṇaba dharma chhāḍibe.
Kṣatri bṛutti dhari padātiṅka saha karibe bahu samara,
Prabhuṅka dekhi Dibyasiṃha Deba paḍibe padmapādara.
Nabalakṣa dharma-hīna byakti tahiṃ turite hebe chhedana,
Lalāṭa Kesharī juddha ghāte tahiṃ medinī je kampamāna.
Kṣetra ṭhāru ādi Oḍishā rājyare jeteka brāhmaṇa thibe,
Gāyatrī mantraku parīkṣā na dei samaste tahiṃ maribe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba brāhmaṇamānaṅku Gāyatrī mantra puchhibe,
Brāhmaṇa hatyā boli brāhmaṇamānaṅku kahi badhya na karibe.
Dibyasiṃha Deba parīkṣā karibe Gāyatrī mantraku jāṇa,
Jeuṃ brāhmaṇaṭi parīkṣā na deba nāshiba je Sudarshana.
Ananta Kesharī Sudarshana chakra shūnya gagane rakhibe,
Chauṣaṭhi akṣara pramāṇa na pāi shūnyare thāi chhedibe.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, Mambo wePuri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, achasiya Vaishnava Dharma yake uye achatora basa reKshatriya uye achirwa hondo dzakawanda. Paachaona Ishe Kalki, achawira patsoka dzaIshe, otora maropafadzo aIshe, uye achaparadza vanyengeri vanosvika mamiriyoni mapfumbamwe. Munzvimbo yeJagannath, Puri, maBrahmins ese aripo achaedzwa neGayatri Mantra. Achitya chivi chekuuraya maBrahmins, Divyasingha Deva haazourayi maBrahmins anokundikana muyedzo. Mahapurush anonyora kuti vese vachaparadzwa neSudarshana Chakra yaIshe pachayo. Ishe vachaisa Sudarshana Chakra Yavo mudenga risina chinhu uye vachagura misoro yemaBrahmins iwayo nechakra imwe chete vasingaverengi Gayatri Mantra.

Pano, Mahapurush anopa humbowo hwekuti Mambo wePuri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, achasangana naIshe Kalki uye achabatsira mukusimbisa Dharma. Zviri pachena kuti mitambo yechimwari yekuparadza yaIshe Kalki ichaitika panguva yehutongi hwaShri Divyasingha Deva.

Zvadaro Mahapurush anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ କ୍ଷତ୍ରିୟ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଫିରିଙ୍ଗୀ କରିଥିବେ ସେ ବନ୍ଧନ।
କ୍ଷେତ୍ରର ନୃପତି ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଯେ ରାଜନ,
ତାହାଙ୍କୁ ଯେ ରଖିଥିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଗୋପନ।
ବନ୍ଧନର ଦୁଇ ଦିନରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି,
ରାତ୍ରିକାଳେ ରାଜା ପାଶେ ବିଜେ ଯେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ରାଜାର ବନ୍ଧନ ଫେଇ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବାସୁ ଦେବେ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ତଳ ପୁରେ ଗୋପନେ ରଖିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dibyasiṃha Deba se je Kṣhatriya rājana,
tāhānku Phiringī karithibe se bandhana.
Kṣhetrara nṛupati Dibyasiṃha je rājana,
tāhānku je rakhithibe Shrī Hari gopana.
Bandhanara dui dinare Prabhu je Shrī Hari,
rātrikāḷe rājā pāśe bije je Shrī Hari.
rājāra bandhana phei Prabhu bāśu debe,
Khaṇḍagiri taḷa pure gopane rakhibe.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, kana Mambo wePuri, Shri Divyasingha Deva, akatorwa nhapwa nemauto ekunze, ipapo mazuva maviri gare gare, Ishe Kalki vachasvika kuna mambo usiku, vachimusunungura kubva muhusungwa, uye vachimuvanza pamakumbo egomo reKhandagiri.

Uyezve, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Padmakalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମୋ ଭକ୍ତ ରହିବେ,
ସେହିବେଳେ ସରକାର ଖାଦ୍ୟ ପକାଇବେ।
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଲଲାଟ କେଶରୀ ଆଦିକରି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ananta gumphā madhyare mo bhakta rahibe,
sehibeḷe sarakāra khādya pakāibe.
Ananta Keshari swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha Lalāṭa Keshari ādikari.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, kana njodzi huru yemvura ikaitika, vateveri vatsvene vaIshe Kalki vachagara vakachengeteka mubako reAnanta riri pagomo reKhandagiri. Panguva iyoyo, hurumende ichapa chikafu nezvinhu zvekubatsira kune vakawirwa nenjodzi. Mahapurush akanyora kuti panguva iyoyo, Ananta Madhava, kureva Ishe Kalki, vachange vari muKhandagiri, Odisha. Mambo Divyasingha Deva vachange variwo Navo.

Mahapurush akanyorazve mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Kalpa Tika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ସ୍ୱାମୀ ଥିବେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ସେ ଯେ ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାବରେ ସେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କୁ ଦେଖିବେ,
ଲଲାଟକେଶରୀ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହକୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Anaṃta kesharī swāmī thibe Khaṇḍagiri,
Dibyasiṃha deba se je lalāṭakesharī.
Khaṇḍagiri ṭhābare se prabhuṃku dekhibe,
Lalāṭakesharī Dibyasiṃhaku chihnibe.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, kana Ishe Ananta Keshari, kureva Ishe Kalki, vari muKhandagiri, ipapo panguva iyoyo Divyasingha Deva achange ariwo muKhandagiri. MuKhandagiri, Divyasingha Deva achawana chiono (Darshan) chaIshe.

Kubva mundima dzose nhatu dzapiwa pamusoro, zvinozivikanwawo kuti panguva yemitambo yechimwari yaIshe Kalki, Mambo wePuri achange ari Divyasingha Deva.

6.1 Kusangana kwaIshe Kalki naBaladeva naDivyasingha Deva neMadzimambo Mazhinji

Bhavishya Malika izere nezviitiko zvakavanzika zvakawanda nezviporofita. Pakati pezviporofita izvi pane chiporofita chemadzimambo mazhinji achisangana naIshe.

Mahapurush anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika kuti mune ramangwana, nguva ichasvika apo masoja ekunze achagara kubva kuChhatia, Jajpur kusvika kuNilagiri kana Nilachal, Puri. Kunyange hondo huru yemuvengi ichapinda mukati meJagannath Temple. Achiona zvese izvi mukutambudzika kukuru, Ishe, vakanyura mukunamata, vachayeuka Sahadeva, Nakula, Ashwatthama, naShalya. Pakarepo Ishe pavachavayeuka, kufungisisa nekunamata kwevarwi vana ava vane ushingi, nyanzvi mukupfura uta, kuchaputsika, uye vese vana vachasvika kuChhatia vachawira patsoka dzaIshe, vachikumbira murairo Wavo. Ishe vacharayira vana vavo kuti vaparadze hondo huru yeMughal uye vachengetedze vatsvene. Zvinoenderana nemurairo waIshe, vana vavo vachaparadza masoja eMughal vozodzokera kuna Ishe. Panguva iyoyo, vatsvene vasingaverengeki vachange vari naIshe muChhatia, uye kwemazuva 27, Chhatia ichava nzvimbo yekusangana yevatsvene. Panguva ino, madzimambo eRussia, Nepal, Manipur, Ali, Kujang, Kanika, nezvimwe, vachasangana naIshe, vachigamuchira Darshan Yavo, uye vachizvipira patsoka Dzavo. Panguva iyoyo, Ishe vachatora vese vavo vosvika kuSealdah kunokumbira Ishe Baladeva kuti vaparadze hondo yasara yekunze. Nenzira iyi, madzimambo ese ari naIshe achaenda kuSealdah kunosangana naBaladeva, uko Arjuna naBhima vachange vatovepo.

Pano Mahapurush anonyora kuti panguva ino, pakati pemadzimambo achasangana naIshe Ananta Keshari naIshe Baladeva, Mambo wePuri, Shrikshetra, achange aripowo. Achiratidza kuti ndiani achange ari, Mahapurush anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika:

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଯେ ରାଜା ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗତେ ସ୍ଥିତି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kṣhetre je rājā Dibyasiṃha Deba tāhāṅka saṅgate sthiti.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, pakati pemadzimambo anogamuchira Darshan yaIshe pachange paine Mambo weShrikshetra Puri, Divyasingha Deva.

Kubva muhurukuro dziri pamusoro, zviri pachenawo kuti Mambo wePuri panguva yemitambo yechimwari yaKalki achange ari Shri Divyasingha Deva.

Kubva mundima 17 dziri pamusoro, zvinoratidzwa zvakajeka kuti pakupera kweKaliyuga, kana Ishe Kalki vazvarwa uye kana mitambo Yavo yekuparadza ikaitika pasi rose, kana kuparadzwa kwevakaipa, ruponeso rwevatsvene, uye basa rekusimbisa Dharma zvichiitika, panguva iyoyo mambo anonzi Divyasingha Deva achange agere pachigaro cheJagannath Puri, uye achange ariwo mambo wekupedzisira kugara pachinzvimbo ichi.

Shri Divyasingha Deva achange ari mambo wekupedzisira—izvi zvakanyorwa naMahapurush Jagannath Das, mumwe wePanchasakhas, mubhuku rake reMalika, ratakataura mundima yekutanga yechitsauko chino.

Asi chinhu chinokosha ndechekuti kwete Jagannath Das chete, asi mumwe Mahapurush pakati pePanchasakhas, Shishu Ananta Das, akanyorawo pamusoro penyaya iyi mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Patta Madana’. Mahapurush anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଆଉ ଶାସନ ନ ଥିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dibyasiṃha nṛupa rājattwa karibe āu śāsana na thiba.

Zvinoreva:
Zvichireva kuti, Mambo Divyasingha Deva vachatonga uye mushure make, hapazovi nekutonga kwemumwe mambo. Divyasingha Deva achange ari mambo wekupedzisira. Mune mamwe mazwi, Kaliyuga ichapera panguva yake, uye mitambo yechimwari yekuparadza yaIshe Kalki ichaitika.

6.2 Shri Divyasingha Deva

Chokwadi chinokosha pano ndechekuti Mahapurush haana kungomira pakutaura kuti zita raMambo wePuri pakupera kweKaliyuga raizova Divyasingha Deva; asi, akataura zvinhu zvakawanda pamusoro pazvo, zvakadai sokuti aizova mambo wechi19, aizova Divyasingha Deva wechina, aizova asina mwanakomana, aizova nevanasikana chete, haaizogara muPuri asi muBhubaneswar, haaizokwanisa kuita mabasa ezuva nezuva aIshe Jagannath, aizotora umambo achiri mudiki uye oramba ari mambo kusvika pakukwegura, nezvimwe.

Zvinhu izvi zvose zvinokodzera Mambo aripo Shri Divyasingha Deva zvakakwana.

7 Zviratidzo zvekupera kweKaliyuga paTemberi yeJagannath

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das nevamwe Panchasakhas vakanyora munzvimbo dzakawanda mumabhuku avo eMalika kuti vatsvene vachagamuchira zviratidzo zvakawanda kubva kuJagannath Temple kuburikidza nazvo vachakwanisa kuziva kuti Kali Yuga yapera uye Ishe Kalki vazvarwa. Zviratidzo izvi zvichaonekwa kwete chete kubva kuJagannath Temple asiwo kubva kune mamwe mamonasteri netemberi muIndia, asi kunyanya zvichaonekwa kubva kuJagannath Temple iri muPuri.

Odia Sloka:

ବାରମ୍ବାର ଶ୍ରୀ ମନ୍ଦିରରେ ପୁଣି,
ନାନା ଅଘଟଣ ସତ୍ୟ ମୋ ବାଣୀ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bārambāra shrīmandirare puṇi,
nānā aghaṭana satya mo bāṇī.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika kuti pakupera kweKali Yuga, marudzi akasiyana-siyana enjodzi (zviratidzo zvakaipa) achaonekwa kakawanda muShrimandir (Jagannath Temple) iri muPuri. Akataurazve kuti shoko rangu iri richaratidza kuva chokwadi chose.

7.1 Nhoroondo yezviratidzo zvakaipa paJagannath Temple

Nhasi tinoona kuti mumakore 15-20 apfuura, zviratidzo zvakaipa zvakawanda zvakaonekwa paJagannath Temple, zvakadai sedombo guru rawa kubva pa"Amla Bedha" yetemberi mugore ra1990; matombo achiwira pasi kubva pazvivakwa zvepamusoro zvetemberi, kusanganisira sanctum sanctorum, kakawanda mumakore achangopfuura; shiri dzakaipa (dzinodya chitunha) dzichigara pamureza wetemberi neAruna Stambha pamberi peSinghadwara (Gedhi reShumba); magunguo asingaverengeki achitenderera pamusoro petemberi; chifukidziro chine matombo anokosha (Ratnachandua) chakaiswa pamusoro pevamwari chichibata moto; 'Patitapabana Bana' (mureza) wakasimudzwa pamusoro petemberi uchibata moto; Neelachakra inorema matani 2.2, yakagadzirwa neAshtadhatu (simbi sere), ichikombama nemhepo ine simba guru; 'Patitapabana Bana' ichipeperetswa ichiwira mugungwa panguva yedutu guru; bazi reKalpavata hombe (muti wemukuyu) riri munzvimbo yetemberi richiputsika; Patitapabana Bana ichipisa nekuda kwemheni; zvitofu zvevhu zvinosvika 40 zvichiparadzwa nevatsotsi mukicheni tsvene yetemberi; mitsetse ichiwanikwa muAruna Stambha yetemberi, danda riri mukati meNatamandapa, horo iri pamberi pe sanctum sanctorum (Jagamohana), uye pedyo neSinghadwara; dombo re'Padma Pakhuda' (petali relotus) richishaikwa kubva paRatnasinghasana (chigaro chine matombo anokosha); mheni ichirova kicheni yetemberi; kuba kuchiitika mutemberi usiku; kuwedzera kwemakonzo mutemberi nekuda kwekuwanda kwevatsvene panguva yeCorona pandemic, zvichikonzera kusagadzikana uye kukanganisa tsika dzetemberi; ropa richionekwa munzvimbo dzakawanda kusanganisira Ratnasinghasana yetemberi, zvichiteverwa nekushambidzwa kukuru kwetsika (Mahasnana) kweChaturdha Murti (vamwari vana); denga richitsvuka kumativi ose etemberi; kunyangarika kamwe kamwe kwemazizi akamboonwa akawanda mutemberi; kushandiswa kwenharembozha kunyange zvazvo dzakarambidzwa mutemberi; kurekodha vhidhiyo yekunyengera munzvimbo yetemberi uchishandisa makamera e-spy akavanzwa mumagirazi; kupinda kwevasiri maHindu nevasiri maSanatani mutemberi kunyange zvazvo zvakarambidzwa; kuzviuraya kana kufa nekuda kwezvimwe zvikonzero zvichiitika mukati metemberi; kufa paBaisi Pahacha, kureva, matanho 22 etemberi; mitsetse ichiwanikwa muGumuta (dome) reHasti Dwara (Gedhi reMaodzanyemba) yetemberi, nezvimwe.

Zviporofita zvakasiyana-siyana pamusoro pezvimwe zvezviitiko izvi zvinowanikwa mumabhuku akasiyana-siyana eMalika, umo zvinotaurwa zvakajeka kuti zviitiko izvi zvichaitika chete pakupera kweKali Yuga uye zvichapa vatsvene chiratidzo chekuburuka kwaIshe Kalki pasi. Naizvozvo, avo vanoratidza kusahadzika kwavo nekusatenda nhasi pamusoro pekupera kweKali Yuga nekuzvarwa kwaIshe Kalki vanofanira kufungisisa zvakare pfungwa dzavo.




Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mu'Jaiphula Malika' yake:

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁଁ ପଥର,
ଖସୁଥିବ ପୁଣି ଥରକୁ ଥର ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ତୁହି ଦେଖିବୁ ବେନି ନେତ୍ରର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Baḍa deuluṃ pathara,
khasuthiba puṇi tharaku thara lo jāiphula,
tuhi dekhibu beni netrara.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush akanyora kuti panguva yataurwa, zviitiko zvematombo achiwira kakawanda muJagannath Temple iri muPuri zvichabuda pachena, izvo vatsvene vachaona zvakajeka nemaziso avo.

Kana tikafunga chokwadi chechiporofita ichi, tichaona kuti mushure medombo rinorema matani 5-6 rawa kubva pa"Amla Bedha" yetemberi musi waChikumi 14, 1990, zviitiko zvakadaro zvakaitika kakawanda. Musi waNyamavhuvhu 13, 1992, matombo maviri makuru akawira zvakananga mu sanctum sanctorum yetemberi. Saizvozvowo, musi waGumiguru 26, 1998, dombo rinorema 15 kg rakakunguruka kubva patemberi huru rikawira pedyo netemberi yeBimala mukati menzvimbo yetemberi huru. Musi waMbudzi 1, 2011, dombo rinorema anenge tani 1 rakawira pasi kubva pedyo neDadhinauti regedhi rekumadokero reJagannath Temple.

Zvakare mugore ra2015, musi waFebruary 2, dombo rairema 30-40 kg rakawira pasi kubva kuJalakrida Mandapa yakavakwa pedyo neBimala Temple kumadokero kweJagannath Temple. Tichitaura nezvenguva dzichangopfuura, musi waAugust 3, 2022, chidimbu chepurasita chairema anenge 1.5 kg chakawira pasi kubva padenga renzvimbo tsvene-tsvene yetemberi iyi yakavakwa muzana ramakore regumi nembiri. Musi waOctober 23, 2022, dombo rakawira kubva kukona yekumaodzanyemba-kumabvazuva kwetemberi pedyo neMajana Mandapa neCharcha Ghara, uye mukati mevhiki imwe chete, musi waOctober 28, 2022, dombo re2-3 kg rakawira pasi kubva padanho rechitatu pamusoro pechifananidzo cheSakhi pedyo nenzvimbo yakabatana yeNatamandapa neJagamohana. Kunyange mugore ra2024, musi waMarch 9, dombo rairema anenge makirogiramu mana rakawira pasi kubva padenga reBaraha Temple iri pamberi peBimala Temple munzvimbo yeJagannath Temple.

Nenzira iyi, chiporofita chakaitwa mazana emakore apfuura naMahapurush Achyutananda Das maererano nematombo anowira "tharaku thara" (kasingaperi) kubva kuJagannath Temple chakasimbiswawo kuva chokwadi mumakore mashoma apfuura.

Odia Sloka:

ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ସେହିଦିନଠାରୁ ସଖୀ ବିପଦ ପଡ଼ିବ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ପଥର ଖସିବ ଯୁଗ ଶେଷ ହେବାପାଇଁ,
ଜାଣି ମୁଁ ଗରୁଡ଼ ଏ ଶକୁନମାନ ଗୁପତେ ଦେଲି ଚେତେଇ।
ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ଏକ ପଥର ଖସିବ,
ତେଇଶି ବରଷେ କଳି ଦେଶ ଛାଡ଼ି ଯିବ।
ଚୁଳରୁ ପଥର ଯେବେ ଖସିବ ସୁତ,
ଖସିଲେ ଅଅଁଳା ବେଢ଼ାରୁ ହେବ ଏ କଳି ହତ।
ମୋର ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ଶ୍ରୀକ୍ଷେତ୍ର ରାଜନ ମୋର ନ ସେବି ପୟର।
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ତୋ ଦେଉଳ ଚୁଳେ,
ଖସିବ ପଥର ଖଣ୍ଡ ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ତଳେ।
ବତାସରେ ହେ। ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବକ୍ର ହେବ ବାରେ ହେ।
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳରୁ ମୋର ଖସିବ ପଥର,
ବସିବ ଯେ ଗୃଧ୍ର ପକ୍ଷୀ ଅରୁଣ ଖମ୍ବର।
ବତାସର ହେ। ବକ୍ର ହେବ ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ମୋର ହେ।
ତରକିବେ ତେରଜଣ ଜାଣିଥା ନିକର,
ବଡ଼ ଦେଉଳୁ ପଥର ଖସି ବାରମ୍ବାର।
ଗୃଧ୍ରପକ୍ଷୀ ମଥାପରେ ବସିଣ ଯେ ଯିବ,
ସେହିଦିନୁ ହତଶିରୀ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Baḍa deularu mora pathara khasiba,
sehidinaṭhāru sakhī bipada paḍiba.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru pathara khasiba yuga sheṣha hebāpāiṃ,
jāṇi mu Garuḍa e shakunamāna gupate deli chetei.
Aaṃlā beḍhāru eka pathara khasiba,
teishi baraṣhe kali desha chāḍi jiba.
Chularu pathara jebe khasiba suta,
khasile aaṃlā beḍhāru heba e kali hata.
Mora baḍa deularu khasiba pathara,
Shrīkṣhetra rājana mora na sebi payara.
Basiba je giddha pakṣhī to deula chule,
khasiba pathara khaṃḍa paḍiba je tale.
Batāsare he. Nīlachakra bakra heba bāre he.
Baḍa deularu mora khasiba pathara,
basiba je giddha pakṣhī Aruṇa khambara.
Batāsara he. Bakra heba Nīlachakra mora he.
Tarakibe terajaṇa jāṇithā nikara,
baḍa deulu pathara khasi bārambāra.
Giddhapakṣhī mathāpare basiṇa je jiba,
sehidinu hatashirī pṛithwī hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akakurukura zvakadzama zviratidzo zvakawanda zvakaipa zvekupera kweKali Yuga zvakawanikwa kubva kuJagannath Temple muPuri mumabhuku akawanda eMalika anosanganisira 'Rukmini Gita', 'Garuda Samvada', 'Hari Arjuna Chautisha', 'Kaliyuga Gita', 'Jai Birachita Chautisha', uye 'Chakada Madana'. Muchirevo ichi, anonyora kuti kana dombo rikawira kubva ku"Amla Bedha" yeJagannath Temple, vatsvene vanofanira kunzwisisa kuti kupera kweKali Yuga kwava pedyo chaizvo uye njodzi nenhamo dzakawanda dzichawira pasi munguva pfupi iri kutevera. Anoti kuwira kwedombo iri kuchava chiratidzo chakakosha uye chakavanzika chekupera kweKali Yuga, uye makore 23 mushure mekuwira kwaro, Kali Yuga ichapera. Anowedzerawo kuti panguva yakataurwa, shiri yakaipa ichagara pamusoro peJagannath Temple neAruna Stambha iri pamberi peSinghadwara yetemberi. Kwete izvi chete, Neelachakra inorema 2.2-ton yakagadzirwa neAshtadhatu pamusoro petemberi ichakombama nesimba guru remhepo.

Vaongorori vachashamisika kuziva kuti zviporofita zvose zvataurwa pamusoro zvakanyorwa mazana emakore apfuura zvakaitika zvino muJagannath Temple. Pakati pemvura zhinji, kutanga, musi waJune 14, 1990, dombo rairema 5-6 matani rakawira kubva ku"Amla Bedha" yetemberi. Ipapo shiri yakaipa yakawanikwawo yakagara paAruna Stambha.

Zvinokosha kuziva kuti hapana shiri yakambowanikwa ichibhururuka pamusoro peJagannath Puri temple, uye masayendisiti nevaongorori vakatadzawo kuongorora kuti sei izvi zvakaitika. Asi munguva ino yekupedzisira yeKali Yuga, zvichisimbisa zviporofita zveMalika kuva chokwadi, shiri dzakaipa dzakadai semakondo/magora dzakaonekwa dzakagara paPatitapabana Bana pamusoro petemberi kakawanda. Mumakore achangopfuura, zvakafanana zvakaitika musi waAugust 11, 2018, December 26, 2018, naJuly 23, 2020, apo shiri dzakaipa dzakaonekwa dzakagara pamureza wetemberi. Musi waDecember 7, 2021, shiri dzakadai dzisingaverengeki dzakaonekwa dzichitenderera dziri boka pamusoro peJagannath Temple. Chiitiko chakafanana chakaonekwa zvakare musi waAugust 17, 2022. Kutevedzana uku hakuna kumira pano; mumwedzi yaMay, June, naSeptember mugore ra2023, vagari vePuri vakaonazve chiitiko ichi chisingafadzi apo shiri dzakafanana dzakaonekwa dzakagara pamureza wakaiswa pamusoro petemberi.

Munguva pfupi yapfuura mugore ra2025 zvakare, shiri yakaonekwa pamusoro peAruna Stambha musi waSeptember 4, ukuwo makondo akaonekwa akagara pamusoro petemberi musi waSeptember 2, September 16, naOctober 14. Musi waDecember 9, shiri dzisingaverengeki dzakawanikwa dzichitenderera Shrimandir.

Pamusoro pezvose izvi, dutu guru remhepo rine simba kwazvo, 'Fani', rakarova gungwa rePuri musi waMay 3, 2019, rakakombamisa Neelachakra pamusoro petemberi, zvichizadzisa chimwe chiporofita chakanyorwa mazana emakore apfuura mukutevedzana uku.

Zvinofanira kucherechedzwa kuti zviporofita izvi zvakanyorwa muzana ramakore rechi16 hazvisi zviratidzo chete zvekupera kweKali Yuga, asiwo zviratidzo zvakapihwa nePanchasakhas zvekusvika kweKalki Avatar, nekuti Kalki Avatar achasvika chete pakupera kweKali Yuga, sezvakataurwa nemagwaro matsvene ose anosanganisira Bhavishya Malika.





Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁ ଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।
ହର ଉଦ୍ୟାନରେ ମାୟା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ମ୍ଳେଚ୍ଛକୁ ଗ୍ରାସୁଥିବେ,
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ବାନା ଝଡ଼ିବ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଲୋଡ଼ିଯିବେ।
ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ପରୁ ପତାକା ଛିଡ଼ିଣ ପଡ଼ିବ ସମୁଦ୍ର କୂଳେ,
କଳକୀ ରୂପକୁ ଚିନ୍ତା ପୁଣ ହେବ ଭାବିବେ କ୍ଷୀରାଧି କୂଳେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Nīlachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṃgā adhara,
Jājanagre janma hoibe Biṣhṇu Sharmā gṛihara.
Hara udyānare māyā prakāshiba mlechchaku grāsuthibe,
Nīlachakra bānā jhaḍiba nishchaya bhakataṃku loḍijibe.
Nīlachakra paru patākā chiḍiṇa paḍiba samudra kūle,
Kalki rūpaku chiṃtā puṇa heba bhābibe kṣhīrādhi kūle.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akataura chimwe chiratidzo chekupera kweKali Yuga nekuberekwa kwaLord Kalki mumabhuku ake eMalika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' ne'Kalikalpa Gita'. Akanyora kuti nguva ichasvika apo Patitapabana Bana yakasungirirwa pamusoro peNeelachakra yeShrimandir ichabhururuka kubva panzvimbo yayo. Mahapurush akanyorawo kuti mureza uchabhururuka wodonhera mugungwa.

Chiporofita ichi chakaitwa naMahapurush Achyutananda Das chakasimbiswawo kuva chokwadi. Zuva rimwe chete risati rasvika dutu guru remhepo rine simba kwazvo 'Fani' rakarova gungwa rePuri, musi waMay 2, 2019, nekuda kwemhepo ine simba, Patitapabana Bana yakabhururuka ikawira mugungwa. Chiitiko chakafanana chaizvo chakaitika zvakare musi waMay 18, 2020, mazuva maviri Cyclone 'Amphan' isati yarova gungwa reOdisha.



Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡ଼ି ଆମ୍ଭେ ଯିବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ,
ଲାଗିବ ରତ୍ନଚାନ୍ଦୁଆ ଅଗ୍ନି ସେତେବେଳେ।
ନିଶାକାଳେ ହେ। ନୀଳାଚଳୁ ଚୋରିଯିବ ହେଲେ ହେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Nīlāchala chāḍi āmbhe jibu jetebele,
lāgiba ratnachāṃduā agni setebele.
Nishākāle he. Nīlāchalu chorijiba hele he.

Zvinoreva:
Achitaura mashoko aIshe, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika 'Jai Birachita Chautisha' kuti kana Lord Jagannath achisiya Nilachala, kureva Shrimandir, kuti azoberekwa mumuviri wemunhu, panguva iyoyo chifukidziro chine matombo anokosha chakaiswa pamusoro pevatatu vatsvene muJagannath Temple chichabata moto.

Zvinokosha kuziva kuti chiitiko cheRatnachandua kubata moto chakaitika mugore ra2004-05, uye nekudaro chiporofita ichi cheMahapurush chakasimbiswawo kuva chokwadi.

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍ପବଟ ଡ଼ାଳ ଛେଦନ ଭୋଇବ ଦେଖିବୁ ତୁ ନୟନରେ,
ଶିବକଳ୍ପ ବାଣୀ ଅନ୍ୟଥା ନୋହିବ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ତୁରେ।
ଏକ ଦକ୍ଷିଣରେ ନବ ଯେ ଥିବ,
କଳ୍ପବଟ ମୋର ଛେଦନ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kalpabaṭa ḍāla chedana hoiba dekhibu tu nayanare,
Shibakalpa bāṇī anyathā nohiba shuṇa Rāmadāsa ture.
Eka dakṣhiṇare naba je thiba,
kalpabaṭa mora chedana hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika 'Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta' makore 600 apfuura kuti nguva ichasvika apo bazi reKalpavata riri munzvimbo yeJagannath Temple richatyoka. Akatonyora mune rimwe remabhuku ake kuti kana paine 'pfumbamwe' padivi pe'imwe', kureva nhamba 19, ipapo chete ndipo pachingaitika chiitiko ichi.

Chaizvoizvo maererano nemashoko eMahapurush, chiporofita ichi chakasimbiswa kuva chokwadi mugore ra2019 apo dutu guru remhepo rine simba kwazvo 'Fani', rakarova gungwa rePuri musi waMay 3, rakakuvadza Kalpavata guru, rekare-kare riri munzvimbo yetemberi.

Odia Sloka:

ରକ୍ତଛିଟା ପଡ଼ିବ ଯେ ରତ୍ନସିଂହାସନେ,
ଏହି ଘଟଣା ଘଟିବ ମନେ ରଖ ଦିନେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Raktachīṃṭā paḍiba je ratnasiṃhāsane,
ehi ghaṭaṇā ghaṭiba mane rakha dine.

Zvinoreva:
Pakati pezviratidzo zvakawanda zvakataurwa naMahapurush kuti zvichawanikwa kubva kuJagannath Temple muPuri pakupera kweKali Yuga, chimwe chaiva kuona madonhwe eropa paRatnasinghasana yezvifananidzo zvitsvene.

Chiporofita ichi chakanyorwa naMahapurush chakasimbiswawo kuva chokwadi. Mumakore mashoma apfuura, madonhwe eropa akawira munzvimbo dzakawanda mutemberi, kusanganisira Ratnasinghasana, mushure mezvo, maererano netsika yetemberi, Chaturdha Murti vakanatswa kuburikidza nemhemberero yeMahasnana (yekugeza kukuru). Semuenzaniso, musi waMarch 6, 2021, ropa rakaonekwa pedyo ne'Bhitar Katha' iri kure kure nenzvimbo tsvene-tsvene yetemberi. Musi waApril 18, 2021, nekuda kwekurwisana pakati pemapurisa maviri akatumirwa kuchengetedza temberi, madonhwe eropa akawira munzvimbo yetemberi. Musi waMarch 7, 2022, panguva ye'Abakasha Niti' (mhemberero), madonhwe eropa akawanikwa pedyo ne'Bhitar Katha' mukati meJagamohana yetemberi (horo iri pamberi penzvimbo tsvene-tsvene). Ipapo musi waApril 11, 2022, madonhwe eropa akaonekwa pedyo neDhukudi Dwara yetemberi. Musi waNovember 3, 2022, mushure memhemberero dze'Mangala Alati', 'Mailama', ne'Tadapa Lagi', madonhwe eropa akaonekwa pedyo ne'Bhitar Katha'. Chiitiko chakadaro chakaonekwa zvakare muvhiki rechitatu raJanuary mugore ra2023. Mushure mekupera kwe'Abakasha' na'Besa Lagi' dzaLord Jagannath, madonhwe eropa akaonekwa muShrimandir, nekuda kweizvi chipiriso (Bhoga) kuna Gopalaballabha hachina kukwanisa kupedzwa. Ipapo musi waJune 4, 2023, mushure mekunge kugeza kutsvene (Devasnana) kwapera, madonhwe eropa akaonekwa paSnana Mandapa. Kunyange munguva pfupi yapfuura, musi waOctober 13, 2023, nekuda kwekurwisana kune chisimba pakati pevaranda vaviri pamusoro pekugoverwa kwePrasad pakarepo mushure me'Madhyahna Dhupa' muShrimandir, madonhwe eropa akawira munzvimbo tsvene-tsvene yetemberi. Mugore ra2024 zvakare, mangwanani-ngwanani aApril 23, mushure mekunge Singhadwara yavhurwa, mukadzi kubva kumhuri yevaranda akanga aisa ruoko rwake pamusuwo wetemberi apo munwe wake wakapwanyika mairi. Nekuda kweizvi, ropa rakabuda kubva mumunwe wake pedyo neDhukudi Dwara pedyo neBedha yemukati. Mumamiriro ezvinhu akadaro, Mahasnana yaIshe Mukuru yakaitwa kunyange Mangala Alati isati yaitwa. Saizvozvowo, zviitiko zveropa munzvimbo yetemberi zvakaenderera mberi mugore ra2025 zvakare.

7.2 Kuwira kwechifananidzo chaLord Balabhadra panguva yeRath Yatra 2024.

Panguva yeRath Yatra yemazuva 11 muShri Jagannath Puri, mhemberero dze'Adapa Mandapa Bije' dzakatanga na7 PM manheru aJuly 9, 2024. Panguva iyi, vachitakura chifananidzo chaLord Balabhadra kubva mungoro kuenda kuAdapa Mandapa yeGundicha Temple, Akawira akatarisa pasi paCharamala, nekuda kweizvi varanda 12 vakakuvarazve. Chiitiko chisingafadzi chakadaro chakaitika kekutanga munhoroondo. Kuwira kwaLord Balabhadra nenzira iyi chiratidzo chechokwadi chenjodzi dziri kuuya, kutaura kwayo kunowanikwa sezvizvi mubhuku re'Niladri' rine chekuita nemitemo nemirau yeShrikhetra, sezvakarairwa naLord Brahma naLord Vishnu.

Odia Sloka:

ମୁରବୈରିଣ ଏତସ୍ୟ ଜଗଦୀଶସ୍ୟ ଭୂପତେହଃ।
ଭଦ୍ରାୟାଶ୍ଚ ବଳସ୍ୟାପି ପ୍ରମାଦାତ୍ ପତନଂ ଯଦି।।
ମହାଭୟଂ ନୃପସ୍ୟାପି ରାଜ୍ୟସ୍ୟାପି ଭବେଦ୍ଧ୍ରୁବମ୍।
ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷଂ ମରଣଂ ଚୈବ ଜାୟତେ ଚ ପୁନଃ ପୁନଃ।।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Murabairiṇa etasya jagadīshasya bhūpatehaḥ.
Bhadrāyāshcha balasyāpi pramādāt patanaṃ yadi.
Mahābhayaṃ nṛipasyāpi rājyasyāpi bhabeddhrubam.
Durbhikṣhaṃ maraṇaṃ chaiba jāyate cha punaḥ punaḥ.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, "O Mambo, kana netsaona Jagannath, Balabhadra, kana Subhadra vakawira pasi, ipapo njodzi huru ichawira mambo nenyika. Nzara ichaitika, uye zvirwere zvinotapukira zvicharova kakawanda."

Moto munzvimbo tsvene-tsvene yeMahakal Temple, Ujjain mugore ra2024

Mangwanani eMuvhuro, March 25, 2024, panguva yemutambo weHoli, moto wakaputika munzvimbo tsvene-tsvene yeMahakaleshwar Temple muUjjain nekuda kwekukanda 'gulal' (hupfu hwemavara) panguva yeBhasma Aarti. Vanhu vanenge 14, kusanganisira mupristi mukuru nevaranda vamwe, vakatsva muchiitiko ichi.

7.3 Zviratidzo zvikuru zvakaipa zvakawanikwa kubva kuJagannath Temple mugore ra2025

Zviratidzo zvakaipa zvakawanikwa kubva kuJagannath Temple zvakaenderera mberi mugore ra2025 zvakare. Kuburikidza nezviratidzo zvakawanda, Lord Jagannath akapa vatsvene Vake chiratidzo chekutanga chenjodzi dziri kuuya, zvimwe zvezviratidzo zvikuru zvatiri kupa pamberi penyu.

(a) Kutyoka kweAkshaya Danda musi waFebruary 14

Sezvinoitika zuva rega rega, masikati aFebruary 14, 2025 (Chishanu), apo muranda weChunara akasvika paNeelachakra pamusoro petemberi kuti achinje Patitapabana Bana kana mureza mukuru, akawana Akshaya Danda (danda remushenjere rinobata Patitapabana Bana) yakatyoka. Mushure meizvi, varanda vakabata mureza kuNeelachakra pachayo neimwe nzira.

Kutyoka kweAkshaya Danda nenzira iyi kwakakuvadza manzwiro evatsvene vasingaverengeki, uye vakataura kusuwa kukuru pamusoro pazvo. Pamwe chete neizvi, vatsvene vakawanda vakachidaidza kuti chiratidzo chenjodzi dziri kuuya munguva pfupi iri kutevera.

(b) Madonhwe eropa akawanikwa mutemberi musi waFebruary 16; Mahasnana yevamwari yakaitwa

Musi waFebruary 16 (Svondo), mushure mekupedzwa kwemhemberero dzemangwanani dzakadai se 'Dwara Phita Niti' ne 'Mangala Alati', pakangotanga Darshan yaIshe Jagannath, nguva pfupi yapfuura, vashandi vakawana mavara eropa pedyo nenzvimbo ye 'Bhitar Katha'. Pakarepo mushure mechiitiko ichi, gadziriro dzakaitwa dze 'Bada Mahasnana Niti' yevamwari, uye Darshan yakamiswawo kwechinguva.

(c) Gunguo richibhururuka nePatitapabana Bana musi waApril 13

Musi waApril 13, 2025, vatsvene vaIshe Jagannath vari paPuri Shrimandir vakaona chiratidzo chisati chamboonekwa. Nguva dzinenge 5 PM, gunguo rakaonekwa richitenderera tembere richitakura Patitapabana Bana mumhuno yaro. Zvinoenderana nemishumo, gunguo rakatenderera tembere kanenge ka4-5 ndokubva rabhururuka richienda kugungwa pamusoro pesuwo rekumadokero.

Vhidhiyo yechiitiko ichi yakapararira pasocial media. Vatsvene vazhinji vakaratidza kusuwa kwavo nekunetsekana pamusoro peizvi. Vatsvene vazhinji vakazvibatanidza nenjodzi dziri kuuya, uye vamwe vakazvidaidza kuti chiratidzo chisati chamboonekwa chakaipa chakapihwa naIshe Jagannath.

(d) Mavara eropa akawanikwa mutembere musi waApril 21 uye Mahasnana yevamwari yakaitwa

Musi waApril 21 (Muvhuro) zvakare, mavara eropa akaonekwa pedyo neBhitar Katha muShrimandir, mushure mezvo Mahasnana yezvifananidzo zvaIshe yakaitwa zvakanaka zvinoenderana netsika. Zvichakadaro, Darshan yaIshe Mukuru yevatsvene yakakanganiswa kweanenge awa 1. Zvinoenderana nemishumo, mavara eropa akawanikwa mukati metembere nekuda kwezvidimbu zvegirazi zvakapinza tsoka yemupurisa.

(e) Gunguo rakagara pamusoro pePatitapabana Bana musi waSeptember 2

Gunguo rakagara paPatitapabana Bana pamusoro peShrimandir rakaonekwa kakawanda mumakore mashoma apfuura. Nguva dzose chiitiko chakadaro chinoonekwa, chinoratidzwa panenge dzose nhepfenyuro dzemuno muOdisha. Musi waSeptember 2, chinhu chimwe chete ichi chakaitika zvakare apo gunguo rakatanga kuonekwa richibhururuka pedyo neNeelachakra uye mushure mechinguva rakawanikwa rakagara paPatitapabana Bana rakabatanidzwa pamusoro peNeelachakra.

(f) Gunguo rakagara paAruna Stambha zvakare musi waSeptember 4

Kwete chete Tembere yeJagannath, Bhavishya Malika inotsanangurawo kugara kwegunguo kana chero imwe shiri yakaipa paAruna Stambha iri pamberi peSinghadwara yetembere sechiratidzo chakaipa. Musi waSeptember 4, kureva, mazuva maviri chete mushure mekunge gunguo raonekwa pamusoro peShrimandir musi waSeptember 2, gunguo rakaonekwawo rakagara paAruna Stambha. Vanhu vakaonawo izvi sechiratidzo chakaipa. Kuburikidza nechiitiko ichi, vazhinji vakatsanangura chiporofita cheMalika chakanyorwa makore 600 apfuura sechiitika.

(g) Gunguo rakagara pamusoro petembere zvakare musi waSeptember 16

Mukati memazuva 15 chete aSeptember 2, mangwanani-ngwanani aSeptember 16, gunguo rakaonekwa zvakare rakagara pamusoro pePatitapabana Bana yetembere.

(h) Gunguo rakawanikwa rakagara paPatitapabana Bana yeShrimandir zvakare musi waOctober 14

Gunguo rakawanikwa rakagara pamusoro petembere kaviri mumwedzi waSeptember. Kutevedzana hakuna kumira pano; vagari vePuri vakaona kudzokororwa kwechiitiko ichi zvakare musi waOctober 14. Kugara kwakadzokororwa kwegunguo pamusoro petembere kwakatsanangurwa nevatsvene vazhinji sechiratidzo chenjodzi dziri kuuya.

(i) Magunguo asingaverengeki achitenderera pamusoro peShrimandir musi waDecember 9

Kungofanana musi waDecember 7, 2021, naAugust 17, 2022, chiratidzo chakaipa kwazvo chakaonekwa musi waDecember 9, 2025, zvakare, apo magunguo asingaverengeki akawanikwa achitenderera pamusoro peTembere yeJagannath. Chiitiko ichi chakakwezva pfungwa kwete chete dzevatsvene muPuri kana Odisha, asiwo dzevaya vanogara kunze kweOdisha. Vatsvene vazhinji vakadaidza chiitiko ichi chiratidzo chechiitiko chikuru chakaipa chiri kuuya munguva pfupi iri kutevera.

7.4 Zviratidzo zvakaipa zvakagamuchirwa kubva kune dzimwe dzimba dzemamongi dzakakosha netembere mugore ra2025

Moto waputika munzvimbo yeTembere yeMahakaleshwar muUjjain

Musi waMay 5, 2025, na12 PM, moto wakaputika mukamuri rekutonga rebhodhi rekudzora kusvibiswa riri pedyo neShankh Dwara munzvimbo yetembere inozivikanwa pasi rose yeBaba Mahakaleshwar muUjjain. Mushure mechiitiko ichi, kutya kwakapararira munzvimbo yetembere. Mushure mekunge moto waputika, kupinda kwevatsvene mutembere kwakamiswa kwechinguva.

Moto waputika munzvimbo yePeetha yaMahapurush Achyutananda Das

Pazuva reDevasnana Purnima, June 11, 2025, moto wakaputika munzvimbo yeNemal Padmabana Peetha yaMahapurush Achyutananda Das iri muNischintakoili, Cuttack wakaonekwawo sechiratidzo chakaipa kwazvo nevatsvene veMalika. Mamiriro ekutya akapararira pakati peboka revatsvene zviuru zvakawanda apo chigadziko chemwenje chakachengetwa munzvimbo chakangobata moto, uye mukati memaminitsi, marimi emoto anopfuta akatanga kubuda.

Muti weNemal banyan wagurwa nehurumende

Pakutanga kwaAugust, ruzhinji rwemiti mikuru yemuti webanyan wekare-kare pamberi petembere iri paSadhana Peetha yemunyori mukuru wemabhuku eMalika, mutsvene mukuru Shri Achyutananda Das, yakagurwa. Zvinonzi, danho rakadaro rakatorwa nehurumende kuti riitwe basa rekuvandudza reAchyutananda Das Peetha. Pakave nekupikiswa kwakapararira kune izvi nevagari vemo nevapristi, uye pakupikisa kwavo, matavi mashoma chete emuti webanyan akasara asina kugurwa. Zvinofanira kucherechedzwa kuti Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mabhuku eMalika achigara pasi pe 'Nemal Banyan Tree' ari mumamiriro ekufungisisa. Kugurwa kwemuti weNemal banyan kwakaonekwa sechiratidzo chakaipa kwazvo nevatsvene veMalika.

Jira regoridhe rakaiswa pamusoro peTembere yeMahakaleshwar rawa

Musi waJune 1, 2025, chiratidzo chakaipa chakaonekwa zvakare kubva kuTembere yeMahakaleshwar inozivikanwa pasi rose iri muUjjain. Chaizvoizvo, nekuda kwemhepo ine simba, jira regoridhe rakaiswa pamusoro peTembere yeMahakaleshwar rakangoerekana rawa. Mupristi wetembere akatsanangura chiitiko ichi sechinhu chinokonzera kunetseka kubva pakuona kwechitendero.

Chifukidzo chaIshe Shiva chakaitwa paShivalinga muTembere yeMahakaleshwar chaputika chikawa

Chiitiko chakasiyana chakaitika musi waAugust 18 paTembere yeMahakal iri muUjjain. Pano, sezuva rega rega na8:00 PM panguva yekushongedza (Shringara) yeShivalinga, chifukidzo chaIshe Shiva chakaitwa neBhang (mushonga wecannabis). Huwandu hukuru hweBhang hwakaiswa panguva iyi. Pakarepo mushure meizvi, Aarti yakanga yava kuda kutanga apo Bhang Shringara yakaputika ikawa. Mhino, miromo, uye ziso rimwe zvakaputika kubva pachifukidzo chaIshe Shiva zvikawa.

Chiratidzo chisina kujairika chaonekwa muTembere yeMaa Samaleswari

Kubva kutembere inozivikanwa yeMaa Samaleswari iri muLakhanpur, Jharsuguda, Odisha, husiku hweChina, August 21, 2025, vatsvene vakagamuchira chiratidzo cheMwarikadzi achisiya chifananidzo Chake achibuda. Apo mupristi akavhura mikova yetembere mangwanani-ngwanani e 'Mangala Alati', tsoka dzaienda kunze, dzakapendwa neAlta (pendi tsvuku), dzeMwarikadzi dzakaonekwa, uye pamwe chete neizvi, bakatwa Rake (Khanda) netrident (Trishul), izvo zvakanga zvakabatanidzwa pachifananidzo cheMwarikadzi, zvakawanikwa zvawa parutivi.

Zvinoenderana ne 'Bhavishya Malika', muchikamu chekupedzisira cheKali Yuga, pamberi pekusimbiswa kweDharma, Vamwarikadzi vose vachasiya nzvimbo dzavo vobuda kuzoita basa rekunyatsoparadza vakaipa nevatadzi. Chiitiko ichi chiratidzo chekuti nguva iyoyo yasvika uye chiporofita ichocho chiri kuitika.

Moto waputika muzvifananidzo zvitsvene muTemberi yeBrahmagiri Jagannath

Musi waNyamavhuvhu 28, 2025, zvifananidzo zvaIshe Jagannath, mukoma Balabhadra, naamai Subhadra zvakabatira moto muTemberi yeBrahmagiri Jagannath iri mumusha weKandagoda pasi peDunhu rePuri muOdisha. Chaizvoizvo, mushure mekupedza kunamata kwemanheru, vapristi vakaenda kudzimba dzavo. Na8:30 PM, nekuda kweutsi hwakabuda mutemberi, apo gonhi retemberi rakaputswa, zvakaonekwa kuti zvifananidzo zvemukati zvakanga zvabatira moto. Mushure mekukurukura neMuktimandapa, Shaligrama yakapihwa rukudzo kubva pazuva iroro zvichienda mberi. Zvichitevera kuunganidzwa kweDaru (huni dzitsvene) muNabakalebara iri kuuya, zvifananidzo zvitsva zvakaiswa.

Chiratidzo chekuparadzwa kuri kuuya chagamuchirwa kubva kuTemberi yeLingaraj, danda remureza retemberi raputika

Musi weMuvhuro, Gunyana 15, 2025, nekuda kwehasha dzemvura zhinji nemhepo, danda remureza reTemberi yeLingaraj yakavakwa muzana ramakore rechi11 iri muBhubaneswar raputika. Mangwanani eChipiri, mushure mekunge raonekwa nevashumiri, mureza wakasimudzwa padanda idzva.

Mureza uri pamusoro peTemberi yeLingaraj iri muBhubaneswar wabvaruka nekuda kwemvura zhinji nedutu. Nzvimbo tsvene-tsvene yakakanganiswa nemheni; rima rakafukidza temberi.

Pakati pemvura zhinji nedutu rakasimba musi weChitatu, Gumiguru 8, 2025, Temberi yeLingaraj iri muguta guru reOdisha, Bhubaneswar, yakawirwa nemheni. Izvi zvakare chaive chiratidzo chechiitiko chisingafadzi. Mureza wetemberi wakabvaruka nekuda kwedutu, apo makamera eCCTV etemberi nemagetsi zvakakanganiswa nemheni. Zvakashumwa kuti vamwe vatsvene nevashumiri vaive mutemberi vakawanawo kuvhunduka kwemagetsi.

(j) Kekutanga munhoroondo yayo yemakore 500, Bal Bhog haina kupihwa muTemberi yeBanke Bihari muVrindavan

Thakur Banke Bihari, uyo akazviratidza makore angasvika mazana mashanu apfuura, anopihwa Bhog (chipo chekudya) panguva dzina dzezuva. Pamberi pekuisa chigaro cheDarshan yemangwanani, Bal Bhog inopihwa. Pamberi petemberi yavhara masikati, Raj Bhog inopihwa, uye kana magonhi avhurwa manheru, Utthapan Bhog inopihwa. Kana magonhi avhara, Shayan Bhog inopihwa. Kondirakiti yekugadzira Bhog inopihwa kune mugadziri wemakeke (Halwai). Mugadziri wemakeke anogadzira Bhog uye anoipa kuvashumiri, uye vanopa Bhog.

Asi musi waZvita 15 na8:45 AM, magonhi etemberi akavhurwa, asi mugadziri wemakeke haana kusvika neBal Bhog. Paakabatwa, zvakaonekwa kuti mugadziri wemakeke haana kusvika nekuda kwekusabhadharwa kwemari yemwedzi. Kekutanga munhoroondo yayo yemakore 500, chiitiko chakadai chakaitika apo Bal Bhog yakatadza kupihwa kuna Banke Bihari Ji pazuva iroro.

7.5 Kurongeka kweRath Yatra panguva isina kufanira

Sezvatinoziva tese, Rath Yatra yagara ichirongwa gore rega rega paDwitiya Tithi yeShukla Paksha yemwedzi weAshadha. Tsika iyi yagara ichitevedzwa mune dzimwe temberi dzeJagannath muIndia nekune dzimwe nyika, kusanganisira Temberi yeJagannath muPuri. Asi mumakore mashoma apfuura, panga paine nguva dzakawanda apo ISKCON yakaonekwa ichityora tsika iyi.

Panyaya iyi, mushure mekunge zvichemo zvasimudzwa neMutungamiriri Mukuru weShree Jagannath Temple Administration uye Sachigaro weShree Jagannath Temple Managing Committee, Gajapati Maharaj Divyasingha Deva, uye chichemo chakanyorwa chakaitwa kumahofisi makuru eISKCON ari muMayapur, kumwe kuvandudzwa kwakaitika zvechokwadi, asi ISKCON ichiri kuonekwa ichironga Rath Yatras dzisina nguva muIndia uye kunyanya kune dzimwe nyika.

Muchitevedzano ichi, ISKCON ichangobva kuronga Rath Yatra muTaiwan musi waMbudzi 23, 2025, iyo yakashorwa nenharaunda yeOdia pasi rose uye vatsvene vasingaverengeki vaJagannath veOdisha. Vatsvene vakaiona kwete kungova kutyora kwemitemo yechinyakare asiwo vakaiona sekusaremekedza murairo waIshe Jagannath Mwenewake.


7.6 Kukanganisa mukarenda yeChirungu ya2026 yakadhindwa neShrimandir Administration

Munguva pfupi yapfuura, kukanganisa kukuru kwabuda pachena mukarenda yeChirungu ya2026 yakapihwa neJagannath Temple Administration. Chaizvoizvo, mufananidzo weRatnabedi wakadhindwa mukarenda umo kutevedzana kweUtatu kusina kururama. Kubva pakuona kwemuoni, mumufananidzo wekarenda, Ishe Jagannath akaiswa kuruboshwe-rurefu, ipapo Mwarikadzi Subhadra, uye ipapo mukoma Balabhadra, nepo mukutevedzana kwakarurama, Ishe Balabhadra anofanira kunge ari kuruboshwe-rurefu, ipapo Mwarikadzi Subhadra, uye ipapo Shri Jagannath. Kureva kuti, kukanganisa ndekwekuti mukarenda, mufananidzo wemukoma Balabhadra wakadhindwa panzvimbo yaIshe Jagannath, uye mufananidzo waIshe Jagannath uri panzvimbo yaIshe Balabhadra. Pamusoro peizvi, Chakraraj Sudarshan haana kupihwa nzvimbo mumufananidzo weRatnasinghasana mukarenda. Kwete izvi chete, mufananidzo weRath Yatra wakadhindwawo mukarenda, unoratidza ngoro yaAmai Subhadra kutanga, ichiteverwa nengoro yaIshe Mukuru Jagannath, uye pakupedzisira ngoro yaIshe Balabhadra.

Nekuda kwekukanganisa uku, pane kusagutsikana nehasha mupfungwa dzevatsvene. Kunewo hasha pakati pevashumiri veTemberi yeJagannath pamusoro pechiitiko ichi. Maererano nevamwe vatsvene, ichi chiratidzo chakapihwa naIshe Jagannath Mwenewake chezvinhamo zviri kuuya uye shanduko muhurongwa hwenyika mugore idzva.

8 Kuzvarwa kwaIshe Kalki – Rini uye Kupiko?

Maererano neBhavishya Malika, Ishe Kalki akatoberekwa. Kwete Ishe Kalki chete, asiwo vese varwi vane ushingi venguva yeMahabharata vakaita saYudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babruvahana, Ekalavya, Ghatotkacha, Duryodhana, Shakuni, nezvimwe, vese vakazvarwa patsva panyika ino. Maererano neMalika, panguva ino vese varwi ava vacharwa pamwe chete parutivi rweDharma vachipokana neAdharma.

Zvisinei, hatizokurukuri nezvevamwe varwi pano uye tichaganhurira mashoko edu kuna Parahbrahma Narayana Mahavishnu Ishe Kalki Ram chete.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳଚକ୍ର ଧ୍ବଜା ଖସିବ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର,
ଯୋଜନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Nīḷachakra dhwajā khasiba prabhu raṅgā adhara,
yojanagre janama hoibe Biṣṇusharmā gṛuhara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana Patitapabana Bana (mureza) weTemberi yePuri Jagannath ukabhururuka kubva panzvimbo yawo woenda kumwe, panguva imwe cheteyo, Mahaprabhu Kalki achazvarwa muJajanagar kana Jajpur yeOdisha mumba meBrahmin anorumbidza Ishe Vishnu.

Ndinofanira kujekesera vaverengi kuti chiitiko ichi chakaonekwa kakawanda kubva kuTemberi yeJagannath, semuenzaniso mugore ra1990-91, mugore ra2005, ipapo panguva yeDutu Fani neDutu Amphan mumakore a2019 na2020 zvichiteerana, nezvimwe.

Mahapurush akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika kuti kuzvarwa kwaMahaprabhu Kalki kuchaitika chete mushure mechiitiko ichi. Sezvo chiitiko ichi chaitika, zvinogona kutaurwa nechokwadi kuti Ishe Kalki akazvarwa. Asi sezvo chiitiko ichi chaitika mumakore akasiyana-siyana, hazviite sekunge zvinogoneka kusvika pagore chairo rekuzvarwa kwaMahaprabhu kubva pachiitiko ichi chete.

Kuti tizive kuti Mahaprabhu akazvarwa mugore ripi, tichafanira kutora rubatsiro rwezvimwe ndima zveMalika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାଘ ସପ୍ତମୀରେ ବିକାଶ ହୋଇବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ନୃପବର,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରାଜତ୍ୱ କରିବେ ଏହି ଏକାମ୍ର ନଗର।
କେଉଁ ଦିନ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ କହେ ରାମଦାସ ଯହିଁ,
ବିଶାଖାପାଟଣା ନୃପ ନାଶ ହେଲେ ମୁହିଁ ବିକାଶ ହୁଅଇ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Māgha saptamīre bikāsha hoiba Dibyasiṃha nṛpabara,
Ananta Keshari rājatwa karibe ehi Ekāmra nagara.
Keuñ dina tumbhe prakāsha hoiba kahe Rāmadāsa yahiñ,
Viśākhāpāṭaṇā nṛpa nāśa hele muhiñ bikāsha huai.

Zvinoreva:
Kuburikidza nendima dziri pamusoro, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anoudza mudzidzi wake Ramadas kuti kana mambo anonzi Shri Dibyasingha Deb achitonga muPuri, panguva iyoyo Ananta Keshari, kureva, Mahaprabhu Kalki, achange achitonga muEkamra Nagar kana Bhubaneswar. Richava zuva rechinomwe (Saptami Tithi) remwedzi weMagha. Mahapurush anowedzera kutaura kuti panguva iyoyo, ini ndichagarawo naIshe ndokukura. Izvi zvichaitika kana Mambo weVisakhapatnam (kureva, Chief Minister weAndhra Pradesh) afa.

Kana tikanyatsoongorora, tichaona kuti kuburikidza nechiratidzo, Mahapurush ari kutaura nezverufu rweChief Minister weAndhra Pradesh, Y.S. Rajasekhara Reddy, rwakaitika mutsaona yeherikoputa mugore ra2009. Kubva mundima iri pamusoro, Mahapurush haasi kutaura gore rekuzvarwa kwaIshe, asi ari kutaura zvechokwadi kuti kutenderedza gore ra2009, mabasa etsvene aKalki (leelas) achaenderera mberi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mu‘Jaiphula Malika’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଶ୍ରୀ କୃଷ୍ଣ ଚୈତନ୍ୟ,
ଜନ୍ମିଛନ୍ତି ଆସି ଏହି ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟେଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସତଚାଳିଶକୁ ତୁହି ଘେନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Sehi Shrī Kṛuṣṇa Chaitanya,
janmichhanti āsi ehi martyeṇa lo jāiphula,
satachāḷishaku tuhi ghena.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe mumwe chete akauya kare saShri Krishna naShri Chaitanya, achauya kunyika yevanofa muchimiro chaKalki panhamba 47.

Zvinonyatsozivikanwa kuti nhamba iyi 47 ndeyaMambo wePuri, Shri Dibyasingha Deb, iyo inowira pakati pemakore a2005 na2008.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Kalpatika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ନୀଳାଦ୍ରି ଛାଡି ପ୍ରଭୁ ପୀତବାସ,
ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟରେ ଜନ୍ମ ଲଭିବେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ।
ଗୁଣରେ ଭେଦି ଭେଦ ଅଙ୍କେ ଜାଣ,
ଦେଲେ ଯେତେ ସାଲ ହେଲା ପ୍ରମାଣ।
ଏହି ସାଲରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମିବେ,
ଅଙ୍କ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଯେ କହିଲୁ ଏବେ।
ବିଂଶ ରେବ ଅଙ୍କ ଯେବଣ ଦିନ,
ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଉଦେ ଗଗନ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ଲକ୍ଷଣ ଦେଖିବୁ ଯେବେ,
ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇଥିବେ ତେବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Nīḷādri chhāḍi prabhu pītabāsa,
martyare janma labhibe abashya.
guṇare bhedi bheda aṅke jāṇa,
dele jete sāla helā pramāṇa.
ehi sālare prabhu janamibe,
aṅka pramāṇa je kahilu ebe.
biṃsha reba aṅka jebaṇa dina,
indu pare bindu ude gagana.
emanta lakṣaṇa dekhibu jebe,
prabhu janma hoithibe tebe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Mahaprabhu achatora zvechokwadi kuzvarwa panyika mushure mekusiya nzvimbo yake yeJagannath. Achitaura gore rekuzvarwa kwaMahaprabhu, Mahapurush anoti kana ukawedzera makumi maviri (20) kunhamba yeRevati Nakshatra (27), ipapo nhamba inobuda inofanira kunzwisiswa segore rekuzvarwa kwaIshe. Kureva kuti, 20+27 = 47.

Zvinonyatsozivikanwa kuti nhamba iyi igore rekutonga (Anka) raMambo wePuri, Dibyasingha Deb, iyo inonongedza kune rimwe remakore pakati pa2005 na2008.

Mahapurush akanyorazve mune imwe yemagwaro ake eMalika –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଥାମାନ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଘଟିବ ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟ ବାନା ଧରି ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଧରିଣ କଳକୀ ବେଶ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ehi kathāmāna nishchaya ghaṭiba Dibyasiṃha pañchachāḷisha,
shūnya bānā dhari āsibe Shrī Hari dhariṇa Kaḷakī besha.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana riri gore rechi45 raMaharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb wePuri, ipapo Ishe Shri Hari achatora kuzvarwa kwaKalki uye achaburukira panyika.

Zvinokosha kuziva kuti gore rechi45 raMaharaj Shri Dibyasingha Deb wePuri rinonongedza kugore riri pakati pa2005 na2008.

Achyutananda Das Ji akanyora zvakare mumagwaro ake eMalika –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତି ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ହୋଇବ ସଂସାର,
କାହାରି ନ ଥିବ କୁଳ ବିଚାର।
ବହୁତ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସଂସାରେ ହେବ,
ନିର୍ଭୟ ହୋଇ ଜଣେ କେ ନଥିବ।
ଦିବ୍ୟସିଂହ ଦେବ ପଞ୍ଚଚାଳିଶେ,
ଆସିବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କଳକୀ ବେଶେ।
ନାଶିବେ ଯାବତ୍ ଅବନୀଭାର,
ଏଇଠୁ ହୋଇବ ପୃଥବୀ ସଂହାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ati anyāya hoiba saṃsāra,
kāhāri na thiba kuḷa bichāra.
bahuta utpāta saṃsāre heba,
nirbhaya hoi jaṇe ke nathiba.
Dibyasiṃha deba pañchachāliśe,
āsibe Śrī Hari Kaḷakī beśe.
nāśibe jābat abanībhāra,
eithu hoiba pṛuthabī saṃhāra.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKaliyuga, pachava nekusaruramisira kukuru munyika. Hapana achava nehanya nemhuri kana kutevera Dharma. Pachava nemhirizhonga kwese kwese; hapana achapedza mazuva ake murunyararo nemufaro. Panguva iyoyo, mugore rechi45 ramambo anonzi Dibyasingha Deb (pakati pa2005-2008), Ishe Shri Hari vachaburukira panyika ino inofa vari muchimiro chaKalki. Vachabvisa mutoro wechivi panyika, vaparadze vakaipa, uye vaparadze 'Mlechhas'.

MaMahapurusha akataura nezvekuzvarwa kwaIshe Kalki munzvimbo dzakawanda mumagwaro akawanda.

Mune rimwe remagwaro ake eMalika, ‘kalikalpa Gita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora –

Odia Sloka:

ବିରଜା ପୀଠ ବୈତରଣୀ ନଦୀ,
ଦଶାଶନୀ ଘାଟ ଉଦୟ ଭେଦି।
ସପତ ମାତୃକା ଟେକିଅଛି ଶିର,
ତାଙ୍କ ଅଧରୁ ବହିବ ରୁଧିର।
ସେହି ସଂକେତକୁ ଜାଣିଥାଅ ରାମ,
କଳଙ୍କି ଉଦୟ ନିଶ୍ଚୟ ଜାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Birajā pīṭha Baitaraṇī nadī,
Dashāshanī ghāṭa udaya bhedi.
Sapata mātṛkā ṭekiachhi shira,
tāṅka adharu bahiba rudhira.
Sehi saṅketaku jāṇithāa Rāma,
Kaḷaṅki udaya nishchaya jāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Rwizi rweBaitarani runoyerera pedyo neTemberi yeBiraja iri mudunhu reJajpur muOdisha. Pedyo neDashashwamedha Ghat yeRwizi rweBaitarani irworwo kune temberi yeSaptamatruka, uko kune Vanamai Vanomwe Vamwarikadzi—kureva Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, naNarasimhi—varipo.

Mahapurush anonyora kuti kana ropa rikabuda pamiromo, kureva kuti, pamukanwa, paVanamai veSaptamatruka, ichava chiratidzo chinofanira kunzwisiswa nevadziviriri kuti Ishe Kalki vazvarwa kana kuti kuzvarwa kwavo kwava pedyo zvikuru.

Zvinonyatsozivikanwa kuti chiitiko ichi chakatotanga kuitika muTemberi yeSaptamatruka, zvichiratidza kuti chiporofita chaMahapurush Achyutananda Das ichokwadi.

Zvinoenderana nevapristi veTemberi yeBiraja, Jajpur, neTemberi yeSaptamatruka, nevanhu veko, kubuda ropa pamukanwa paVanamai veSaptamatruka kwakatotanga kuitika mugore ra2005.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Shibakalpa Nabakhanda Nirghanta’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେ ଦିନ ବିରଜା ଆସ୍ଥାନ ଛୋଡ଼ିବ ଭୀମ ସିଂହନାଦ ହେବ,
ଗର୍ଭସ୍ଥିତ ଶିଶୁଭୂମିରେ ପଡ଼ିବେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ଯିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Je dina Birajā āsthāna choḍiba bhīma siṃhanāda heba,
