garbhasthita śiśubhūmire paḍibe Bhubaneśwaraku jiba.

Zvinoreva:
Pano, Mahapurush ari kutsanangura nguva yekuzvarwa kwaMahaprabhu nemutauro wechiratidzo. Anonyora kuti pazuva iro Maa Biraja achasiya chigaro chake, panguva iyoyo mwana (Ishe Kalki) achapinda mudumbu ramai vake, uyo achazoenda kuBhubaneswar.

Zvinonyatsozivikanwa kuti Maa Biraja akasiya chigaro chake mugore ra2004. Mumwedzi waGunyana 2004, vamwe vanhu vezvematongerwo enyika vakaronga ndokuba chifananidzo chaMaa Biraja (chiri muTemberi yeBiraja, Jajpur). Mushure memwedzi inenge 6, muna Kurume 2005, chifananidzo chaAmai chakadzokera kutemberi.

Kubva mundima nhanhatu dziri pamusoro, zvinonyatsoonekwa kuti Mahaprabhu Kalki vazvarwa, uye nguva yavo iri pakati pemakore a2005-2008. Naizvozvo, ngazvive pachena kuvaverengi vose kuti avatar yaKalki iripo panyika panguva ino.

8.1 Mahaprabhu vazvarirwa kupi?

Zvinoenderana nemaPuranas, nzvimbo yekuzvarirwa kwaIshe Kalki musha weSambhal. Bhavishya Malika inotaurawo kuti Ishe Kalki vachazvarirwa mumusha weSambhal. Munhu wese anobvumirana pane izvi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କ କଷଣ ଦେଖି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଭଗବାନ,
କଳିରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ଜନମ ହୋଇଣ।
ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ପିତାଙ୍କର ନାମଟି ଅଟଇ,
ମାତା ନାମ ସୁମତି ଯେ କହିଲୁ ବୁଝାଇ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhaktaṅka kaṣaṇa dekhi prabhu bhagabāna,
Kaḷire Sambaḷa nagre janama hoiṇa.
Biṣṇusharmā pitāṅkara nāmaṭi aṭai,
mātā nāma Sumati je kahilu bujhāi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, vachiona kutambudzika kwevadziviriri panguva yepamusoro yeKaliyuga, Ishe Kalki vachazvarirwa muSambal Nagar. Zita rababa vaMahaprabhu richava Vishnusharma uye zita ramai vavo richava Sumati.

Mahapurush akanyora zvakare mu‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ହେବେ ଜନମ,
ଛତିଆ ବଟରେ ଲାଗିବ ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି ସଂଗ୍ରାମ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Sambaḷa nagare prabhu se gupte hebe janama,
Chhatiā baṭare lāgiba dibārātri saṅgrāma.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Mahaprabhu Kalki vachazvarirwa muchivande muSambal Nagar. Kurwisana kwemasikati neusiku kana hondo ichaputika paChhatia Bata.

Achyutananda Das Ji akanyora zvakare mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ମାରୁ ମହରଗ ରାଜକର ଆସି ଘରେ ଘରେ ପଶିଯିବ,
ବାରଣ ବେଳରେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ କଳ୍କୀ ଉଦୟ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Māru maharaga rājakara āsi ghare ghare paśijiba,
bāraṇa beḷare Sambaḷanagre Kaḷkī udaya hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana rufu, nzara, nemitero zvapinda mudzimba dzose uye mhirizhonga yapara kwese kwese, Ishe Kalki vachabuda muSambal Nagar panguva iyoyo yekupedzisira yeKaliyuga.

Mahapurush akanyora zvakare mu‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କହନ୍ତି ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଶୁଣ ରାମଦାସ ଭକତ ମୋ ଗଳା ମାଳ,
ଭକତ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସମ୍ବଳନଗ୍ରେ ହେବି କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kahanti Achyuta śuṇa Rāmadāsa bhakata mo gaḷā māḷa,
bhakata nimante Sambaḷanagre hebi Kaḷkī abatāra.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mu‘Tera Janma Sharana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳକୀର ସୀମା କାଳ ପୂରିଗଲେ କଳ୍କୀ ବିଷ୍ଣୁଯଶା ପୁରେ,
ସମ୍ବଳ ଗ୍ରାମରେ ଜାତ ହୋଇଥିବେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାର କାଳରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷakīra sīmā kāḷa pūrigale Kaḷkī Biṣṇujashā pure,
Sambaḷa grāmare jāta hoithibe mlechha saṃhāra kāḷare.

Zvinoreva:
"Kana nguva yeKaliyuga yapera, Kalki vachazvarirwa mumba maVishnuyasha mumusha weSambal kuti vaparadze Mlechhas."

Zvisinei, kuti 'Sambal' iri kupi inyaya inokonzera kusawirirana mupfungwa dzevanhu vazhinji kunyange nanhasi. Muchinyorwa chino, tichagadzirisa izvi.

8.2 Bhavishya Malika inonyatsochii pamusoro penyaya iyi?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Jaiphula Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ବେଳ କାଳ ଜାଣି,
ଓଡିଶାରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନ୍ମିବେ ପୁଣି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କେହି ତାଙ୍କ ମାୟାକୁ ନ ଚିହ୍ନି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Sehi beḷa kāḷa jāṇi,
Oḍishāre prabhu janmibe puṇi lo jāiphula,
kehi tāṅka māyāku na chinhi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva iyoyo apo chivi chiri pamusoro, Ishe Kalki vachazvarirwa mudunhu reOdisha. Manyepo aIshe achava akasimba zvekuti hapana achakwanisa kuvaziva.

Mahapurush anonyora zvakare mune rimwe remagwaro ake eMalika –

Odia Sloka:

ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁରେ କଳ୍କୀର ଜନମ,
ଗ୍ରାମ ନାମ ରାଜହଂସ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଜନମି ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ଏହା କହିଲି ପ୍ରକାଶ୍ୟ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Utkala bhuiñre Kaḷkīra janama,
grāma nāma Rājahansa.
Kaḷkī janami līḷā prakāśiba,
ehā kahili prakāshya.

Zvinoreva:
"Kuzvarwa kwaKalki kuchava panyika yeUtkala (Odisha), mumusha unonzi Rajahansa. Mushure mekuzvarwa, Kalki vacharatidza mitambo yavo yemweya."

Zvino, maererano nekuti Ishe Kalki vachazvarirwa kupi muOdisha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das pachake akanyora mune rimwe gwaro reMalika, ‘Bhabishya Jatipataka Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ନଦୀ ତୀର,
ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରେ ବିଷ୍ଣୁବିପ୍ର ଦ୍ୱିଜବର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Oḍiśā deśare gupta Gaṅgā nadī tīra,
Sambaḷa nagre Biṣṇubipra dwijabara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nzvimbo yekuzvarirwa kwaMahaprabhu ichava pedyo nemahombekombe eGupta Ganga (Rwizi rweBaitarani) muSambal Nagar, Odisha. Panzvimbo iyoyo, mumba memuBrahmin ane hunhu anorumbidza Ishe Vishnu, Mahaprabhu Kalki vachazvarirwa.

Kubva mundima mbiri dziri pamusoro, zvinonyatsojeka kuti zvinoenderana neBhavishya Malika, Ishe Kalki vachazvarirwa muSambal, iri mudunhu reIndia reOdisha.

8.3 Sambal iri kupi muOdisha?

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Jagannath Das, akanyora mumagwaro eMalika ‘Bhabishyat Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ରରେ,
ଦୁଇ ଜଣ ଅଛନ୍ତି ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଘରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jājapura Sambaḷa nagrare,
dui jaṇa achhanti brāhmaṇa ghare.

Zvinoreva:
Mundima iri pamusoro, Mahapurush anonyora – “Jajapura Sambala nagrare”. Kureva kuti, “MuSambal Nagar yeJajpur”. Zvinonyatsojeka kuti Sambal Nagar iri muJajpur.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

କ୍ଷୀର ନଦୀ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଯେ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ଖ୍ୟାତ ଯେ କମଳପୁର ଗୁପ୍ତ ଯେ ସମ୍ବଳ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kṣhīra nadī dakṣhiṇa je Birajā maṇḍala,
khyāta je Kamalapura gupta je Sambaḷa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kumaodzanyemba kweRwizi rweKhira nekuchamhembe kweMahanadi kune nzvimbo yetemberi yeBiraja. Pedyo nenzvimbo iyi yeBiraja pane musha unozivikanwa seKamalapur. Izvi ndizvo zvinonzi musha weSambal muchivande.

Kuburikidza nendima iyi, Mahapurush akaratidza kuti Sambal iri munzvimbo yeBiraja kana dunhu reJajpur muOdisha.

Ngatinzwisise kuti Sambal iri kupi muOdisha kuburikidza nemamwe mavhesi.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Gurubhakti Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘର,
ଜନମିଣ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଚକ୍ରଧର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Biṣṇusharmā ghara,
janamiṇa tapasyā karibe Chakradhara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Chakradhara (Ishe Vishnu) vachazvarwa mumba memutendi anogara muSambal Nagar, Gaya, uye vachazoita kuranga muviri.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyorazve mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଗୃହେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହୋଇବେ ଜାତ,
ଗୟାରେ ସମ୍ବଳ ନଗ୍ର ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Biṣṇusharmā gruhe prabhu hoibe jāta,
Gayāre Sambaḷa nagra Harihara kṣhetra.

Zvinoreva:
"Ishe vachazvarwa mumba maVishnusharma, mudunhu reHarihara reSambal Nagar, Gaya."

Zvinoenderana nemavhesi maviri ari pamusoro, kuzvarwa kwaIshe kuchaitika muSambal Nagar yeGaya. Zvino, kuti Gaya ipi ichave iyoyo uye kuti iri kupi muOdisha zvakataurwa naIshe Krishna pachavo kumaGopas avo gumi nevaviri, izvo Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akataura mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Biraja Mahatmya’. Mahapurush akanyora –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ବାର ସୁତ ନିହାର ବଚନ ଏ ଅଟେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଠାର,
ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥେ ହରିହର କ୍ଷେତ୍ର ଗ୍ରାମଟି ସମ୍ବଳ ପୁର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Śuṇa bāra suta nihāra bachana e aṭe Achyuta ṭhāra,
Nābhigayā tīrthe Harihara kṣhetra grāmaṭi Sambaḷa pura.

Zvinoreva:
"Teererai imi vanakomana gumi nevaviri, iri izwi rechokwadi kubva kuna Achyuta: musha weSambalpur uri mudunhu reHarihara panzvimbo tsvene yeNabhigaya."

Kureva kuti, dunhu reHarihara riri panzvimbo tsvene yeNabhigaya, inonzi Sambal.

Muhurukuro dziri pamusoro, taona munzvimbo dzakawanda kuti Mahapurush akatsanangura nzvimbo yekuzvarirwa kwaMahaprabhu sedunhu reBiraja kana nzvimbo tsvene yeGaya (Nabhigaya). Zvino tichaziva chaizvo kuti matunhu aya ari kupi, kuti zvitendero zvengano zvine chekuita nawo zvii, uye kuti kukosha kwawo kwakadii.

8.3.1 Dunhu reBiraja

‘Temberi yeBiraja’ itemberi huru iri pamahombekombe eRwizi rweBaitarani muJajpur, Odisha, uko Adi Shakti anonamatwa muchimiro cha ‘Biraja’. Chaizvoizvo, munguva dzekare, Brahma Ji pachake akaita Yajna (chibayiro) pano paBrahmakunda, mushure mezvo Amai Parvati vakabuda mumoto wechibayiro ndokuraira Brahma Ji kuti vamugadzike nezita rekuti ‘Biraja’. Dunhu iri rinonziwo dunhu reBiraja mushure mezita raMwarikadzi Biraja.

Paiva panzvimbo iyi apo guvhu (Nabhi) raMwarikadzi Sati rakawira, zvichiita kuti ive imwe ye51 Shakti Peethas. Iyi ndiyo temberi chete uko Mwarikadzi anonamatwa muchimiro chaMahishasura Mardini ane maoko maviri.

Nzvimbo iyi inozivikanwawo seNabhigaya yePinda Daan (zvipo zvemadzitateguru). Kutaura zvazviri, ndiyo chete Shakti Peetha uko Pinda Daan inoitwa. Pedyo neBrahmakunda, pane tsime rakadzika rinonzi Nabhikunda, nemvura yaro vanhu vanoita Pinda Daan kumadzitateguru avo. Nzvimbo iyi inotaurwawo muSkanda Purana.

8.3.2 Nabhigaya

Zvinotaurwa muPuranas kuti muSatya Yuga, paiva nedhimoni rainzi Gayasura. Kunyange akazvarwa mumhuri yemadhimoni, moyo wake wakafemerwa neDharma uye kuranga muviri. Aiva munamati zvikuru, ane simba guru, uye aiva nemuviri muhombe. Aigara achishandira rubatsiro rwenyika uye kubatsira vamwe. Zvinotaurwa muPuranas kuti dhimoni rakaita kuranga muviri kwakanyanya, izvo zvakafadza Ishe Vishnu, uye Ishe vakamukumbira kuti akumbire chikomborero. Gayasura akakumbira chikomborero chakasiyana. Akati, “Ngavawane denga nekungobatwa neni.” Ishe Vishnu vakapa chikomborero ichi vachiona kuranga muviri kwake kwakasimba. Asi semugumisiro, Yamaloka (nyika yevakafa) yakatanga kuva isina vanhu, nekuti chero ani zvake akabatwa naGayasura aienda akananga kudenga. Izvi zvakava dambudziko guru kuvamwari naYamraj. Vamwari vese vakasvika kuUtatu (Brahma, Vishnu, Mahesh) ndokukumbira kuti vawane mhinduro kudambudziko iri. Vamwari vakakumbira Utatu kuti vamise Gayasura. Utatu hwakasarudza kumisa Gayasura, uyo aikonzeresa zvipingamupinyi mumitemo yekusika kunyange aiva akarurama. Vatatu vavo vakatora chimiro cheBrahmins vatatu, vakaenda kuna Gayasura, ndokuti vaida kuita Yajna nguva dzose kwemazuva manomwe uye nekuda kweizvi, vaida nzvimbo yakakura uye tsvene. Vakaudza Gayasura kuti ape muviri wake weYajna, asi haafanire kufamba kwemazuva manomwe aya. Gayasura akabvuma izvi. Gayasura akatambanudza musoro wake muGaya (Bihar) uko Ishe Vishnu vaigara, akaisa dunhu reguvhu rake muNabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha) uko Brahma Ji vaigara, uye akaisa tsoka dzake muPithapuram (Andhra Pradesh) uko Ishe Shiva vaigara, uye nekudaro Yajna yakatanga. Utatu hwakaita Yajna nguva dzose kwemazuva manomwe.

Gayasura akaramba achiverenga mazuva nekunzwa kukukuridza kwejongwe mangwanani. Achiziva izvi, pazuva rekupedzisira, Ishe Shiva vakatora chimiro chejongwe ndokukukuridza husiku husati hwapera. Achinzwa ruzha rwejongwe, Gayasura akafunga kuti kwaedza ndokufambisa muviri wake achifunga kuti mazuva manomwe apera. Utatu hwakabva hwazviratidza pamberi pake ndokuti Gayasura akanga atyora vimbiso yake yekuchengeta muviri wake wakanyarara kwemazuva manomwe. Vakati chirango chekutyora vimbiso yaiva rufu. Gayasura akarurama akagamuchira izvi pasina chichemo. Utatu hwakafadzwa nekunamata kwake ndokumukumbira kuti akumbire chikomborero. Ipapo Gayasura akavakumbira kuti zita rake rirangarirwe pamwe neUtatu. Akavakumbira kuti vagare vashongedze muviri wake nenguo dzeBrahmins. Akakumbirawo chikomborero chekuti kana madzitateguru emunhu apiwa zvipo (Pinda Daan neTarpan) panzvimbo idzi nhatu, haafanire kuzvarwa zvakare.

Vachigamuchira chishuwo chaGayasura, Ishe Vishnu vakatora chimiro chaGadadhar muGaya, Brahma Ji anonamatwa muchimiro chaGirija Devi muNabhigaya, uye Ishe Shiva anonamatwa muPithapuram saKukkuteshwar (Ishe akatora chimiro chejongwe). Nenzira iyi, nzvimbo idzi nhatu dzakava dzinozivikanwa nePinda Daan neTarpan:

  • Shirogaya (Gaya, Bihar)
  • Nabhigaya (Jajpur, Odisha)
  • Padagaya (Pithapuram, Andhra Pradesh)

Uchapupu hwekuti nzvimbo tsvene yeNabhigaya iri muJajpur inowanikwawo mumabhuku eMalika ePanchasakha Mahapurushas.

Mumabhuku eMalika ‘Chakada Madana’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁରଠାରେ ନାଭିଗୟା ତୀର୍ଥ ଏକଥା ଜାଣ ସତ୍ୱର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jājapurathāre Nābhigayā tīrtha ekathā jāṇa satwara.

Zvinoreva:
"Ziva izvi nechokwadi chose, nzvimbo tsvene yeNabhigaya iri muJajpur."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, achiprofita nezvekuzvarwa kwake kwechigumi makore anenge 600 apfuura, akanyora mumabhuku eMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦଶମ ଜନମ ମୋହର ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଯାଜପୁର ନାଭିଗୟା ହରିହର ଖ୍ୟାତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Daśama janama mohara hoiba prakāśa,
Jājapura Nābhigayā Harihara khyāta.

Zvinoreva:
"Kuzvarwa kwangu kwechigumi kucharatidzwa mudunhu rinozivikanwa reHarihara reNabhigaya, Jajpur."

Kuburikidza nevhesi iyi zvakare, zviri pachena kuti nzvimbo tsvene yeNabhigaya iri muJajpur.

Mahapurush anonyora pamusoro penyaya imwe chete iyi mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Kalikalpa Gita’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜପୁର ପଞ୍ଚକୋଶ ବିରଜା ମଣ୍ଡଳ,
ନାଭିଗୟା ବିଷ୍ଣୁଶର୍ମା ଘରେ ହେବ ଖେଳ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jājapura pañchakośa Birajā maṇḍala,
Nābhigayā Biṣṇusharmā ghare heba kheḷa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, dunhu reBiraja riri mukati me radius yemakosha mashanu eJajpur. Ishe Kalki vachazvarwa mumba memutendi waVishnu anogara mudunhu iroro reBiraja, Nabhigaya.

Naizvozvo, kubva muhurukuro iri pamusoro, zviri pachena chose kuti Sambal Nagar, nzvimbo tsvene yeGaya, uye dunhu reBiraja uko Panchasakhas akaprofita kuzvarwa kwaIshe Kalki, iri chaizvo mukati medunhu reJajpur reOdisha.

Kuti tiwane kujeka kwakawanda pamusoro penzvimbo yekuzvarirwa kwaIshe Kalki, tichatora mamwe mavhesi akataurwa muMalika.

Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das akanyora mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ଓଡିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକତଙ୍କୁ ଉଶ୍ୱାସୀ ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷkī abatāre Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśi bhaktaṅku uśwāsī dharmaku tumbhe pāḷiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Kalki Avatar achazvarwa mudunhu reOdisha. Mahaprabhu Kalki vachaparadza vakaipa, vachasvitsa vatendi, uye vachadzorera Dharma Panyika.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, achitaura mashoko aIshe, anonyora mumabhuku ake eMalika –

Odia Sloka:

ଇନ୍ଦୁ ପରେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ଦେଖିବ ଜାଣ,
ଆମ୍ଭ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ ଯାଜପୁରେଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Indu pare bindu dekhiba jāṇa,
āmbha ābirbhāba Jājapureṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana kadonhwe kana nyenyedzi ikaonekwa pamusoro pemwedzi mudenga, ziva kuti kuratidzwa kwaIshe Mukuru kuchaitika muJajpur.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Jai Birachita Chautisha’, anonyora achidzokorora chaizvo mashoko aIshe pachavo –

Odia Sloka:

ଯେତେବେଳେ ନୀଳାଚଳ ଛାଡିକରି ଯିବୁ,
ଯାଜପୁର ବିପ୍ର ଘରେ ଯାଇଁ ଜନମିବୁ।
ଜଗିଥିବୁ ହେ। ଯେ ଯେତେ କହିଲେ ନ ଶୁଣିବୁ ହେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jetebeḷe Nīḷāchaḷa chhāḍikari jibu,
Jājapura bipra ghare jāiñ janamibu.
Jagithibu he. Je jete kahile na śuṇibu he.

Zvinoreva:
"Kana ukasiya Nilachala (Puri), uchaenda undozvarwa mumba meBrahmin muJajpur. Chenjera; usateerere chero zvinotaurwa nemunhu."

Mumabhuku eMalika ‘Tatwabodhini’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହେବେ,
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ବ୍ରାହ୍ମଣ ଭାବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jājanagre prabhu janama hebe,
janama hoibe brāhmaṇa bhābe.

Zvinoreva:
"Ishe vachazvarwa muJajanagar; Vachazvarwa seBrahmin."

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika anonzi ‘Bigyanakalpa’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଯାଜନଗ୍ରେ ମହାପ୍ରଭୁ ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ,
ଗରୁଡକୁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନି ଲୀଳା ଯେ କରିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jājanagre Mahāprabhu janama hoibe,
Garuḍaku saṅge gheni līḷā je karibe.

Zvinoreva:
"Mahaprabhu achazvarirwa muJajanagar; achitora Garuda, achaita mitambo yake yeumwari."

Kubva mundima dziri pamusoro, hapachina kusahadzika kuti kuzvarwa kwaMahaprabhu Kalki kwaive nechokwadi chekuitika muSambal Nagar, nzvimbo tsvene yeNabhigaya, dunhu reBiraja, dunhu reJajpur, Odisha.

Kana tikatarisa Bhagavata Purana neMahabharata zvakanyorwa Bhavishya Malika isati yanyorwa, zvinoratidzawo Jajpur, Odisha maererano nenzvimbo yekuzvarirwa kwaIshe Kalki.

Munguva dzekare, Maharishi Vedavyas akataura nzvimbo yekuzvarirwa kwaIshe Kalki se 'musha weSambhal' muShrimad Bhagavata Mahapurana. Akanga anyora –

Ndima yeSanskrit:

संभलग्राममुख्यस्य ब्राह्मणस्य महात्मनः ।
भवने विष्णुयशसः कल्किः प्रादुर्भविष्यति ।।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Śambhalagrāmamukhyasya brāhmaṇasya mahātmanaḥ |
bhavane viṣṇuyaśasaḥ kalkiḥ prādurbhaviṣyati ||

Zvinoreva:
"Mumba memuBrahmin ane mweya mukuru, mukuru wemusha weSambhal, anonzi Vishnuyasha, Ishe Kalki vachazviratidza."

Gare gare, mu 'Vanaparva' yeMahabharata, Maharishi Vyas pachake akachinja izvi zvishoma ndokutumidza nzvimbo yekuzvarirwa kwaIshe Kalki se 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama'. Akanyora –

Ndima yeSanskrit:

कल्कि विष्णुयशा नाम द्विजः कालप्रचोदितः ।
उत्पत्स्यते महावीर्यो महाबुद्धिपराक्रमः ।।
सम्भूतः सम्भलग्रामे ब्राह्मणावसथे शुभे ।
मनसा तस्य सर्वाणि वाहनान्यायुधानि च ।।
उपस्थास्यन्ति योधाश्च शस्त्राणि कवचानि च ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kalki Vishnuyashā nāma dwijaḥ kālaprachoditaḥ |
utpatsyate mahāvīryo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ ||
sambhūtaḥ sambhalagrāme brāhmaṇāvasathe shubhe |
manasā tasya sarvāṇi vāhanānyāyudhāni cha ||
upasthāsyanti yodhāshcha shastrāṇi kavachāni cha |

Zvinoreva:
Kuburikidza nendima dziri pamusoro, Maharishi Vyas akataura kuti Ishe Kalki vaizozvarirwa mu 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama', zvichireva musha weSambhal 'wakavambwa' kana 'wakagadzwa', musha weSambhal waizenge wakagadzwa sekoloni yevaBrahmin yekuitira maYajnas nekunamata. Akanyora kuti Ishe Kalki vaizozvarirwa mumba memuBrahmin ane hunhu aigara mumusha uyu weSambhal uye achiimba nziyo dzekurumbidza Ishe Vishnu.

Zvino, kana tikatarisa nekutsvaga mumapeji enhoroondo, tichaona kuti nzvimbo yevaBrahmin yakagadzwa yeYajna nekunamata iri mudunhu reJajpur, Odisha.

Chaizvoizvo, zita reJajpur rakapihwa muzana ramakore rechi10 mushure mezita raMambo weSomavamsi Jajati (Yayati). Mambo Jajati akavamba guta iri ndokuita Abhinav Jajatinagar guta rake guru. Gare gare, achichinja zita raro, rakatanga kuitwa Jajanagar ndokuzova Jajpur. Mambo Jajati Keshari akanga aunza vaBrahmin vanenge 10,000 kubva kuKannauj muUttar Pradesh ndokuvagadzirisa muJajpur, Odisha, kuti vaite maAshwamedha Yajnas gumi (Dashashwamedha). Kunyange nanhasi, Dashashwamedha Ghat pamahombekombe eRwizi rweBaitarani inotiyambira chiitiko ichi. Zvisinei, Maharishi Vyas akanga aratidza koloni iyi yevaBrahmin yakagadzwa kumabvazuva kweTemberi yeBiraja muJajpur achiida kuti 'Sambhoota Sambhal Grama' mu 'Vanaparva' yeMahabharata.

9 Khandagiri – MuBhubaneswar here kana kumwe kunhu?

Chero munhu anofarira Bhavishya Malika haana kusahadzika nezvekwachazvarirwa Mahaprabhu Kalki. Vanonyatsoziva kuti avatar yaKalki ichazvarirwa mumusha weSambal uri muJajpur, Odisha.

Havanawo kusahadzika kuti mushure mekuzvarirwa muJajpur, Ishe vachagara panzvimbo inonzi Khandagiri.

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Shishu Ananta Das, akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି କଳିଯୁଗେ କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାରେ ହରି,
ଶ୍ରୀ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ehi kaliyuge Kalki abatare Hari,
Shri khaṇḍagirire thibe prabhu bije kari.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, muKaliyuga, Ishe Hari vachagara muKhandagiri muchimiro chavo cheKalki.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mu ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

ଗୋପନେ ମୁକୁନ୍ଦ ଯେ ଆସି,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ ଥିବେ ବସି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Gopane Mukunda je asi,
Khandagirire thibe basi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe vachagara muchivande muKhandagiri.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das akanyorazve mune imwe yemagwaro ake eMalika, 'Patta Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭକତ ସୁମେଳ ହୋଇ ଏକ ଠୁଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଶେ ଯିବେ,
ଅନନ୍ତ ମୂରତି ଦର୍ଶନ ସାରିଣ ସର୍ବେ ସର୍ବଙ୍କୁ ଚିହ୍ନିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhakata sumeḷa hoi eka ṭhuḷa Khaṇḍagiri pāshe jibe,
ananta mūrati darshana sāriṇa sarbe sarbaṅku chinhibe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panzvimbo inonzi Khandagiri, pachava nekuungana kwevatsvene. Ikoko, vatsvene vachawana darshan (chiono) chaIshe Kalki uye vachazivana.

Kusvika panguva ino, munhu wese anobvumirana, asi kusahadzika kwevanhu kunomuka maererano nekuti Khandagiri iyi iri kupi. Mazuva ano, kuburikidza nemamwe mavhidhiyo nemaPDF, zvirevo zvakasiyana-siyana zviri kuitwa nezveKhandagiri. Maererano nevamwe vanhu, nzvimbo iyi inonzi Khandagiri, kwachazogara Ishe, iri muJajpur, Odisha, asi vamwe vanoti Khandagiri iri mudunhu reDhenkanal, Odisha.

9.1 Khandagiri iri kupi?

Kuburikidza nechinyorwa ichi, regai nditaure kuti mazita ose akataurwa pamusoro maererano neKhandagiri (Jajpur, Dhenkanal), ose akakanganisa zvachose.

Achitsanangura kuti Khandagiri iri kupi, mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Basanta Manjari’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମହିମା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଭକ୍ତ ତହିଁ ସେଠାରେ ଅଛଇ।
ଗୁପତ ଲୀଳା ହୋଇବ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ,
ତପସ୍ୟାରେ ରହିଥିବେ ଋଷିଙ୍କ ସଙ୍ଗରେ।
ଗୁପ୍ତ ଗଙ୍ଗା ସେକାଳରେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବ,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରକୁ ସେହି ଭସାଇଣ ଦେବ।
ତ୍ରିଭୁବନ ସ୍ୱର କଥା କହିତ ନୁହଁଇ,
ବାରଭୁଜା ଦୁର୍ଗାମାତା ଜଗି ରହିଛଇଁ।
ଆହୁରି କଥାଏ ବାବୁ ଶୁଣ ମନଦେଇ,
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଜଗିଅଛି ସେଠାବକୁ ତହିଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Khaṇḍagiri mahimā kahita nuhañi,
lakṣha lakṣha bhakta tahin seṭhāre achhai.
Gupata līḷā hoiba Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre,
tapasyāre rahithibe ruṣhiṅka saṅgare.
Gupta Gaṅgā sekāḷare bāhara hoiba,
Bhubaneśwaraku sehi bhasāiṇa deba.
Tribhubana swara kathā kahita nuhañi,
bārabhuja Durgāmātā jagi rahichhaiṃ.
Āhuri kathāe bābu śuṇa manadei,
Bhūāsuṇī jagiachhi seṭhābaku tahiñ.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kubwinya kweKhandagiri hakugone kutsanangurwa. Kune zviuru zvevatsvene ikoko. Zviito zvaMwari zvakavanzika (leelas) zvichaitika muKhandagiri. Vatsvene vazhinji nemamonki vari kuita kutenda ikoko. Rwizi rwakavanzika (Gupta Ganga) rwuri kuyerera ikoko, rwuchakonzera kuparadzwa muBhubaneswar panguva yezviito zvinoparadza zvaKalki. Kubwinya kweBhubaneswar iyoyo kwakakurawo. Ikoko, Amai Barabhuji naAmai Bhuasuni varipo, vachaita basa rekuparadza pakupera kweKaliyuga.

Mundima iri pamusoro, Mahapurush akajekesa kuti Khandagiri iri muBhubaneswar.

Achitsanangura nzvimbo yeKhandagiri, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyorawo mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଲାଗିବ ଗୋଳ,
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଯେ ଅଛି ସେଠାର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପକୁ ଧ୍ୟାନ କରନ୍ତି।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହିଯାଏ ଗୁପ୍ତର,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ହୁଏ ସତ୍ୱର।
ଘଟଣା ସ୍ଥାନ ଯେ ଅଟଇ ସେହି,
କଳିର ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ସେଠାହିଁ।
ଉଦୟଗିରି ଯେ ଅପୋଡା ସ୍ଥାନ,
ସେଠାବରେ ବହୁ ସାଧୁ ଆଶ୍ରମ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhubaneśwarare lāgiba goḷa, ekāmra bana je achhi seṭhāra.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sādhu achhanti, Kaḷkī rūpaku dhyāna karanti.
Kṣhīra dhāra bahijāe guptara, gumphāre bhajana hue satwara.
Ghaṭaṇā sthāna je aṭai sehi, Kaḷira bhārata yuddha seṭhāhiñ.
Udayagiri je apoḍā sthāna, seṭhābare bahu sādhu āśrama.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pachava nehondo muBhubaneswar, uye sango reEkamra riri ikoko. Ipapo Mahapurush akataura kuti Khandagiri iriwo ikoko, uko vatsvene vazhinji vakakwana vachagara uye vachiramba vachidana zita raKalki nekufungisisa chimiro Chake. Mumapako iwayo eKhandagiri, masikati neusiku bhajans nekirtans zvichaitika. Ikoko, rwizi rwakavanzika (Kshira Nadi) ruchayererazve. Khandagiri iyoyo ichava "Nzvimbo Yezviitiko" (Ghatana Sthana), uko hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichaitika. Pedyo pane Udayagiri, uko ashrams evatsvene vazhinji vakakwana ari.

Kuburikidza nendima iri pamusoro, Mahapurush akajekesa kuti Khandagiri uko vatsvene vasingaverengeki vachagara, uko kudanwa kwakavanzika kwezita raKalki kuchaenderera mumapako, uye uko hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichaitika, iri muBhubaneswar, uko sango reEkamra ririwo.

Pano ndinoda kukwezva pfungwa dzevaverengi kune chokwadi chakakosha. Mumagwaro eMalika, Bhubaneswar inowanzonzi Ekamra Kshetra, Ekamra Van, kana Ekamra Kanan. Chaizvoizvo, maererano neEkamra Purana yezana ramakore rechi13, pakanga paine muti wemango mumwe chete pedyo nenzvimbo yemwari mukuru Lingaraj. Naizvozvo, nzvimbo yeLingaraj inonziwo Ekamra Kshetra (nzvimbo ine muti wemango mumwe chete).

Ichi ndicho chikonzero nei Mahapurushas kakawanda vachidaidza Bhubaneswar Lingaraj Kshetra (nzvimbo yetemberi yeLingaraj) kana Ekamra Van Kshetra (nzvimbo yemuti wemango mumwe chete).

Kwete izvi chete, nekuda kwenzvimbo yeBindu Sarovar muBhubaneswar, maPanchasakhas mumagwaro avo eMalika vakataurawo Bhubaneswar senzvimbo yeBindu Sarovar.

Mahapurush akanyora achijekesa mu 'Garuda Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ଏ ସବୁ ମୋ ଗୁପ୍ତ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଆଉ ପୃଥ୍ବୀରେ ଯେତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନ ପୁରାଣେ ଅଛି ବର୍ଣ୍ଣନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhubaneśwara je ekāmra kānana e sabu mo gupta sthāna,
āu pruthwīre jete siddha sthāna purāṇe achhi barṇana.

Zvinoreva:
Ishe Krishna vanoudza mutsvene wavo, Garuda, kuti Ekamra Kanan iri muBhubaneswar inzvimbo yakavanzika uye Siddha nzvimbo (yakakwana).

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika 'Chhayalisha Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଏକାମ୍ରରେ,
ରହିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ତିନିଘରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhubaneśwara Ekāmrare, rahibe bhakta tini ghare.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, vatsvene vaIshe vachagara munzvimbo yeBhubaneswar Ekamra.

Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika 'Chumbaka Malika' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ସୀମା ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିରେ,
ପଞ୍ଚବିଂଶ ଭକ୍ତ ଥିବେ ତଟରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhubaneśwara sīmā Khaṇḍagirire, pañchabiṃśha bhakta thibe taṭare.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Khandagiri iri pamuganhu weBhubaneswar uko vatsvene 25 vachatora chikamu mumitambo yeumwari.

Mahapurush anonyorazve mu 'Chakada Madana' –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଗୋଳ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାର,
ଭାବି କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ନିଜ ନାମ ସ୍ମର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhubaneśwarare goḷa Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāra,
bhābi kahai Achyutī nija nāma smara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pachava nehondo munzvimbo yeKhandagiri muBhubaneswar.

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Yasovanta Das, anonyora mumabhuku ake eMalika –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଠାରେ ଜାଣ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସେ ସରୋବର ପୁଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhubaneśwara ṭhāre jāṇa,
Bindu se sarobara puṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, ziva kuti Bindu Sarovar iri muBhubaneswar.

Mahapurush Yasovanta Das anonyora mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Bhavishyata Parardha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜ ସ୍ଥଳୀ,
ଦୁଇ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକଜଣ ସ୍ତିରୀ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhubaneśwara Liṅgarāja sthaḷī,
dui puruṣha ekajaṇa stirī.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Bhubaneswar ndiyo nzvimbo yaLingaraj, zvichireva kuti tembere yaLingaraj iri muBhubaneswar.

Kana tikashandisa Google, kunyange ipapo tinogona kuziva nyore kuti ingave Khandagiri kana Ekamra Kanan kana Bindu Sarovar kana tembere yaLingaraj, zvese zviri muBhubaneswar.

Khandagiri, iyo ichava nzvimbo yekugara kwaIshe, uko vatsvene vazhinji vachange vachidana zita raKalki—kutsanangura kuti Khandagiri iyi iri muBhubaneswar (Ekamra Kshetra), Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare mu‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ପାଦଦେଶେ ଭରତ ପାଦୁକା,
ଛଦ୍ମବେଶୀ ସାଧୁ ପୂଜା କରୁଅଛି ଏକା।
ଗୁପ୍ତଗଙ୍ଗା ବହିଅଛି ଗୁପ୍ତ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି,
ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଗୁପ୍ତ ବେଶେ ରହିଛନ୍ତି ପୁରି।
ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ ଅଟଇ ଶିବ ଯୋଗ ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶୁକପକ୍ଷୀ ପାଇଲା ଯେଉଁଠି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଜ୍ଞାନ।
ଭୂଆସୁଣୀ ଦେବୀ ଅଛି ଜାଣିଥା ସତ୍ୱର,
ସେ ଦେବୀ ଘାତରେ ପୃଥବୀ ହେବ କମ୍ପମାନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Khaṇḍagiri pādadeśe Bharata pādukā, chhadmabeśī sādhu pūjā karuachhi ekā.
Guptagaṅgā bahiachhi gupta Khaṇḍagiri, siddha sādhu gupta beśe rahichhanti puri.
Ekāmra bana aṭai Śiba yoga sthāna, śukapakṣhī pāilā jeuṇṭhi brahmajñāna.
Bhūāsuṇୀ debī achhi jāṇithā satwara, se debī ghātare pruthibī heba kampamāna.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pamakumbo pegomo reKhandagiri pane Bharata Paduka, iyo iri kunamatwa nemutsvene akavanzika. MuKhandagiri imwe cheteyo, rwizi rweGupta Ganga rwuri kuyerera muchivande. Ikoko (muKhandagiri), vatsvene vazhinji vakakwana vari kurarama vakavanzika. Sango reEkamra riripo, iyo inzvimbo yaIshe Shiva yekuzorora. Ikoko, shiri yeShuka yakambowana ruzivo rweBrahma. Pedyo pane Mwarikadzi Bhuasuni, uyo achaita basa rekuuraya Pasi pakupera kweKali Yuga.

Ndinoda kuudza vaverengi pano kuti mune imwe yemavhesi ari pamusoro, Mahapurush akataura kuti Khandagiri iri munzvimbo yesango reEkamra uye vatsvene vazhinji vari kuita penance muKhandagiri. Rwizi rwuri kuyererawo ikoko muchivande. Muvhesi rino razvino, Mahapurush ari kudzokorora chinhu chimwe chete.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare mu‘Tera Janma Sharana’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

ଲୀଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବ ଲୀଳାମୟଙ୍କର ସତ୍ୟ ଯେ ଏକାମ୍ର ବନ,
ଲୀଳା କରୁଥିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମାଧବ ସର୍ବେ ଆନନ୍ଦ ହୋଇଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Līḷā prakāśiba līḷāmayankara satya je Ekāmra bana,
līḷā karuthibe Ananta Mādhaba sarbe ānanda hoiṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Pano Mahapurush akataura zvakare kuti nzvimbo yekugara kwaIshe iBhubaneswar. Asi haana kutora zita reBhubaneswar kana Khandagiri. Mahapurush akataura nzvimbo imwe cheteyo se‘Ekamra Van’. Mahapurush akanyora kuti zviito zvechinyakare zvaIshe (Leelamaya) zvicharatidzwa kubva munzvimbo yeEkamra Van. Munzvimbo imwe cheteyo yeEkamra Van, Ishe Ananta Madhava, kureva Kalki, vachaita zviito zvechinyakare, kuona nekunzwa izvo mufaro wevatsvene hauzivi miganhu.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare mu‘Aagata Bhavishyata Malika’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

କଳା ଧଳା ଅଶ୍ୱ ଉପରେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ହେବେ ବାହାର,
ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱରରେ ରହିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ରଙ୍ଗା ଅଧର।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସିଦ୍ଧ ସ୍ଥାନରେ ଯେତେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ଋଷି,
ସକଳେ ବାହାର ହୋଇବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ନାମକୁ ଘୋଷି।
ସକଳେ ସେବେଳେ ଠୁଳ ଯେ ହେବେ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗର,
କପିଳେଶ୍ୱରରେ ଭେଟିଣ ହୋଇବେ ସୂପକାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷā dhaḷā ashwa upare prabhu hebe bāhāra,
Bhubaneshwarare rahibe prabhu raṅgā adhara.
Khaṇḍagiri siddha sthānare jete achhanti ruṣi,
sakaḷe bāhāra hoibe prabhu nāmaku ghoṣi.
Sakaḷe sebeḷe ṭhuḷa je hebe Bindu Sāgara,
Kapileshwarare bheṭiṇa hoibe sūpakāra.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe vachaenda kunoparadza vari pabhiza re“Kala-Dhala” kana dema-ne-chena. Pamberi peizvozvo, Ishe vachagara muBhubaneswar. Vatsvene vese nevarume vechitendero vari kuita penance munzvimbo yakakwana yeKhandagiri vachabuda mumapako panguva iyoyo, vachiparidza zita raIshe, uye vachaungana paBindu Sarovar iri muBhubaneswar. Ipapo, vabiki vachasangana navo vese patembere yeKapileshwar iri pedyo.

Muvhesi riri pamusoro zvakare, Khandagiri inonzi inzvimbo yakakwana iyo ichava nzvimbo yekugara yevatsvene vazhinji nevarume vechitendero. Uye zvinonziwo kuti ingave Khandagiri kana Bindu Sarovar kana Tembere yeKapileshwar, zvese izvi zviri muBhubaneswar uye zviri pedyo nemumwe.

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, paakabvunzwa nemudzidzi wake Baranga Das kuti Ishe Kalki vachazvarirwa kupi uye vachagara kupi, anotsanangura mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Chumbaka Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଶୁଣ ହେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ କହିବା ସେ ରଙ୍ଗ ପ୍ରଭୁ ଅବତାର ସ୍ଥାନ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିରଜା କ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ଜନମ ଲଭିବେ ଅନନ୍ତ ମିଶ୍ର ଗୃହେଣ।
ଜନମ ହୋଇବେ ଗୃହକୁ ଛାଡିବେ ତପସ୍ୟା କରିବେ ଯାଇଁ,
ସିଦ୍ଧଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ସିଦ୍ଧଙ୍କ ସଦନ ରହିବେ ସେ ଭାବଗ୍ରାହୀ।
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଏକାମ୍ର କାନନ ସୁବର୍ଣ୍ଣଚ୍ୟୁତ ଗିରିରେ,
ସେହି ସ୍ଥାନେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ବିଜେ କରିଥିବେ ଭକ୍ତ ଜନ ସଙ୍ଗତରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ଅନନ୍ତ ବାସୁଦେବ ବିପ୍ରଲିଙ୍ଗ ଭେଟ ସେ ଯହିଁ ଆଗରେ,
ବିନ୍ଦୁସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ବିଧି ସାରିଣ ସିଦ୍ଧ କେଦାର ମଧ୍ୟରେ।
ଶ୍ରୀ ବିନ୍ଦୁ ସାଗରେ ସ୍ନାନ ସାରିବେ ସମସ୍ତ ଭକତ ମିଳି,
ନିଜ କଳା ଭକ୍ତି କଳା ପ୍ରକାଶିବେ ଛାଡିବେ କଳିର ମଳି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Shuṇa he bāraṅga kahibā se raṅga prabhu abatāra sthāna,
Shrī Birajā kṣetre janama labhibe Ananta Mishra gruheṇa.
Janama hoibe gruhaku chhāḍibe tapasyā karibe jāiñ,
Siddhagiri sthāna siddhaṅka sadana rahibe se bhābagrāhī.
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna Ekāmra kānana Subarṇachyuta girire,
sehi sthāne prabhu bije karithibe bhakta jana saṅgatare.
Shrī Ananta Bāsudeba bipraliṅga bheṭa se jahiñ āgare,
Bindusāgare snāna bidhi sāriṇa siddha Kedāra madhyare.
Shrī Bindu Sāgare snāna sāribe samasta bhakata miḷi,
nija kaḷā bhakti kaḷā prakāshibe chhāḍibe kaḷira maḷi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe vachazvarirwa mumba memutsvene anonzi Ananta Mishra muShri Viraja Kshetra (Jajpur). Pashure pekuberekwa, Ishe vachasiya musha wavo voenda kunzvimbo yevatsvene vakakwana kunoita penance. Mahapurush haana kungomira pakutaura kuti "nzvimbo yakakwana," akataurawo kuti zita renzvimbo iyoyo richava Khandagiri, iyo ichava muEkamra Kanan. Munzvimbo imwe cheteyo yeKhandagiri yeEkamra Kanan, Ishe vachagara nevatsvene vavo. Mahapurush akataura kuti tembere yeShri Ananta Vasudev ichavapo, uye Linga, kureva tembere yaLingaraj, ichavapowo. Ishe vachageza muBindu Sagar uye vachashanyira tembere yeSiddha Kedara (Kedareshwar).

Mahapurush akataurawo kuti vatsvene vese vachageza muBindu Sarovar imwe cheteyo, pashure peizvozvo chiedza chitsvene chichaonekwa muvatsvene, uye vese vachabviswa tsvina yeKali Yuga.

Kuburikidza nevhesi riri pamusoro, Sishu Ananta Dasji akajekesa kuti patembere yeShri Ananta Vasudev, patembere yeKedareshwar, paLingaraj, muBhubaneswar imwe cheteyo (Ekamra Van region), Khandagiri iri. Khandagiri iyi inzvimbo yevatsvene vakakwana, uye Ishe vachagara pano.

Kuzvarwa patsva kwaAchyutananda Dasji, Mutsvene Hadi Das, akanyora mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଦେଇ ଶୁଣ ସୁଜନେ କଳିଯୁଗ ବିଚାର,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ଜନମ ହେବେ ଶ୍ରୀ ଚକ୍ରଧର।
କ୍ଷୀର ଧାର ବହି ଯାଉଛି ସ୍ଥାନ ଅମରାବତୀ,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ସ୍ଥାନ ଗୁପତେ ସିଦ୍ଧ ସାଧୁ ଅଛନ୍ତି।
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ ଲାଗିଛି ରାତ୍ର ଦିନ ମଧ୍ୟରେ,
ଗୁପତେ ଖେଳ ସେ ଖେଳିବେ ପ୍ରଭୁ ସେହି ଠାବରେ।
ଝାଡଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଗୁମ୍ଫା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପ୍ରଭୁଙ୍କର ସେ ଠାବ,
ଝଲମଲ ଜ୍ୟୋତି ବାହାରେ ଲୀଳା ଚହଟ ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Karṇa dei shuṇa sujane kaḷiyuga bichāra,
Kaḷkī rūpare janama hebe Shrī Chakradhara.
Kṣīra dhāra bahi jāuchhi sthāna Amarābatī,
Khaṇḍagiri sthāna gupate siddha sādhu achhanti.
Gumphāre bhajana lāgichhi rātra dina madhyare,
gupate kheḷa se kheḷibe prabhu sehi ṭhābare.
Jhāḍakhaṇḍagiri gumphā madhyare prabhuṅkara se ṭhāba,
jhalamala jyoti bāhāre līḷā chahaṭa heba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKali Yuga, Ishe Maha Vishnu vachazvarwa Pasi vari mukuzvarwa patsva kwaKalki. Mitambo yavo yechinyakare ichatanga kuitika munzvimbo yerwizi rweKshira (Vaitarani), Amaravati, kureva Jajpur. Ipapo mitambo yavo ichaitika muKhandagiri uko vatsvene vakakwana vachange vakanyura mubhajani-kirtan masikati neusiku mumapako muchivande. Mahapurush akataura kuti Ishe vachagara uye vachaita mitambo yavo munzvimbo imwe cheteyo yeKhandagiri kana Jhadakhandagiri.

Mutsvene Hadi Das akanyora mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Kali Chautisha’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଘଟଣା ପ୍ରତିମା ପୁରୁଷ ଲିଙ୍ଗରାଜଙ୍କ ପୁର,
ଘୋର କଳି ମହାଭାରତ ହେବ ସେହି ଠାବର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ghaṭaṇā pratimā puruṣha Liṅgarājaṅka pura,
ghora kaḷi mahābhārata heba sehi ṭhābara.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush anonyora kuti "Nzvimbo Yezviitiko" ichava nzvimbo yeLingaraj kana Bhubaneswar, uye hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichaitika ikoko. Zvinonyatsozivikanwa kuti takamboona vhesi uko Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora – “Ghatana sthana je atai sehi, kalira bharata yuddha sethahin”. Achyutananda Dasji akanyora vhesi iyi nezveKhandagiri mumabhuku ake eMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ uye akataura kuti Khandagiri ndiyo “Ghatana Sthana,” uko hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichaitika.

Gare gare, Hadi Das akanyora – Bhubaneswar kana nzvimbo yeLingaraj ndiyo “Ghatana Sthana” uye hondo yeKali Mahabharata ichaitika ikoko.

Izvi zvinoreva kuti izvo Achyutananda Das ari kutaura nezveKhandagiri mu‘Chakada Madana’ ndizvo chaizvo izvo kuzvarwa kwake patsva, Hadi Das, ari kutaura nezvenzvimbo yeLingaraj (Bhubaneswar) mu‘Kali Chautisha’. Izvi zvinoratidza kuti nzvimbo dzese, kureva Khandagiri neBhubaneswar, dziri kunongedza kunzvimbo imwe chete. Muchidimbu, Khandagiri iri muBhubaneswar.

Cherechedza – Kusvika parizvino mumavhesi mazhinji ari pamusoro, tadzidza kuti Khandagiri muBhubaneswar inzvimbo yevatsvene vakakwana uye vatsvene. Vatsvene ivavo vakanyura mubhajani-kirtan yaIshe Kalki masikati neusiku mumapako eKhandagiri. Zvino tichadzidza kuti ndevapi vatsvene vakuru nevarume vechitendero vachange vachiita penance kuna Ishe Kalki muKhandagiri iyoyo uye kuti vatsvene vangani vese vachange vachiita bhajani-kirtan mumapako.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mu‘Aagata Bhavishya Prabandha Malika’ yake –

Odia Sloka:

ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରିକି ଯେ ଭୁବନେଶ୍ୱର କେତେକ ଦୂର,
ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅଛନ୍ତି ସାଧୁସନ୍ଥ ନିକର।
ନାରଦ ମାର୍କଣ୍ଡ ଶମ୍ଭୁ ଯେ ଅଗସ୍ତି ଉପଲସ୍ତି,
ଦୁର୍ବାସା କଶ୍ୟପ ଚ୍ୟବନ କୁମ୍ଭଋଷି ସନ୍ତତି।
ପରାଶର ବଶିଷ୍ଠାଦି ହୋ ବିଶ୍ୱାମିତ୍ରଙ୍କୁ ଘେନି,
ଷାଠିଏ ସହସ୍ର ଋଷି ହୋ ଗିରି ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣି।
ଦେବଋଷି ବ୍ରହ୍ମଋଷି ଯେ ସର୍ବେ ମେଳି ହୋଇଣ,
ଗୁମ୍ଫାରେ ଭଜନ କରନ୍ତି କଳ୍କୀ ନାମ ପୁଣ।
ଦେବତାମାନେ ଯେ ସେଠାରେ ରାତ୍ରେ ଗୋଷ୍ଠୀ କରନ୍ତି,
କଳ୍କୀର ଆଦ୍ୟ ମନ୍ତ୍ରକୁ ନିତ୍ୟେ ଜପୁଛନ୍ତି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Khaṇḍagiriki je Bhubaneshwara keteka dūra,
Khaṇḍagiri madhye achhanti sādhusantha nikara.
Nārada Mārkaṇḍa Shambhu je Agasti Upalasti,
Durbāsā Kashyapa Chyabana Kumbharuṣi santati.
Parāshara Bashiṣṭhādi ho Bishwāmitraṅku gheni,
ṣāṭhie sahasra ruṣi ho giri madhyare puṇi.
Debaruṣi Brahmaruṣi je sarbe meḷi hoiṇa,
gumphāre bhajana karanti Kaḷkī nāma puṇa.
Debatāmāne je seṭhāre rātre goṣṭhī karanti,
Kaḷkīra ādya mantraku nitye japuchhanti.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush akataura nhaurirano yaKrishna-Garuda mumabhuku ake eMalika. Garuda anoti nzvimbo yeKhandagiri haisi kure neBhubaneswar (iri pedyo chaizvo). Kune vatsvene vazhinji muKhandagiri. Ikoko, Narada, Markandeya, Shambhu, Agastya, Upalasti, Durvasa, Kashyapa, Chyavana, “Kumbharushi Santati” zvichireva Dridhasyu, Parashara, Va\-shish\-tha, Vishwamitra, nezvimwe, vese vari vatsvene gumi nevaviri uye kusanganisira vese vatsvene 60,000, Devarishis, neBrahmarishis vakanyura muzita-bhajani raIshe Kalki mumapako. Vamwari vanoita misangano mumapako iwayo usiku uye vanodana mantra yaIshe Kalki zuva nezuva.

Muvhesi rino, Garudaji haana kungotaura nezvevatsvene vari kuita penance mumapako eKhandagiri asi akataurawo kuti bako reKhandagiri harizovi kure neBhubaneswar, kana kuti, richava pedyo chaizvo.

Kubva pamavhesi gumi nemashanu ari pamusoro, zvinofanira kunge zvajeka kuvaverengi kuti maererano neBhavishya Malika, Khandagiri—uko Kalki achagara, uko vatsvene vakakwana vasingaverengeki vachange vachirarama, uko bhajani-kirtan yezita raKalki ichange ichienderera mberi mumapako, uko rwizi ruchayerera muchivande, uko hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichaitika—iri muBhubaneswar chete kana nzvimbo yeLingaraj kana nzvimbo yeEkamra Van, uye hapana kumwe.

10 Chinangwa Chekuzvarwa Patsva kwaKalki

Sezvatinoziva tese, Ishe vanotora kuzvarwa patsva kagumi Pasi pano kuti vasimbise kururama (Dharma). Ingave Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, kana Dvapara Yuga, chinangwa chekuzvarwa kwaIshe Pasi chinoramba chiri chimwe chete—kusimbiswa kweDharma.

MuShrimad Bhagavad Gita, Ishe Krishna vakati kuna Arjuna –

Ndima yeSanskrit:

परित्राणाय साधूनां विनाशाय च दुष्कृताम् ।
धर्मसंस्थापनार्थाय संभवामि युगे युगे ।।

Zvinoreva:
Kuti ndidzivirire vakarurama (vatsvene), kuti ndiparadze vakaipa, uye kuti ndisimbise kururama zvakasimba, ndinozviratidza nguva nenguva.

Mumabhuku eBhavishya Malika zvakare, zvinotaurwa kuti kuti aparadze vakaipa, adzivirire vatsvene nevarume vechitendero, uye asimbise Dharma zvakare, Ishe Maha Vishnu vachaburuka Pasi vari mukuzvarwa kwavo kwegumi uye kwekupedzisira, kuzvarwa patsva kwaKalki.

Nhaurirano pakati paIshe Krishna neshamwari Arjuna muDvapara Yuga yakataurwa makore anenge 600 apfuura muKali Yuga naMahapurush Achyutananda Das mumabhuku ake eMalika 'Chausathi Patala'. Mahapurush akanyora –

Odia Sloka:

ପାପ ଭାରା ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ସହି ନପାରି,
ମୋ ଆଗରେ କଲା ବେଗେ ଗୁହାରି।
କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ମୁହିଁ ହୋଇବି,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ବିନାଶିବି।
ବସି ନରପତି ହୃଦ ଦେଶରେ
ପ୍ରଜା ପାଳିବି ଉତ୍ତମ ଭାବରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pāpa bhārā pṛthwī sahi napāri,
mo āgare kalā bege guhāri.
Kaḷkī abatāra muhiñ hoibi,
duṣṭa prāṇīmānaṅku bināshibi.
Basi narapati hṛda deshare,
prajā pāḷibi uttama bhābare.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Pasi, risisakwanisi kutakura mutoro wezvivi pakupera kweKali Yuga, richandikumbira. Ipapo ndichatora kuzvarwa patsva kwaKalki uye ndichaparadza vakaipa ndinunure vatsvene. Ndichigara muIndia, ndichabata hutongi hwenyika yese uye ndichatonga vanhu nenzira yakarurama uye yakakodzera.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mune mamwe mabhuku eMalika –

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭୟଙ୍କର ଉତ୍ପାତ ହେବ ଧରିତ୍ରୀ ବକ୍ଷରେ ଜାଣ,
କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପରେ ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ସଂହାରି ଭାରା ଉଶ୍ୱାସିବେ ପୁଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mahābhayaṅkara utpāta heba dharitrī bakṣhare jāṇa,
Kaḷkī rūpare mlechha saṃhāri bhārā uśwāsibe puṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pachava nemhirizhonga inotyisa Pasi. Kalki pachake achaparadza vakaipa uye achabvisa mutoro wezvivi zvePasi.

Achitaura nhaurirano yaKrishna-Arjuna, Mahapurush anonyora zvakare mumabhuku ake eMalika –

Odia Sloka:

କଳି ଶେଷ ବେଳେ ମୁହିଁ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପ ହେବି,
ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିଣ ଦୁଷ୍ଟଜନମାନଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷi śeṣha beḷe muñhi Kaḷkī rūpa hebi,
santhaṅku rakhiṇa duṣhṭajanamānanku nāśhibi.

Zvinoreva:
Shri Krishna anoti pakupera kweKali Yuga, ndichatora kuzvarwa patsva kwaKalki. Ndichidzivirira vatsvene, vanhu vatsvene, nevanhu vane mwoyo yakachena, ndichaparadza vese vakaipa nevatadzi.

Achitaura mazwi aIshe zvakare, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumabhuku ake eMalika 'Chausathi Patala' –

Odia Sloka:

ବେଶୀ ପାପ ଯେତେବେଳେ ହେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରୂପେ ମୁଁ ଉଦୟ ହେବି,
ଓଡିଶା ମଧ୍ୟରେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ସ୍ଥାନେ ମନ ପରୀକ୍ଷା ଯାଇ କରିବି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Beśī pāpa jetebeḷe heba Kaḷkī rūpe mu udaya hebi,
Oḍiśā madhyare sthāne sthāne mana parīkṣhā jāi karibi.

Zvinoreva:
Ishe anoti muKali Yuga, kana chivi chosvika pamusoro, ipapo ndichamuka muchimiro changu cheKalki. Ndichayedza vanamati panzvimbo dzakasiyana-siyana muOdisha, ndisarudze pakati pechakanaka nechakaipa, uye ndichaita zvinoenderana nazvo.

Achitaura mashoko aIshe, mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Balaram Das, akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika 'Kali Aagata Bhavishyanta' –

Odia Sloka:

ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଯେ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ମାତିବେ,
ଖପର କରେ ଘେନିଥିବେ।
ରୋଧିର ବୃଷ୍ଟି ଯେ ହୋଇବ,
କେ ଅବା କାହିଁ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ଯାହାକୁ ରଖିବିଟି ଟିକିଏ ମୁହିଁ,
ସେ ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନେ ଥିବେ ରହି।
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଲୋକ ଯେ ନାଶ ହେବେ,
ସନ୍ଥ ଯେ ଅବଶ୍ୟ ରହିବେ।
ଅନେକ ଉତ୍ପାତ ଲାଗିବ,
ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଚହଳ ପଡିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chaṇḍī je Chāmuṇḍā mātibe,
khapara kare ghenithibe.
Rodhira bṛṣṭi je hoiba,
ke abā kāhiñ nāsha jiba.
Jāhāku rakhibiṭi ṭikie muhiñ,
se prāṇīmāne thibe rahi.
Duṣṭa loka je nāsha hebe,
santha je abashya rahibe.
Aneka utpāta lāgiba,
shūnyare chahaḷa paḍiba.

Zvinoreva:
Ishe anoti panguva yekusimbiswa kweDharma pakupera kweKali Yuga, Amai Ashta Chandis nevamwe Yoginis vachaparadza zvisingaenzaniswi. Kuchava nemvura yeropa ichanaya kubva kudenga. Kuchava nezvinetso zvakawanda uye njodzi dzichabva mukusina, kureva, kubva muchadenga. Vaya chete vandichadzivirira ndivo vachasara vakachengeteka. Vakaipa vachaparadzwa, uye vatsvene nevanhu vatsvene vachaponeswa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika 'Kali Kalpa Gita' –

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ ରୂପ ଧରି ହରି ଦେଶ ଭ୍ରମଣ କରିବେ,
କ୍ଷତ୍ରୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିକ୍ଷତ୍ରୀ କରିଣ ବସୁଧାକୁ ଉଦ୍ଧାରିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷkī Rāma rūpa dhari Hari desha bhramaṇa karibe,
kṣhatrīmānanku nikṣhatrī kariṇa basudhāku uddhāribe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKali Yuga, Ishe achatora chimiro chake cheKalki uye achafamba pasi rose uye achaponesa Amai Pasi nekuparadza vakaipa.

Mune 'Kali Kalpa Gita' pachayo, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora zvakare –

Odia Sloka:

ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନିଧନ ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ସଂହାରଣ,
କରିବେ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଦେବ କଳ୍କୀ ରାମ।
ଏମନ୍ତ ମହୀ ଯେ ହେବ ଉଶ୍ୱାସ,
କଳିକଳ୍ପ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଦାସ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mlechha nidhana duṣhṭa saṃharaṇa,
karibe niśhche deba Kaḷkī Rāma.
Emanta mahī je heba uśwāsa,
Kaḷikalpa kahe Achyuta dāsa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe Kalki achaponesa Pasi zvechokwadi nekuparadza Mlechhas (vasina tsika/vakaipa) uye nekuparadza vakaipa.

10.1 Kuuya kwaIshe Kalki

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mweya Mukuru Sishu Ananta Das, akanyora pamusoro penyaya iyi mumagwaro ake eMalika 'Chumbaka Malika':

Odia Sloka:

କଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର ଓଡ଼ିଶା ମଣ୍ଡଳେ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ଜନମ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ଜନ ନାଶି ଭକ୍ତଙ୍କୁ ଆଶ୍ୱାସି ଧର୍ମକୁ ତୁମ୍ଭେ ପାଳିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷkī abatāra Oḍiśā maṇḍaḷe tumbhe janama hoiba,
duṣhṭa jana nāśhi bhaktaṅku āśwāsi dharmaku tumbhe pāliba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe Mukuru Kalki achazvarirwa muOdisha. Mushure mezvo, Ishe achaparadza vakaipa uye achadzivirira vanamati kuti asimbise Dharma zvakare.

11 Zviito zveKuparadza: Kutanga uye Kuguma Kwakajeka?

Sezvatinoziva kubva kune zvinyorwa zveBhabishya Malika, munguva ino yekuchinja pakati peKali Yuga neSatya Yuga, zviitiko zvekuparadza zvisati zvamboitika zvichaitika pasi rose pasi pehutungamiriri hwaIshe Kalki, kuburikidza nazvo Mlecchas vese (vasina tsika), vatadzi, vakaipa, nevasina kururama vachaparadzwa. Bhabishya Malika inopawo vimbiso yekuti vanamati vese vachasara vakachengeteka panguva yezviitiko izvi uye havafaniri kutya.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Chhayalisha Patala’, anotaura mashoko aIshe Mukuru maIshe anopa ruzivo rwekupera kwenyika kuchauya (Khanda Pralaya).

Mahapurush anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

କାଳଭାବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟରେ,
ସୃଷ୍ଟି ନାଶ କରି ଲୀଳା କରେ ପାପ ଭାରେ।
ମାତ୍ର ମହାପ୍ରଳୟକୁ ରହିଥିବୁ ଟାକି,
ସେ କାଳରେ ଚରାଚର ନ ରହିବେ ବାକି।
ଚଣ୍ଡୀ ଚାମୁଣ୍ଡା ସହିତ ସଂହାରିଣୀ ଶକ୍ତି,
ଖଣ୍ଡା ଖପର ହାତରେ ବୁଲି ଦିବାରାତ୍ରି।
ରୋଗ ଶୋକ ଦୁଃଖ ଜାଲ ସଂସାରେ ପାତିବେ,
ପର ଆପଣାର ଭେଦ କିଛି ନ ରଖିବେ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ପବନ ଯେ ରୁଦ୍ର ମୂର୍ତ୍ତି ଧରି,
ଉପାଡ଼ିବ ସୁମେରୁ ସହିତ ଯେତେ ଗିରି।
ଚାରିମେଘ ଚାରିଦିଗେ ଆଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ କରିବେ,
ମହାଘୋର ବିପତ୍ତିକୁ ଆଣି ଘଟାଇବେ।
ଅନ୍ଧାର ହୋଇବ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ବଢ଼ିବ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ନକ୍ଷତ୍ର ଉଲକା ଛିଣ୍ଡିବ ନାହିଁ ଆଦି ଅନ୍ତ।
ଭୈରବୀ ରଡ଼ିବେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ କାନ୍ଦିବ ସକଳ,
ପାପମାତ୍ରା ପ୍ରବଳରେ ହେବ ଏହି ଫଳ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kālabhābe mahāshakti khaṇḍa praḷayare,
Sṛṣṭi nāsha kari līḷā kare pāpa bhāre.
Mātra mahāpraḷayaku rahithibu ṭāki,
Se kāḷare charāchara na rahibe bāki.
Chaṇḍī Chāmuṇḍā sahita saṃhāriṇī shakti,
Khaṇḍā khapara hātare buli dibārātri.
Roga shoka duḥkha jāla saṃsāre pātibe,
Para āpaṇāra bheda kichhi na rakhibe.
Aṇachāsha pabana je rudra mūrtti dhari,
Upāḍiba Sumeru sahita jete giri.
Chārimegha chāridige āchhanna karibe,
Mahāghora bipattiku āṇi ghaṭāibe.
Andhāra hoiba pṛthwī baḍhiba utpāta,
Nakṣatra ulakā chhiṇḍiba nāhiñ ādi anta.
Bhairabī raḍibe pṛthwī kāndiba sakaḷa,
Pāpamātrā prabaḷare heba ehi phaḷa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Simba Repamusoro muchimiro cheNguva (Kala) richaunza kupera kwenyika kwediki uye richaparadza nyika. Panguva yekuparadzwa, zvipenyu zvese zvichakanganiswa. Chandi-Chamunda nemasimba anoparadza vachafamba masikati neusiku vakabata mapfumo (Khanda) nemisoro (Khapar). Marwadzo, kutambura, zvirwere, uye kusuwa zvichapararira pasi rose. Misiyano yese pakati pe'wako' ne'vatorwa' ichanyangarika. Mhepo makumi mana nepfumbamwe, dzichitora chimiro chakaipa chaRudra, dzichadzura makomo ese kusanganisira Sumeru huru. Makore mana, kureva Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, naDrona, achakomba mativi mana uye achagadzira njodzi dzinotyisa. Rima guru richafukidza Pasi; zuva nemwedzi hazvizobudi. Panguva iyoyo, meteors ichadonha kubva kudenga uye ichava chikonzero chekuparadzwa. Murima iroro, Amai Bhairavi vachadzvova zvine simba uye vachaparadza vatadzi vasingaverengeki.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, achitaura mashoko aIshe, anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Agata Bhabishyata Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

କାହିଁ ଜଳ ରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ଜଗତ ନାଶିବି,
କାହିଁ ପବନ ରୂପରେ ସକଳ ଗ୍ରାସିବି।
କାହିଁ ଯୋଗମାୟା ନେଇ ସଂହାର କରିବି,
କାହିଁ ନିଜେ ବିଶ୍ଵରୂପ ଧାରଣ କରିବି।
କାହିଁ ମଉନ ବ୍ରତ ମୁଁ ଧାରଣ କରିବି,
କାହିଁ କାଳରୂପେ ମୁହିଁ ପାପୀଙ୍କୁ ନାଶିବି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kāhiñ jala rūpe muhiñ jagata nāshibi,
kāhiñ pabana rūpare sakaḷa grāsibi.
kāhiñ yogamāyā nei saṃhāra karibi,
kāhiñ nije bishwarūpa dhāraṇa karibi.
kāhiñ mauna brata muñ dhāraṇa karibi,
kāhiñ kāḷarūpe muhiñ pāpīṅku nāshibi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe Kalki achaunza kuparadzwa kukuru munyika ino muzvimiro zvake zvakasiyana-siyana. Mune dzimwe nzvimbo, achatora chimiro chemvura uye achanyudza zvose; mune dzimwe, achapeperutsa zvose mukuparadzwa kwemhepo. Mune dzimwe nguva, achaunza kuparadzwa achichengeta mhiko yekunyarara, uye mune dzimwe, achauraya vakaipa muchimiro cheNguva. Mune dzimwe nzvimbo, achatora Chimiro chake Chepasi Pose, uye mune dzimwe, achaunza kuparadzwa kuburikidza nesimba rake reYogamaya.

Zvinoenderana neBhabishya Malika, Ishe Kalki achaunza kuparadzwa uku kuburikidza nemafashamo emvura anotyisa, kudengenyeka kwenyika kukuru, madutu emhepo anoparadza, mafashamo emoto, njodzi dzemuchadenga, denda dzine ngozi, nehondo yenyukireya yepasi rose.

11.1 Kudengenyeka kwenyika

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ହିମାଳୟ ଏଭରେଷ୍ଟ ଟଳି ଯେ ପଡିବ,
ଯେବେ ଏହି ଧରା ପରେ ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Himālaya Everest ṭaḷi je paḍiba,
Jebe ehi dharā pare bhūmikampa heba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva yezviito zvekuparadza zveKalki pakupera kweKali Yuga, kudengenyeka kwenyika kwakasimba kudaro kuchawira Pasi zvekuti kunyange 'Himalayas' nemisoro yayo se'Everest' zvichaparadzwa. Vanamati vachasara vakachengeteka, asi vatadzi vachaparadzwa.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora zvakare mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପେ ପ୍ରକମ୍ପିତ ହୋଇବ ଧରଣୀ,
ମାଟି ତଳେ ଲୁଚିଯିବ କୋଠାବାଡି ପୁଣି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhūmikampe prakampita hoiba dharaṇī,
māṭi taḷe luchijiba koṭhābāḍi puṇi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Pasi rose richadedera nekudengenyeka kwenyika, uye nekuda kwekudengenyeka kwenyika ikoko, zvivakwa zvikuru zvichavigwa pasi pevhu.

Mahapurush akanyora mune mamwe magwaro ake eMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ପାତାଳେ ବାସୁକୀ ଟେକିବ ମୁଣ୍ଡ,
ତିନିଥର ଯେ କମ୍ପିବ ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pātāḷe Bāsukī ṭekiba muṇḍa,
tini thara je kampiba brahmāṇḍa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nekuda kwemutoro wakawandisa wezvivi pakupera kweKali Yuga, nyoka Vasuki, inobata Pasi pamusoro payo, ichazunza musoro wayo kakawanda. Nekuda kweizvi, kudengenyeka kwenyika kukuru kutatu kuchaitika paPasi rino.

Mutsvene mukuru akazvarirwa muOdisha muzana ramakore rechi20, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭୂମିକମ୍ପ ହେବ ଅପାର,
ନାଶ ହୋଇବେ ନାରୀ ନର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhūmikampa heba apāra,
nāśha hoibe nārī nara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, muchikamu chekupedzisira cheKali Yuga, kuchava nekudengenyeka kwenyika kusingaverengeki makawanda varume nevakadzi vasingaverengeki vachaparara.

11.2 Mafashamo Emvura (Jala Pralaya)

Achikurukura nezvekunyanya kutyisa kwemafashamo emvura, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଭାରତକୁ ଛାଡି ଦେଲେ ସବୁ ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଯିବ, ମହାସାଗର ଜଳରେ ଡୁବି ମରୁଥିବ।
ଧରଣୀ ଦେହରେ ଜଳ ମାରିବ ଲହରୀ, ଜଳାର୍ଣ୍ଣବ ହୋଇଯିବ ଚଉଦିଗ ଭାରି।
ଅଛଇ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ଉତ୍ତର ମେରୁରେ, ତରଳିବ ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କ ରଶ୍ମିରେ।
ରୌଦ୍ରତାପେ ଯେତେବେଳେ ମେଦିନୀ କମ୍ପିବ, ସେତିକିବେଳେ ବରଫଗଡ଼ ତରଳିବ।
ବହିବ ପାଣିର ସୁଅ ସମୁଦ୍ରକୁ ପୁଣ, ଆଟଲାଣ୍ଟିକ ବଢ଼ିବ କାହିଁ କେତେ ଜାଣ।
ସମୁଦ୍ରର ପାଣି ଧୀରେ ବଢ଼ି ବଢ଼ି ଯିବ, ସାଗର ମାତିବ ଶେଷେ ଆଉ ଯାହା ଥିବ।
ଅନାଉ ଅନାଉ କାହିଁ ଜଳ ମାଡିଯିବ, ମାଟିର ଲକ୍ଷେ ଘନ ଫୁଟ ଉପରେ ରହିବ।
ଭୂଗୋଳର ସ୍ଥିତି ବଡ଼ ବିଚିତ୍ର ତୁ ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ରହିଛି ସର୍ବ ଉର୍ଦ୍ଧ୍ଵରେ ତ ପୁଣ।
ସୃଷ୍ଟିର କର୍ତ୍ତା ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତପତି ଜାଣ, ଭାରତ ଭୂମିରେ କରିବେ ବୈକୁଣ୍ଠ ସ୍ଥାପନ।
ପାଣିର ଘେରରେ ଆଉ ସବୁ ମହାଦେଶ, ଧୀରେ ଜାଣ ବୁଡିଯିବ ସେ ମହାବିନାଶ।
ଦେଖିବ ଏ ଦୁଇ ଆଖି ସମୟ ଆସିଛି, ସାମନା କରି ପାରିଲେ ସମୟକୁ ଇଛି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bhāratku chāḍidele sabu rāṣṭra jiba, Mahāsāgara jalare ḍubi maruthiba.
Dharaṇī dehare jala māriba laharī, Jalārṇaba hoijiba chaudiga bhāri.
Achai baraphagaḍa uttara merure, taraḷiba dhīre dhīre sūryāṅka raśmire.
Raudratāpe jetebeḷe medinī kampiba, setikibeḷe baraphagaḍa taraḷiba.
Bahiba pāṇira sua samudruku puṇa, Āṭlāṇṭika baḍhiba kāhiñ kete jāṇa.
Samudrara pāṇi dhīre baḍhi baḍhi jiba, sāgara mātiba śeṣe āu jāhā thiba.
Anāu anāu kāhiñ jala māḍijiba, māṭira lakṣhe ghana phuṭa upare rahiba.
Bhūgoḷara sthiti baḍa bichitra tu jāṇa, Bhārata rahichi sarba urdhware ta puṇa.
Sruṣṭira kartā prabhu jagatpati jāṇa, Bhārata bhūmire karibe Baikuṇṭha sthāpana.
Pāṇira gherare āu sabu mahādeśa, dhīre jāṇa buḍijiba se mahābināśa.
Dekhiba e dui ākhi samaya āsichi, sāmnā karipārile samayaku ichhi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kunze kweIndia, nyika dzese dzichanyudzwa mumvura yegungwa. Kuchava nemafashamo emvura paPasi, uye mativi ese mana achanyudzwa. Nekuda kwekuwedzera kwekupisa kwezuva, chando chiri kuNorth Pole chichatanga kunyungudika, izvo zvichanyanya kukanganisa Atlantic Ocean. Chiyero chemvura mumakungwa chicharamba chichikwira, zvichikonzera kuti maguta ari pamhenderekedzo agare pasi pemvura. Mune dzimwe nzvimbo, masaisai etsunami akakwirira semakomo achamuka. Nzvimbo yese ichachinja. Asi Ishe Mukuru achadzivirira India, uye kukanganisa kwemafashamo emvura aya kuchava kushoma muIndia. Kunze kweIndia, makondinendi ese achawira pasi pemafashamo emvura.

11.3 Mafashamo Emhepo (Pavan Pralaya)

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das, anonyora mumagwaro ake eMalika ‘Patta Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଧର୍ମହୀନ ହେବେ ସକଳ ଏ ଜନ ଧର୍ମାଧର୍ମ ନ ରହିବ,
ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ବହିବ ଏମନ୍ତ ପୃଥିବୀ ଟଳଟଳ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dharmahīna hebe sakaḷa e jana dharmādharma na rahiba,
aṇachāśha bāyu bahiba emanta pruthibī ṭaḷaṭaḷa dhruba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKali Yuga, munhu wese achava asina kururama; hapana achava nechitendero/basa rasara. Ichi chichava chikonzero chekuti Ishe Mukuru Kalki asimbise Dharma. Mhepo makumi mana nepfumbamwe dzichavhuvhuta nenzira yekuti Pasi richadedera.

Mahapurush anonyora mune mamwe magwaro ake eMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ପବନ ବହି ଅଣଚାଶ,
କ୍ଷିତିକି କରିଦେବ ଧ୍ଵଂସ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pabana bahi aṇachāśha,
kṣhitiki karideba dhwaṃsa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva yemutambo unoparadza waIshe Kalki, mhepo makumi mana nepfumbamwe dzichavhuvhuta uye dzichaparadza Pasi.

Mutsvene bofu akazvarirwa muOdisha muzana ramakore rechi19, Bhima Bhoi, akanyorawo pamusoro penyaya iyi mumagwaro ake eMalika. Mahapurush anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପବନ ବହିବ ପର୍ବତ ଦୋହଲିଯିବ,
ଚାରିମେଘ ମିଳି ବର୍ଷିବେ ଚାରିଦିଗ ଅନ୍ଧାର ଘୋଟିବ।
ମୋ ଭାଇ, ଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ସୂର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦେଖାଯିବ ନାହିଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Aṇachāśha khaṇḍa pabana bahiba parbata dohalijiba,
chārimegha miḷi barṣhibe chāridiga andhāra ghoṭiba.
Mo bhāi, chandra sūrya dekhājiba nāhiñ.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKali Yuga, nguva ichasvika apo mhepo makumi mana nepfumbamwe dzichavhuvhuta uye kunyange makomo nemakomo madiki zvichadedera. Makore mana anonzi Avarta, Samvarta, Pushkara, naDrona achanaya pamwe chete. Zuva nemwedzi hazvizoonekwi mudenga, uye rima guru richafukidza mativi ese mana.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ଟଳଟଳ ହେଉଥିବ ସପ୍ତଦ୍ଵୀପ ମହୀ,
ସେ ବେଳେ ଥଳକୂଳ ରହିବଟି ନାହିଁ।
ଅଣଚାଶ ଖଣ୍ଡ ହୋଇ ପବନ ବହିବ,
ସରକାର ବୁଦ୍ଧିବିଦ୍ୟା ଉଭେଇ ଯିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ṭaḷaṭaḷa heuthibā saptadwīpa mahī,
se beḷe thaḷakūḷa rahibāṭi nāhiñ.
aṇachāśha khaṇଡa hoi pabana bahiba,
sarakāra buddhibidyā ubhei jiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva yekusimbiswa kweDharma pakupera kweKali Yuga, kuchava nemhirizhonga pasi rose kusanganisira makondinendi manomwe. Dzichivhuvhuta nehasha dzakanyanya, mhepo makumi mana nepfumbamwe dzichaparadza zvivakwa zvese. Panguva iyoyo, hapana achava akachengeteka. Simba rehurumende nehungwaru hwayo hazvizobatsiri. Vaya chete vanotevera nzira yeDharma uye vakazvipira zvakakwana kuna Ishe Mukuru ndivo vachawana dziviriro.

Odia Sloka:

କର ପତ୍ର ଯୋଡି କହଇ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଗୁରୁପାଦେ ଶିର ଦେଇ,
ପ୍ରଳୟ କାଳର ବାରଣ କହିବା କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ ଯହିଁ।
ଶୁଣରେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଏମନ୍ତ କାଳ ଯେ ଦିନେ ଆସି ପହଞ୍ଚିବ,
ଦଶ ଗ୍ରାମ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଜଣେକ ରହିବେ ଦେବତାଙ୍କ ଆବିର୍ଭାବ।
ସେ କାଳେ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ଅଣଚାଶ ବାୟୁ ଭାଦ୍ରବ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣିମା ଦିନ,
ଏମନ୍ତ ପବନ ବହିବ ବାରଙ୍ଗ ସକଳ ହୋଇବ ଲୀନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kara patra joḍi kahai Bāraṅga gurupāde śira dei,
praḷaya kāḷara bāraṇa kahibā kaḷi śeṣha heba jahiñ.
Śuṇare Bāraṅga emanta kāḷa je dine āsi pahañchiba,
daśha grāma madhye jaṇeka rahibe debatāṅka ābirbhāba.
Se kāḷe Bāraṅga aṇachāśha bāyu Bhādraba pūrṇimā dina,
emanta pabana bahiba Bāraṅga sakaḷa hoiba līna.

Zvinoreva:
Paakabvunzwa nemudzidzi Baranga nezvemutambo unoparadza waKalki unoitika pakupera kweKali Yuga, Mahapurush Sishu Ananta Das akanyora mumagwaro ake eMalika kuti vanhu vachafanira kuona zuva apo munhu mumwe chete, semwari, achapona pakati pemisha gumi. Panguva iyoyo, mhepo makumi mana nepfumbamwe dzichavhuvhuta nekumhanya kukuru uye dzichaparadza zvivakwa zvese. Iro richava zuva remwedzi wakazara wemwedzi waBhadra.

11.4 Mafashamo Emoto (Agni Pralaya)

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika rinonzi 'Gupta Kheda Malika':

Odia Sloka:

ଧନ, ସମ୍ପତ୍ତି ଓ ଟଙ୍କା ଯାହା ଅଛି ଜମା,
ସବୁଯିବ ପୋଡି ଭାଇ ନିଆଁର ଗଞ୍ଜମା।
ଚଉଦିଗେ ନିଆଁ କାହୁଁ ଦଗ୍ଧି ଏ ଧରଣୀ,
ଓଦା କଞ୍ଚା ନ ମାନିବ ସବୁ ଖାଏ ଅଗ୍ନି।
ଅଗ୍ନିର କରାଳ ରୂପ ଜୀବାତ୍ମା ଦେଖିବ,
ହାଁ ହାଁ ହୋଇ ତାର ପ୍ରାଣ ଛାଡିଯିବ।
ସମଗ୍ର ବିଶ୍ଵରେ ଧ୍ଵଂସ ଲୀଳୋ ଯେ ଚାଲିବ,
କେଉଁଠି କମ୍ ବେଶୀ ଜାଣେ ତା ଦଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dhana, sampatti o ṭaṅkā jāhā achi jamā,
sabujiba poḍi bhāi niāñra gañjamā.
Chaudige niāñ kāhuñ dagdhi e dharaṇī,
odā kañchā na māniba sabu khāe agni.
Agnira karāḷarūpa jībātmā dekhiba,
hāñ hāñ hoi tāra prāṇa chāḍijiba.
Samagra biśware dhwaṃsa līḷā je chāliba,
keuṇṭhi kam beśī jāne tā daiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mutambo wemoto uchaonekwa munzira dzose ina. Pasi richatsva. Vanhu vazhinji vachafa. Pfuma, midziyo, nemari zvichatsva kuita dota. Zviito izvi zvinoparadza zvichapararira pasi rose, zvichioneka zvishoma kune dzimwe nzvimbo uye zvakawanda kune dzimwe.

11.5 Njodzi Yemuchadenga/Mumhepo

Odia Sloka:

ନାନା ଉତ୍ପାତ ଯାକ ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷେ ହେବ,
ଭକ୍ତ ବିନା ଅନ୍ୟ କେହି ଜାଣି ନ ପାରିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

nānā utpāta jāka antarikhye heba,
bhakta binā anya kehi jāni na pāriba.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika 'Chakada Madana' kuti munguva yekupedzisira yeKali Yuga, apo kusimbiswa kweDharma kuri kuenderera mberi, pachava nekukanganiswa kwakawanda muchadenga. Pachava nekuchinja kwakawanda mumapuraneti nemumapoka enyeredzi, pamwe chete nenzvimbo dzavo nemhanya dzavo. Mahapurush akanyora kuti kunze kwevanamati, vanhuwo zvavo havazokwanisi kuziva nezvezvinhu izvi.

Mahapurush akanyorazve pamusoro penyaya iyi mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’:

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷବେଳେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀ ଉତ୍ପାତ,
ରଣ ଗୋଳ ସେ କାଳେ ହୋଇବ ବହୁତ।
ଚାରିଦିଗମାନଙ୍କରେ ଲାଞ୍ଜାମାନ ପଡି,
ସେ କାଳେ ବହୁତ ଶବ୍ଦ ହେବ ଘଡ଼ଘଡ଼ି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷiyuga śheṣhabeḷe pruthbī utpāta,
raṇa goḷa se kāḷe hoiba bahuta.
chāridigamānaṅkare lāñjāmāna paḍi,
se kāḷe bahuta śhabda heba ghaḍaghaḍi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, muchikamu chekupedzisira cheKali Yuga, pachava nekukanganiswa kwakawanda nehondo dzakaomarara Pasi. Pachava nemeteor/asteroids anowira munzira dzose ina uye manzwi achabva mukusina chinhu.

11.6 Chirwere neDenda

MaMahapurush akanyora mumabhuku akawanda eMalika kuti pakupera kweKali Yuga, zvirwere zvakawanda nemadenda zvichamanikidza vanhu kukotama. Malika yakataura zvakajeka kuti vanachiremba vachakundikana mukuongorora nekurapa zvirwere nemadenda iwayo, uye mishonga yavanopa icharatidza kusashanda.

MaMahapurush vakataurawo kuti muchokwadi, zvirwere nemadenda aya zvichaunzwa nemasimba-amai akadai saYogamaya, Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, Dakinis, Pisachinis, nezvimwe. Masimba-amai aya achafamba pasi rose mumhando dzisingaonekwe sezvirwere izvi uye opinda mumiviri yevanhu.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Bigyanakalpa’:

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁର୍ଭିକ୍ଷ ପଡିବ ଲୋକେ ଯିବେ ବହୁ ମରି,
ଚଉଷଠି ରୋଗ ଭ୍ରମୁଥିବେ ଫେରି ଫେରି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Durbhikṣha paḍiba loke jibe bahu mari,
chauṣhaṭhi roga bhramuthibe pheri pheri.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva yekusimbisa Dharma pakupera kweKali Yuga, pachava nenzara nekusanaya kwemvura, zvichikonzera kufa kwevanhu vazhinji. Marudzi makumi matanhatu nemana ezvirwere achapararira pasi rose.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyorazve mubhuku reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ବାଡି ବେମାରୀରେ ଅନେକ ମରିବେ କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Bāḍi bemārīre aneka maribe kahe dīna Achyuta.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKali Yuga, zvirwere zvakawanda zvichava chikonzero cherufu kuvanhu vasingaverengeki.

Mahapurush akanyorazve mubhuku rake ‘Jaiphula Malika’ ne ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଚିହ୍ନା ରୋଗ ଘୋଟିବ,
ବଇଦର ବୁଦ୍ଧି ବଣା ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୌଷଧି କାମ ନ କରିବ।
ଅଚିହ୍ନା ବ୍ୟାଧି ଯେ ଜଗତ୍ରେ ଘୋଟିବ ବଇଦ ବଣା ହୋଇବେ,
ରୋଗ ନ ଚିହ୍ନିଣ ବଣା ହେଉଥିବେ ଅନେକ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ମରିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Achinhā roga ghoṭiba,
baidara buddhi baṇā hoiba lo jāiphula,
mahauṣadhi kāma na kariba.
Achinhā byādhi je jagatre ghoṭiba baida baṇā hoibe,
roga na chinhiṇa baṇā heuthibe aneka prāṇī maribe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKali Yuga, zvirwere zvisingazivikanwe zvakadaro zvichauya zvekuti huchenjeri hwevanachiremba hucharega kushanda. Zvirwere izvi hazvizivikanwe, uye mishonga haizoshandi pakurapa kwazvo, nekuda kweizvi vanhu vasingaverengeki vachafa.

Mahapurush anonyora mune rimwe remabhuku ake eMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ରୋଗ ରୂପେ ଯୋଗମାୟା ଶରୀରେ ଖେଳିବ,
କାହାର ଆୟତ ପୁଣି ସେଠାରେ ନ ଥିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Roga rūpe Yogamāyā sharīre kheḷiba,
kāhāra āyata puṇi seṭhāre na thiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakupera kweKali Yuga, Amai Yogamaya vachapinda mumiviri yevanhu muchimiro chechirwere.

Mahapurush anoenderera mberi achinyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଗଦ ମହୌଷଧି କରିବନି କାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ମାଡି ଆସିବଟି ରୋଗ,
ସେ କାଳେ ଜାଣିବୁ ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନେ ପୃଥ୍ବୀକୁ ପ୍ରଳୟ ଯୋଗ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Gada mahauṣhadhi karibani kārjya māḍi āsibaṭi roga,
Se kāḷe jānibu alpa dine pruthbīku praḷaya yoga.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kana madenda ezvirwere akadaro auya zvekuti mishonga inoratidza kusashanda pakurapa kwazvo, ipapo zvinofanira kunzwisiswa kuti nguva yekuparadzwa kwePasi yava pedyo.

Mahapurush anonyorazve:

Odia Sloka:

ବ୍ୟାଧି ହୋଇ ପିଶାଚୀ ଆସିବେ,
ପ୍ରାଣୀମାନଙ୍କୁ ଗୁପ୍ତେ ନାଶିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Byādhi hoi piśhāchī āsibe,
prāṇīmānanku gupte nāśhibe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, masimba-amai achapinda muchivande mumiviri muchimiro chechirwere uye aparadze vanhu vazhinji. Asi pamwe chete neizvi, Mahapurush akataurawo mubhuku rake reMalika nzira yekuti vanhu vapone kubva kuzvirwere nemadenda aya.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Chausathi Patala’:

Odia Sloka:

ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମାରେ ଯେଉଁ ପ୍ରାଣୀ ରହିବେ,
ରୋଗ କବଳରେ କେବେହିଁ ସେମାନେ ନ ପଡିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Satya śhānti dayā kṣhamāre jeuñ prāṇī rahibe,
roga kabaḷare kebehiñ semāne na paḍibe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, vanhu avo vanotora hunhu hwakanaka hwakadai sechokwadi, rugare, tsitsi, nekukanganwira mumaitiro avo vachagara vakasununguka kubva kuzvirwere izvi.

11.7 Hondo Yenyika

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Jaiphula Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଲାଗିବ ମହାସମର,
ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ଅତି ପ୍ରବଳ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ପୃଥିବୀ ହୋଇଯିବ ନାରଖାର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Lāgiba mahāsamara,
deśha deśha madhye ati prabala lo jāiphula,
pruthibī hoijiba nārakhāra.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo huru ichaitika Pasi. Pachava nehondo yakaomarara pakati penyika, zvichikonzera kuparadzwa kwePasi.

Achyutananda Das ji akanyorazve mu‘Jaiphula Malika’ pachayo:

Odia Sloka:

ଦେଶ ଦେଶ ମଧ୍ୟେ ପୁଣ,
ଭୟଙ୍କର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ ପ୍ରମାଣ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ଏହା କଳି ଶେଷ ବୋଲି ଜାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Deśha deśha madhye puṇa,
bhayaṅkara juddha heba pramāṇa lo jāiphula,
ehā kaḷi śeṣha boli jāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, muchikamu chekupedzisira cheKali Yuga, hondo dzakaomarara kwazvo dzichaputika pakati penyika dzakasiyana-siyana. Mune mamwe mazwi, kana hondo inotyisa yatanga pakati penyika, zvinofanira kunzwisiswa sekuguma kweKali Yuga.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake reMalika ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଅଣୁ ପରମାଣୁ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରି ଭୟଙ୍କର,
ଗୋଟିଏ ଅଣୁ ବୋମାର କରାମତି ସାର।
ଲକ୍ଷ ଲକ୍ଷ ଅଣୁବୋମା ଗର୍ଜିବ ମହୀରେ,
କାନ୍ଦିବ ଧରଣୀ ରାଣୀ ହା ହୁତାଶରେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Aṇu paramāṇu juddha bhāri bhayaṅkara,
goṭie aṇu bomāra karāmati sāra.
lakṣha lakṣha aṇubomā garjiba mahīre,
kāndiba dharaṇī rāṇī hā hutāśhare.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yeatomu-nyukireya ichaitika Pasi iyo ichave inotyisa kwazvo. Pasi rose richazununguka nekushandiswa kwemabhomba enyukireya asingaverengeki. Amai Pasi vachachema.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anotaura kuti munguva ino iripo, hondo yakaomarara ichaitika pakati peIndia nePakistan. Zvinoenderana neMahapurush, hondo iyi pakati peIndia nePakistan ichazochinja kuva hondo yenyika nekuti nyika dzakawanda dzichatsigira Pakistan muhondo iyi, ukuwo dzimwe nyika dzicharwira India. Muhondo iyi, nyika huru dzenyika dzichakamurwa kuita mapoka maviri.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubhuku rake ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚୀନ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ ପୁଣି ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ଋଷିଆ ଭାରତ ପୁଣି ଜର୍ମାନ ଜାପାନ।
ଭାରତ ସପକ୍ଷ ହେବେ ମହାଶକ୍ତି ଜାଣ,
ଇରାକ ଯେ ସମର୍ଥନ ଦେବ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chīna Pākisthāna puṇi Āmerikā jāṇa,
Ruṣhiā Bhārata puṇi Jarmāna Jāpāna.
Bhārata sapakṣha hebe mahāśhakti jāṇa,
Irāka je samarthana deba Pākisthāna.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, muhondo iyi, nyika dzakadai seChina, America, neIraq dzichatsigira Pakistan, ukuwo masimba akadai seRussia, Germany, neJapan acharwira India.

Mahapurush anonyora mubhuku reMalika rine zita rekuti ‘Bhabishyat Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ଭାରତ ସଙ୍ଗେ ପାକିସ୍ଥାନଟି ରଙ୍ଗେ,
ମହାସମର କରିବ ଜାଣିଥା ବେଗେ।
ପୁଣି ଚୀନ୍ ସଇନୁ ତୁର୍କୀ ଇରାନ ଜାଣ,
ବ୍ରିଟିଶ ଯେ ସଇନ୍ୟ ସବେ ମିଳିଣ।
ପାକସ୍ଥିାନକୁ ବଳ ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ସର୍ବେ ଆବର,
ପାକିସ୍ଥାନକୁ ଦେବେ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଲାଗିବ ଘୋର।
ଏହା ଦେଖି ରୁଷିଆ ଆସି ହୋଇବ ଠିଆ,
ଭାରତ ସମରକ୍ଷେତ୍ରେ ସେ ଏକୁଟିଆ।
ଅସ୍ତ୍ର ଶସ୍ତ୍ର ସଇନ୍ୟ ଯୋଗାଇ ଦେବ ଜାଣ,
ଭାରତ ସହିତ ମିଶି କରିବ ରଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ehi Bhārata saṅge Pākisthānṭi raṅge,
mahāsamara kariba jāṇithā bege.
Puṇi Chīna sainu Turkī Irāna jāṇa,
Briṭisha je sainya sabe miḷiṇa.
Pākisthānku baḷa astra sarbe ābara,
Pākisthānku debe juddha lāgiba ghora.
Ehā dekhi Ruṣiā āsi hoiba ṭhiā,
Bhārata samarakṣetre se ekuṭiā.
Astra shastra sainya jogāi deba jāṇa,
Bhārata sahita mishi kariba raṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush akanyora kuti nyika inonzi Pakistan ichapinda muhondo yakaomarara neIndia. Muhondo iyi, nyika dzakadai seChina, Turkey, Iran, England, neAmerica dzichabatsira Pakistan uye dzichapa rutsigiro rwemauto. Kuona zvese izvi, shamwari yakanakisisa yeIndia, Russia, ichauya kutanga uye yega kuzobatsira India. Ichaipa India marudzi ese ezvombo, kubatana kweupfumi, nerutsigiro rwemauto uye icharwa hondo yakaomarara kurwisa nyika dzehondo ichimiririra India.

Mahapurush akanyora kuti kwete Russia chete, asiwo Japan neGermany zvicharwira India muhondo iyi. Mu‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ pachayo, anonyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ଜାପାନର ରାଗ ଅଛି ହିରୋଶିମା କଥା,
ପକାଇଥିଲା ବୋମା ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଜାଣିଥା।
ସେହି କ୍ରୋଧ ଲିଭିନାହିଁ ଜାପାନର ଜାଣ,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଭାରତ ପଟ ନେବେ ସେହି ପୁଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jāpānara rāga achi Hirośhimā kathā,
pakāithilā bomā juddhe jāṇithā.
Sehi krodha libhināhiñ Jāpānara jāṇa,
juddhe Bhārata paṭa nebe sehi puṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Japan haizokwanisi kukanganwa kudonhedzwa kwebhomba reatomu pairi neAmerica. Hasha dzeJapan kuAmerica hadzizodzikamiswi. Murazvo wekutsiva ucharamba uchipfuta mumoyo mayo, nekuda kweizvi Japan icharwa neAmerica kurutivi rweIndia panguva yehondo yenyika yechitatu. Pakukumbira kweJapan, Germany icharwirawo India.

12 Hondo Yakasara yeMahabharata muKali Yuga

VaPanchasakha vakanyora mumabhuku eBhabishya Malika kuti Hondo Yenyika inoitika pakupera kweKali Yuga ichave chaizvo hondo yakasara yeMahabharata. MaMahapurush vakajekesa kuti hondo yemazuva 18 yeMahabharata pakupera kweDwapara Yuga yakanga isina kupera zvizere nekuti, panguva iyoyo, Ishe Krishna vakasarudza kusaita chikamu chekupedzisira chehondo ipapo, asi kuipedzisa pakupera kweKali Yuga.

Kwete izvi chete, Vamahapurusha vakanyora zvakadzama nezvehondo iyi mumabhuku avo eMalika uye vakaratidza zvinhu zvakawanda, zvakadai sekuti ichaitika riini, vanhu vangani vachafa panguva yayo, ichagara kwenguva yakareba sei, ichatora chimiro chipi, ichaitika pasi pehutungamiri hwaani, ndiani achatora chikamu kubva kudivi reIndia, ichapera kupi, uye chii chichaitika mushure mehondo iyi, nezvimwe.

12.1 Hondo iyi ichaitika riini?

Odia Sloka:

ଯୁଗଶେଷ କଳି ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଯମ ମାଆ ସାତ ଭଉଣୀ ଧରିଥିବେ ଖପର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Yugaśheṣha kaḷi Bhārata juddha hoiba ghora,
Yama māā sāta bhauṇī dharithibe khapara.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichapedzwa pakupera kweKali Yuga. Muhondo iyi, "Yama Maa Sata Bhauni" kana kuti Vanamwari Vanomwe Vanaamai (Saptamatruka) vari muBiraja Khetra (Chamunda, Varahi, Indrani, Vaishnavi, Brahmani, Kaumari, Maheshwari, naNarasimhi) vachaparadza vatadzi.

Achitsanangura kuti hondo iyi ichaitika riini pakupera kweKali Yuga, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଆସି ଦେଉଥିବ ଘାଣ୍ଟି ଭାରତ ଲଢ଼ାଇ ହେବ,
ଦଶ ଅଣା ଯିବ ଛଅ ଅଣା ରହିବ ମୋ ବଚନ ଅଟେ ଧ୍ରୁବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna Shani āsi deuthibā ghāṇṭi Bhārata laḍhāi heba,
Dasha aṇā jiba chha-a aṇā rahiba mo bachana aṭe dhruba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, kurwa kwasara kweIndia (Mahabharata) kuchatanga panguva yekubatana kweMeena-Shani (Pisces-Saturn), muchiitiko icho vanhu vasingaverengeki vachafa.

12.2 Hondo iyi ichaitika muchimiro chipi uye icharwirwa kupi padanho rekupedzisira?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mubuku rake reMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ମହାଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଓଳିଏ ବାକି ଅଛି ଜାଣ,
କଳିଯୁଗରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇ ପୂରଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mahābhārata juddha oḷie bāki achi jāṇa,
Kaḷiyugare biśhwajuddha hoi pūraṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, ziva kuti chikamu chehondo yeMahabharata chakasara, iyo ichapedzwa sehondo yepasi rose muKali Yuga.

Mahapurush anowedzera kunyora mu‘Jaiphula Malika’ yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ,
ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ପୁଣି ହୋଇବ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
କହେ ଯବନ ବାହି ଆସିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha,
Oḍiśhā deśhare puṇi hoiba lo jāiphula,
kahe jabana bāhi āsiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichaitika muOdisha, uye panguva iyoyo, masoja mazhinji emarudzi anovenga achapinda mukati meIndia.

Mutsvene Achyutananda Das anonyora mune rimwe remabhuku ake eMalika:

Odia Sloka:

ଏ କଳିଯୁଗରେ ମହାଭାରତଟି ଉତ୍କଳ ଭୂଇଁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳରୁ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପତ୍ତି ହୋଇବ ଠିକେ ଠିକେ ଏହା ଶୁଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

E kaḷiyugare Mahābhārataṭi Utkaḷa bhūiñ pramāṇa,
Utkaḷaru sabu utpatti hoiba ṭhike ṭhike ehā śhuṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yasara yeMahabharata icharwirwa muKali Yuga, iyo ichaitika munyika yeUtkala kana Odisha.

12.3 Hondo iyi ichagara kwenguva yakareba sei?

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akaprofitawo mubuku rake reMalika ‘Amara Jumara Samhita’ kuti hondo iyi yasara yeMahabharata ichagara kwemwedzi 13 yose.

Mahapurush akanyora:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଯେ ରହିଥିବ ଶୁଣ ହେତୁ ଦ୍ୱାରେ,
ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ସେତିକି ବେଳରେ।
ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ,
ସେ ଓଳିକେ ତେର ମାସ ଭୋଗ ଯେ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Olie je rahithiba shuṇa hetu dwāre,
Bhārata juddha hoiba setiki xelare.
Sehi olikare nātha kali shesha hexa,
Se olike tera māsa bhoga je hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichagara kwemwedzi gumi netatu yose, uye nekupedzwa kwehondo iyoyo, simba reKali Yuga panyika ino richapera zvachose.

12.4 Ndiani achatora chikamu kubva kudivi reIndia padanho rekupedzisira?

Vamahapurusha vakanyora kuti muchikamu chekupedzisira chehondo iyi, neparutivi rumwe pachange paine mauto makuru emarudzi anovenga, neparutivi rumwe, varwi vose vane ushingi venguva yeMahabharata nezviuru zvevatsvene, vanhu vakachenjera, nevaprofita vachange varipo pasi pehutungamiri hwaIshe Mukuru Kalki pachake.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mu‘Bhabishyat Malika’ yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଏହି ବେଳେ ଭାରତ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ହାରିଯିବ ତ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଚାରିଆଡ଼େ ତ।
ଭାରତ ନରନାରୀ ସର୍ବେ ଡାକିବେ ହରି,
ରକ୍ଷାକର ପ୍ରଭୁ ନିଶ୍ଚେ ଗଲୁଟି ସରି।
ଆହେ କୁଞ୍ଜବିହାରୀ ବାରେ ଶୁଣ ଗୁହାରି,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ତେଜି ଥରେ ଆସ ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି।
ଦେଖ ନେତ୍ର ଫେଡ଼ିତ ପୁଣ୍ୟଭୂମି ଭାରତ,
ବିଦେଶୀଙ୍କ ହସ୍ତେ ହୁଏ କି ହନ୍ତସନ୍ତ।
ଭାରତର କାଳର ଶୁଣି ସେ ଆଦିମୂଳ,
ବଇକୁଣ୍ଠ ପୁର ତେଜି ହେବେ ବାହାର।
କଳ୍କୀ ଦେବ ନାମ ବହି ସେ ନାରାୟଣ,
ଉତ୍କଳ ଦେଶ କଟକରେ ଦେବେ ଦର୍ଶନ।
ସେହି ବେଳରେ ଜାଣ ଶ୍ରୀକୃଷ୍ଣ ବଳରାମ,
ଭୀମ ସହ ବାହାରିବେ ବୀର ଅର୍ଜୁନ।
ଭୀଷ୍ମ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଆବର ଅଶ୍ୱତ୍ଥାମା ଯେ ବୀର,
କର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଶଲ୍ୟ ଜୟଦ୍ରଥ ହେବେ ବାହାର।
ବେଲାଲସେନ ବୀର ପୁଣି ହେବ ବାହାର,
ବାଳବୀର ଅଭିମନ୍ୟୁ ଘେନି ସଙ୍ଗର।
ବୀର ବବ୍ରୁବାହନ ବାହାରିବଟି ଜାଣ,
ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ଘଟୋତ୍କଚ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଘେନିଣ।
ସର୍ବେ ହୋଇଣ ମେଳି ବିଦେଶୀୟଙ୍କୁ ଦଳି,
ଦେବେ ମୁହୂର୍ତ୍ତକ ମଧ୍ଯେ ଲଗାଇ କଳି।
ମନ୍ତ୍ର ଶକତି ବଳେ ଅପଲକ ନେତ୍ରରେ,
ଚାହିଁ ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଶକ୍ତିକି ଜିଣିବେ ହେଳେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ehibeḷe Bhārata nishche hārijiba ta,
hāhākāra paḍijiba chāriāḍe ta.
Bhārata naranārī sarbe dākibe Hari,
rakhākara prabhu nishche galuṭi sari.
Āhe Kuñjabihārī bāre shuṇa guhāରି,
Baikuṇṭha teji thare āsa Shrī Hari.
Dekha netra pheḍita puṇyabhūmi Bhārata,
bideshīṅka haste hue ki hantasanta.
Bhāratara kāḷara shuṇi se ādimūḷa,
Baikuṇṭhapura teji hebe bāhāra.
Kalkī deba nāma bahi se Nārāyaṇa,
Utkaḷa desha Kaṭakare debe darshana.
Sehibeḷere jāṇa Shrī Kṛṣṇa Baḷarāma,
Bhīma saha bāhāribe bīra Arjuna.
Bhīṣma Droṇa ābara Ashwatthāmā je bīra,
Karṇa Shalya Jayadratha hebe bāhāra.
Belāḷasena bīra puṇi hebe bāhāra,
bāḷabīra Abhimanyu gheni saṅgara.
Bīra Babhrubāhana bāhāribaṭi jāṇa,
Ekalabya Ghaṭotkacha saṅge gheniṇa.
Sarbe hoiṇa meḷi bideshīyaṅku daḷi,
debe muhūrttaka madhye lagāi kaḷi.
Mantra shakti baḷe apalaka netrare,
chāhi bijñāna shaktiki jiṇibe heḷe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panguva yeHondo Yenyika Yetatu, vachiona kurwiswa kwakaitwa India kubva kumativi mana nevavengi vekunze, vanhu veIndia vachavhunduka uye vachachema nekutambudzika. Vasingabatsiriki, vose vachayeuka Ishe uye vachikumbira kuchengetedzwa. Vachati—Haiwa Ishe, inzwa chikumbiro chedu, tiponese, siya Vaikuntha uuye Pasi kuti utichengetedze.

Vachifambiswa nekudanidzira kunonzwisa tsitsi kwevatsvene uye vachiona kupinda kweIndia nemaoko evavengi vekunze, Ishe Mukuru Brahman Narayana (Maha Vishnu) pachake, muchimiro chake cheKalki, achaonekwa muCuttack, Odisha, uye achapa darshan kuvatsvene.

Mahapurush akanyora kuti pamwe naIshe Kalki panguva imwe chete, Bhima, Arjuna, Bhishma, Drona, Ashwatthama, Karna, Shalya, Jayadratha, Barbarika, Abhimanyu, Babhruvahana, Ekalavya, naGhatotkacha, avo vanenge vakatoberekwa, vacharwa kudivi reIndia uye vachakunda marudzi anovenga zvakanyanya. Simba remantra raMwari revarwi ava vane ushingi richaratidza kuva repamusoro pane simba remazuva ano rinoenderana nesainzi revavengi.

Panyaya imwe chete iyi, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora mubuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ପ୍ରସ୍ତାବିତ କଳି ଭାରତ ସରିବ ହେବ ଭାରତ ସମର।
ତିନିଯୁଗ ଯୋଦ୍ଧା ଏକ ଯୁଗରେ ମହାସମର ଲାଗିବ,
କହଇ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ଭକ୍ତ ହେବେ ମୁକ୍ତ ମନୋସ୍କାମନା ପୂରିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Prastābita kaḷi Bhārata sariba heba Bhārata samara.
Tinijuga joddhā eka jugare mahāsamara lāgiba,
kahai Achyuta bhakta hebe mukta manoskāmanā pūriba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yeMahabharata icharwirwa muKali Yuga, muchiitiko icho varwi vane ushingi vemazera matatu apfuura vose vachatora chikamu.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anowedzera kunyora mu‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ସମର ହେବ ଦେଲି ଚେତାଇ,
ସ୍ୱୟଂ ପରଂବ୍ରହ୍ମ ମୂରତି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଜଗତସାଇଁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Oḷie Bhārata samara heba deli chetāi,
swayaṃ Parambrahma mūrati prabhu Jagatasāiñ.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichaitika pasi pehutungamiri hwaIshe Mukuru Brahman Narayana (chimiro cheKalki) pachake.

Panyaya iyi, mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, akanyorawo mubuku rake reMalika ‘Nidagha Nighanta’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ରହି ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହେବ,
ଆଦେଶ ଦେବେଟି ମାଧବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Oḷie rahi juddha heba,
ādeśha debeṭi Mādhaba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, hondo yasara yeMahabharata ichapedzwa, uye Madhava pachake, Ishe Mukuru Kalki, achapa murairo wekutanga hondo iyi.

Mutsvene mukuru weOdisha akaberekwa muzana ramakore rechi20, Thakur Shri Abhiram Paramahansa, akanyorawo panyaya iyi mubuku rake reMalika ‘Baishnaba Gita’:

Odia Sloka:

ତୋପର ତପର ସମର,
ହୋଇବ ଅତି ଭୟଙ୍କର।
ତପକୁ ତୋପ ନ ପାରିବ,
ତପର ବଳ ବେଶୀ ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Topara tapara samara,
hoiba ati bhayaṅkara.
tapaku topa na pāriba,
tapara baḷa beśhī heba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pachange paine hondo inotyisa pakati pesimba remakanoni emarudzi anovenga India nesimba rekuita Tapasya revatsvene nevanhu vakachenjera veIndia vasingaverengeki, muchiitiko icho simba remazuva ano remisile revavengi richapera simba pamberi pesimba reTapasya reIndia, uye pakupedzisira India ichabuda yakunda muhondo iyi huru.

12.5 Hondo iyi ichapedzwa kupi muIndia?

Vamahapurusha vakanyora mumabhuku eMalika kuti kana mauto ekunze asvika kuOdisha, vacharwa munzvimbo dzakasiyana-siyana muOdisha, dzakadai semuBirat Gadi yeCuttack, Choudwar, Jobra, Barabati, neKaliaboda; muPuri; muJajpur; muGohira Tikiri yeBhadrak; muParadip; muBalasore, nezvimwe.

Kunyange zvazvo hondo iyi ichaitika munzvimbo dzose idzi muOdisha, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akataura zvakajeka kuti ichapera zvachose paKhandagiri muBhubaneswar kana Ishe Mukuru pachake aparadza masoja ose emarudzi anovenga neSudarshana Chakra yake.

Mahapurush akanyora mubuku rake reMalika ‘Kalpatika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଡ଼ିଶା ଦେଶରେ ଖଣ୍ଡଗିରି ଠାରେ ବହୁତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ,
ଅନନ୍ତ କେଶରୀ ରୂପ ଧରି ପ୍ରଭୁ ଚକ୍ର ଧରି ବିନାଶିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Oḍiśhā deśhare Khaṇḍagiri ṭhāre bahuta juddha hoiba,
Ananta Keśharī rūpa dhari prabhu chakra dhari bināśhiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pachange paine hondo inotyisa kwazvo paKhandagiri muOdisha. Panzvimbo imwe chete iyoyo, Ishe Mukuru Kalki achaparadza masoja asingaverengeki neSudarshana Chakra yake.

12.6 Chii chichaitika kuburikidza nehondo iyi uye chii chichaitika mushure mayo?

Mu‘Amara Jumara Samhita’, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora ndima ine chikamu chayo chekutanga chinoti:

Odia Sloka:

ସେହି ଓଳିକରେ ନାଥ କଳି ଶେଷ ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Sehi oḷikare nātha kaḷi śheṣha heba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, pakangopedzwa hondo iyi yeMahabharata, simba reKali Yuga richapera.

Zvadaro Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyorawo ndima mubuku rake reMalika ‘Chakada Madana’, mutsara wayo wekutanga uchiti:

Odia Sloka:

ଓଳିଏ ଭାରତ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଯେ ସରିବ ପୃଥିବୀ ଶାନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Oḷie Bhārata juddha je sariba pruthibī śhānta hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nekupedzwa kwehondo yasara yeMahabharata, Pasi richava nerunyararo. Zvadaro mhirizhonga yose Pasi ichapera.

12.7 Tsanangudzo yemamiriro ezvinhu eMasimba Makuru akadai seAmerica neEurope muHondo Yenyika

VaPanchasakha Mahapurushas vakanyora muzvinyorwa zvavo zveMalika kuti Hondo Yenyika Yetatu ichave yakanyanya kuipa zvekuti nyika dzose dzichakanganiswa nayo. Nyika dzose dzinoonekwa nhasi semasimba makuru epasi rose, hondo iyi yekupera kweKali Yuga ichasiya kunyange idzo dzaparadzwa.

Achitsanangura mamiriro ezvinhu eAmerica muhondo iyi, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das akanyora muzvinyorwa zvake zveMalika zvinonzi ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ରାଷ୍ଟ୍ର ଭାଇ ମୂଳୁ ଧ୍ୱଂସ ଯିବ,
ଗର୍ବରେ ଜଳୁଛି ରାଜା ବିଶ୍ୱ ମୁଖ୍ୟ ହେବ।
ଧରାକୁ ମଣୁଛି ସରା କହେ ମୁହିଁ ଶ୍ରେଷ୍ଠ,
ଉଡ଼ାଇବି ପୃଥିବୀକୁ କରେ ମୁଁ ଶପଥ।
କାହାରି ଗର୍ବ ସହି ନ ପାରିବେ ଧାତା,
ଜଳାଇ ଦେବେ ତାହାକୁ ଉଡ଼ାଇବେ ମଥା।
ନ୍ୟୁୟର୍କ ସହର ଧ୍ୱଂସ ପଦା ହୋଇଯିବ,
ହାହାକାର ପଡ଼ିଯିବ ଅଥର୍ବ ନିର୍ବେଦ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Āmerikā rāṣhṭra bhāi mūḷu dhwaṅsa jiba,
garbare jaḷuchi rājā biśhwa mukhya heba.
dharāku maṇuchi sarā kahe muiñ śhreṣhṭha,
uḍāibi pruthibīku kare muñ śhapatha.
kāhāri garba sahi na pāribe dhātā,
jaḷāi debe tāhāku uḍāibe mathā.
New York sahara dhwaṅsa padā hoijiba,
hāhākāra paḍijiba atharba nirbeda.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nyika inonzi America ichazviona semutungamiri wenyika. Yakanyura mukuzvitutumadza nekudada, ichazvidaidza kuti ndiyo yakanakisisa uye ichatyisidzira kuparadza dzimwe nyika muhondo. Mahapurush akataurawo kuti Ishe Mukuru haatenderi kuzvitutumadza kwemunhu, saka achaparadzawo kudada kweAmerica. Guta reNew York reAmerica richaparadzwa, uye nyika iyoyo ichatarisana nekuparadzwa kwakakwana sechapupu chekuparadzwa kwakanyanya.

Mune ‘Gupta Kheda Malika’ pachayo, Mutsvene Achyutananda Das anonyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ଆମେରିକା ଇଉରୋପ ମହାଦେଶ ଜାଣ,
ସ୍ଥିତି ହରାଇବ ତାର ବିଷାକ୍ତ ପବନ।
ସବୁରି ଧ୍ୱଂସର କାରଣ ଆମେରିକା ଜାଣ,
ନିଶ୍ଚିହ୍ନ ହୋଇବ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ଗର୍ବି ଅଟେ ପୁଣ।
ନିଜର ଇଜ୍ଜତ ପୃଥିବୀ ପରେ ଦେଖାଇଲା,
ଯୁଦ୍ଧକୁ ତ ଜୀବନରେ ସିଦ୍ଧହସ୍ତ କଲା।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Āmerikā Iuropa mahādesha jāṇa,
Sthiti harāiba tāra biṣākta pabana.
Saburi dhwaṃsara kāraṇa Āmerikā jāṇa,
Nishchinha hoiba juddhe garbi aṭe puṇa.
Nijara ijjata pṛthwī pare dekhāilā,
Juddhaku ta jībanare siddhahasta kalā.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, chikonzero cheHondo Yenyika Yetatu ichave nyika inonzi ‘America’. Nyika iyoyo ichasimbisa rukudzo rwayo nekudada kwayo pamusoro peNyika yose uye ichawana unyanzvi mukurwa hondo. Mahapurush akanyorawo kuti nekuda kwekudada uku, zita rayo nemucherechedzo wayo zvichadzimwa muHondo Yenyika Yetatu. Mushure mehondo, mamiriro ekunze ikoko achave ane chepfu, uye nekuda kweizvozvo, vanhu vasingaverengeki vachafa mumakondinendi eAmerica neEurope.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mu‘Bhabishyat Malika’ yake:

Odia Sloka:

ଶେଷେ ଇଉରୋପ ଦେଶ ଯୁଦ୍ଧେ ହୋଇବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ,
ବଞ୍ଚି ରହିବେ କେବଳ ଭକ୍ତ ଅଂଶ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Śheṣhe Iuropa deśha juddhe hoiba dhwaṅsa,
bañchi rahibe kebaḷa bhakta aṅśha.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, muhondo iyi, kondinendi yeEurope ichaparadzwa zvachose. Kunze kwevatsvene vaIshe, hapana mumwe achapona munyika idzodzo.

Mu‘Jaiphula Malika’, Mutsvene Achyutananda Das anonyora:

Odia Sloka:

ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଉତ୍ତର ଦେଶ,
ହାଡ଼ ମାଳ ହୋଇଯିବ ବିଶେଷ ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ମହୀ ଦୁଲୁ ଦୁଲୁ ହେବ ଧ୍ୱଂସ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Paśhchima uttara deśha,
hāḍa māḷa hoijiba biśheṣha lo jāiphula,
mahī dulu dulu heba dhwaṅsa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, nyika dziri kumadokero nekuchamhembe dzichaparadzwa muHondo Yenyika.

Achyutananda Das anonyora zvakare mu‘Jaiphula Malika’ pachayo:

Odia Sloka:

ଜର୍ମାନୀ ରୁଷିଆ ଆଦି,
ଇଟାଲୀ ଜାପାନ ତୁରସ୍କ ଗାଦି ଲୋ ଯାଇଫୁଲ,
ସେହି ସରବେ ହୋଇବେ ରଦ୍ଦି।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Jarmānī Ruṣhiā ādi,
