Itālī Jāpāna Turuska gādi lo jāiphula,
sehi sarabe hoibe raddi.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Germany, Russia, Italy, Japan, neTurkey, nezvimwe, nyika dzichaparadzwa zvakanyanya neHondo Yenyika.

Sezvatatotaura, zviitiko zvose zviri pamusoro zvichanyanya kuitika panguva yeMeena-Shani yoga, kureva, pakati pemakore 2025 na 2028.

Musi waKurume 29, 2025, Saturn yakapinda muchiratidzo chePisces (Meena). Dzimwe nguva ichifamba mune retrograde (Vakri) kana kufamba kumashure uye dzimwe nguva ichifamba mune direct (Margi) kana kufamba kwakananga, Saturn icharamba iri muchiratidzo chePisces kusvika Kukadzi 23, 2028. Zvichakadaro, kubva Chikumi 3, 2027, kusvika Gumiguru 20, 2027, pachave nekufamba kwenguva pfupi kweSaturn muchiratidzo cheAries (Mesha).

Zvino mubvunzo unokosha unomuka pano. Kwapfuura mwedzi inenge 10 kubva Meena-Shani yoga yatanga, asi zviitiko izvi zvinoparadza hazvisati zvatanga. Sei zvakadaro? Chokwadi, njodzi dzinongoitika dzoga dziri kuitika pasi rose, hondo dziri kuitikawo pakati penyika dzakawanda, asi hadzisi kuitika padanho guru sezvakafanotaurwa nePanchasakhas muzvinyorwa zveMalika! Mubvunzo unomuka kakawanda mupfungwa dzedu dzakawanda kuti danho rezviitiko izvi richawedzera riini!

Kuparadzwa Kukuru kuchatanga riini chaizvo? Bhabishya Malika inoti kudii pamusoro penyaya iyi? Malika inotaura zvechokwadi kuti mitambo inoparadza ichaitika muMeena-Shani, asi Malika inotsanangura kutanga kwemitambo iyi inoparadza sechikamu chekupedzisira chegore ra2026 panzvimbo pegore ra2025.

Mumwe wePanchasakhas, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das, akanyora nezvekunetsa kusati kwamboitika pasi rose panguva yeMeena-Shani muzvinyorwa zvake zveMalika uye akapa ruzivo nezvenguva yavo chaiyo. Mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake zveMalika, akanyora:

Odia Sloka:

ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଅକାଳେ ମଡ଼କ ପଡ଼ିବ ସେହି ସମୟରେ ଜାଣ।
ଭୂମିକମ୍ପରେ କମ୍ପିବ ସକଳ ମେଦିନୀ,
କାଳ ଭୈରବୀ ଛାଡ଼ିବେ ଶୂନ୍ୟରେ ଯେ ରଡ଼ି।
ମୀନ ଶନି ଯୋଗରେ ଯେ ତୃତୀୟ ଚରଣ,
ଜଳ ପ୍ରଳୟ ହୋଇବ ଭଲେ ତୁହି ଜାଣ।
ହୁତୁ ହୁତୁ ହୋଇ ଜଳୁଥିବେ ହିଙ୍ଗୁଳା ମାତିବେ,
ବଡ଼ ବଡ଼ କୋଠା ଘର ଜାଳି ଯେ ଦେବେ।
ଚୋର ଟାଉଟର ସଂଖ୍ୟା ପ୍ରବଳ ବଢ଼ିବ,
ସେଥିପାଇଁ ଦେବୀଙ୍କର କୋପେ ନାଶ ଯିବ।
ନ ଦେଖିଲା ନେତ୍ର ପୁଣି ସେ ସମୟେ ଦେଖିବ,
ତ୍ରାହି ତ୍ରାହି ବୋଲି ପୁଣି ଡାକ ଛାଡୁଥିବ।
ହରି ଆଶ୍ରା କର ତୁହି ନିସ୍ତାର ପାଇବୁ,
ଦାସ ଯଶୋବନ୍ତ କହେ ବିହନ ହୋଇବୁ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Mīna śhani yogare je dwitīya charaṇa,
akāḷe maḍaka paḍiba sehi samayare jāṇa.
bhūmikampare kampiba sakaḷa medinī,
kāḷa bhairabī chāḍibe śhūnyare je raḍi.
mīna śhani yogare je tṛutīya charaṇa,
jaḷa pralaya hoiba bhale tuhi jāṇa.
hutu hutu hoi jaḷuthibe hiṅguḷā mātibe,
baḍa baḍa koṭhā ghara jāḷi je debe.
chora ṭāuṭara saṅkhyā prabala baḍhiba,
sethipāiñ debīṅkara kope nāśha jiba.
na dekhilā netra puṇi se samaye dekhiba,
trāhi trāhi boli puṇi ḍāka chāḍuthiba.
Hari āśhrā kara tuhi nistāra pāibu,
dāsa Yaśhobanta kahe bihana hoibu.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mumakore maviri nehafu eMeena-Shani yoga, panguva yechikamu chechipiri (makore 2026-2027) nechikamu chechitatu (makore 2027-2028), rufu rusingatarisirwi ruchaitika muhuwandu hukuru, uye nyonganyonga ichatonga kwose kwose. Kudengenyeka kwenyika kwakanyanya kuchaitika, Nyika yose ichadedera. Pachave nekuchema kweBhairavi, kureva kuti, Amai Bhairavi vachangoerekana vaita ruzha runotyisa kubva pasina. Zvinotyisa zvemafashamo emvura zvichaonekwa paNyika. Nekuda kwehasha dzaAmai Hingula, dzimba huru, dzimba dzemadzimambo, nezvimwe, zvichapisa zvakanyanya. Mahapurush akanyora kuti vanhu vose vakaipa vachava vanyajambwa vehasha dzaAmai Vamwari. Panguva iyoyo, vanhu vachaona zvose zvavasina kumbokwanisa kuona kusvika panguva iyoyo, kureva kuti, zvose zvichaitika nenzira isati yamboitika. Vanhu vose vachachemera ngoni. Avo vanozvipira kutsoka dzaIshe Shri Hari uye vachitora pokugara pake, Mahapurush akati ndivo chete vachawana ruponeso kubva pane zvose izvi uye vachava mbeu dzeSatya Yuga.

Muvhesi iri pamusoro, Mahapurush Yashovanta Das akataura kuti mitambo iyi inoparadza ichatanga zvine mutsindo kubva pachikamu chechipiri cheMeena-Shani yoga, kureva, makore 2026-2027.

Munguva iyi yeKali Yuga, Mahapurushas vakawanda vakazvarwa muOdisha nguva nenguva. Muzana ramakore rechi20, Mahapurush akazvarwa muOdisha akazivisa zviprofita zvakataurwa naIshe Mukuru pachake, Amai Yogamaya, nevamwari nevanamwarikadzi kuti zvibatsire vatsvene. Mahapurush akanyora:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ,
ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି ଆଦେଶ ଦେବୀଙ୍କୁ ହେବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Binśha chabisha sāla sariba,
Śhrī Hari ādeśha debīṅku heba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mushure mekunge chikamu chekupedzisira chegore ra2026 chasvika kana gore ra2026 rapera, Ishe Kalki vachapa murairo kumasimba matsvene ose (Amai Ashta Chandis, 64 Yoginis, mapfumbamwe crore Katyayanis) kuti atange kuparadza.

Mahapurush akabva anyorawo:

Odia Sloka:

ସମୟ ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ସାଲ ସରିବ ଯେବେ,
ସବୁ ଦେବା ଦେବୀ ବାହାରେ ବିଶ୍ୱଯୁଦ୍ଧ କରିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Samaya binśha chabisha sāla sariba jebe,
sabu debā debī bāhāre biśhwajuddha karibe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mushure megore ra2026, vamwari nevanamwarikadzi vose vacharidza hwamanda yehondo yeDeva-Asura. Kutanga kwehondo (Hondo Yenyika) kuchaitika munyika.

Zvakakosha kucherechedza kuti mumavhesi ose ari pamusoro, Mahapurush akanyora zvakajeka “Gore ra2026”.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora muzvinyorwa zvake zveMalika zvinonzi ‘Chakada Madana’:

Odia Sloka:

ଛଅ ସାତ ମିଶାଇଣ ତେର ଯୋଗ କର,
ଏହିଠାରୁ ଘୋର ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ଭାରତ ବକ୍ଷର।
ସେ ବେଳେ ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟଟି ହେବ ଜାଣ,
ଛିନ୍ନଛତ୍ର ଏ ପୃଥିବୀ କହିଲି ପ୍ରମାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chha-a sāta mishaiṇa tera yoga kara,
ehi thāru ghora juddha bhārata bakṣhara.
se beḷe khaṇḍa pralayaṭi heba jāṇa,
chhinnachhatra e pruthibī kahili pramāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, sanganisa nhanhatu (6) nenomwe (7) wobva wawedzera gumi netatu (13) kune huwandu, kureva, 6+7+13=26, kubva ipapo hondo yakanyanya ichatanga muIndia. Mushure menguva iyoyo, zviitiko zvekuparara kwediki (Khanda Pralaya) zvichaitika uye Nyika ichatanga kuparadzwa.

Zvakakosha kucherechedza kuti pano Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, kusiyana nemavhesi maviri ari pamusoro, haana kunyora “Gore ra2026” asi akangonyora 26. Zvichida pano, ne26, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das ari kureva gore ra2026 chete.

Mahapurush akazvarwa muzana ramakore rechi20 anonyora zvakare:

Odia Sloka:

ବିଂଶ ଛବିଶ ପରେ ତ କରିବୁ ବିଚାର,
ମନେ ନ କରିବୁ ଯଦି ଯିବୁ ଯମପୁର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Binśha chabisha pare ta karibu bichāra,
mane na karibu jadi jibu Yamapura.

Zvinoreva:
Muvhesi iri pamusoro, Amai Yogamaya vanoti mushure megore ra2026, munhu wose achatongwa. Vose vasingatendi, vakaipa, vatadzi, nezvimwe, vachaparadzwa.

Naizvozvo, zvinofanira kunzwisiswa zvakajeka kuti mitambo inoparadza yaKalki inotsanangurwa neMalika ichaitika panguva yeMeena-Shani yoga pachayo, asi izvi hazvizoitiki mugore ra2025 asi zvichatanga kubva muchikamu chekupedzisira cha2026.

Kuti mitambo iyi inoparadza ichagara makore mangani, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora pamusoro penyaya iyi muzvinyorwa zvake zveMalika zvinonzi ‘Kaliyuga Malika’:

Odia Sloka:

ଚବିଶ ଠାବରୁ ଅଣତିରିଶ ଯାଏ ପ୍ରମାଦ ଅଛି ଅପାର,
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଅଙ୍କରେଟି ହୋ ଗରୁଡ଼ ମହାକଳ୍କୀ ଅବତାର।
ଅଣତିରିଶ ଭିତରେଟି ଗରୁଡ଼ ସବୁ ଉତ୍ପାତ ସରିବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Chabiśha ṭhābaru aṇatiriśha jāe pramāda achi apāra,
aṇatiriśha aṅkareṭi ho Garuḍa mahā Kaḷkī abatāra.
aṇatiriśha bhitareṭi Garuḍa sabu utpāta sariba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mitambo inoparadza ichatanga kubva panhamba 24 uye ichagara kusvika panhamba 29. Mitambo yose inoparadza ichapera panhamba 29.

Cherechedza kuti muvhesi iri pamusoro, Mahapurush akakurukura nhamba 24, kwete gore ra2024. Asi Mahapurush akajekesa kuti mitambo iyi inoparadza, ichitanga kubva panhamba 24 uye ichigara kusvika panhamba 29, ichaenderera mberi kwemakore 6. Mahapurush haana kujekesa kuti izvi zvichatanga riini uye zvichapera riini, asi akataura kuti kubva panhamba 24 kusvika panhamba 29, kureva, kwemakore 6, mitambo iyi inoparadza ichaenderera mberi.

Sezvo kubva pamavhesi ari pamusoro tatova nazvo kuti mitambo iyi inoparadza ichatanga kubva muchikamu chekupedzisira chegore ra2026, tinogona ikozvino kusvika nyore pamhedziso iyi kuti nhamba 24 ye‘Kaliyuga Malika’ iri kuratidza gore ra2026-2027. Saizvozvo, nhamba 29 yeiyi nhevedzano imwe chete yeiyi Malika text iri kureva gore ra2031-2032.

Mahapurush Achyutananda Das, kuburikidza neMalika Pothi yake, akatsanangura zvakare –

Odia Sloka:

ଏହା ତ ହେଉଛି କାଳର ଭୟ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ତହିଁ,
ଏହା ତ କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳଟି ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କଳ୍କୀ ଖେଳ କରୁଅଛନ୍ତି ହେତୁ ପ୍ରମାଣ,
ଆଉ ବା ଏକତିରିଶ ସେ ତ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ।
ମହାମାରୀ ସେ ତ ଆସିବ କହେ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ ଆସ,
ତେତିଶି ମସିହା ସୁଦ୍ଧାଟି କହେ ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ।
ଠା ଠିକଣା ଯେ ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କହି,
ଏଭଳି ସମୟ ଆସିବ ଦାସ ଦେଲି ବୁଝାଇ।
କାହାରି ପତା ରହିବ ନାହିଁ ହେତୁରେ ଜାଣ,
ବୈଜ୍ଞାନୀମାନେ ବାଟଟି ପାଇବେ ନାହିଁରେ ଜାଣ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Ehā ta heuchhi kāḷara bhaya Achyutī tahiñ,
ehā ta Kaḷkī kheḷaṭi dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kaḷkī kheḷa karuachhanti hetu pramāṇa,
āu bā ekatiriśa se ta heture jāṇa.
Mahāmārī se ta āsiba kahe Achyutī āsa,
Tetiriśhi masihā suddhāṭi kahe dīna Achyuta.
Ṭhā ṭhikaṇā je rahiba nāhiñ Achyutī kahi,
ebhaḷi samaya āsiba dāsa deli bujhāi.
Kāhāri patā rahiba nāhiñ heture jāṇa,
Baijñānīmāne bāṭaṭi pāibe nāhiñ re jāṇa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe Kalki vachaita mutambo Wake (mitambo Yake inoparadza). Mitambo iyi inoparadza ichaenderera mberi kusvika gore ra2033. Zvichakadaro, denda dzakawanda dzichauya, izvo zvichakonzera nyonganyonga munharaunda yevanhu. Nguva ichasvika apo pachave nenyonganyonga kwose kwose. Vanhu vose vachave vari mumamiriro ezvinhu akaipa. Kunyange ruzivo rwesainzi haruzobatsiri.

Zvisinei, Achyutananda Das akataurawo mu‘Kaliyuga Malika’ yake kuti zviitiko zvikuru zvinoparadza zvichapera panhamba 27.

Mahapurush akanyora –

Odia Sloka:

ସତାଇଶ ଅଙ୍କେ ଦକ୍ଷିଣ ଦିଗରୁ ସମୁଦ୍ର ଆସିବ ମାଡ଼ି,
ଉତ୍ତରରୁ ଗଙ୍ଗା ଉଛୁଳି ପଡ଼ିଣ ତହିଁ ସଙ୍ଗେ ଦେବ ଧାଡ଼ି।
ପଶ୍ଚିମ ଦିଗରୁ ଜଳ ସ୍ରୋତ ଏକ ଆସି ତହିଁ ଯିବ ମିଶି,
ଖଣ୍ଡ ପ୍ରଳୟର ସୃଜନା କରିବ ପାପୀ ତହିଁ ଯିବେ ଭାସି।
ପୁଣ୍ୟାତ୍ମାମାନଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱୟଂ ବଳଦେବ ଉଦ୍ଧାର କରି ରଖିବେ,
ଭାରତର ଗିରି ପର୍ବତ ପାହାଡ଼େ ନେଇ ଯତନେ ପାଳିବେ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Satāisha aṅke dakhina digaru samudra āsiba māḍi,
uttararu Gaṅgā uchhuḷi paḍiṇa tahiñ saṅge deba dhāḍi.
Pashchima digaru jala srota eka āsi tahiñ jiba mishi,
khaṇḍa praḷayara sṛujanā kariba pāpī tahiñ jibe bhāsi.
Puṇyātmāmānaṅku swayaṃ Baḷadeba uddhāra kari rakhibe,
Bhāratara giri parbata pāhāḍe nei jatane pāḷibe.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, panhamba 27, mafungu egungwa achauya kubva kuChamhembe uye achasangana nerwizi rweGanges rwunofashukira kubva kuChamhembe. Panguva imwe chete, mafungu akanyanya etsunami achauyawo kubva kuMadokero uye achakonzera kuparadzwa. Mukuparara kwediki uku, vatadzi vasingaverengeki vachafa. Vose vari vatsvene nemweya yakanaka, Baladev Pachake vachavanunura uye vachavachengeta zvakachengeteka pamakomo nemakomo eIndia.

Zvinoenderana neMahapurush, iyi ichave njodzi yekupedzisira pakati penjodzi huru dzichaitika panhamba 27, kureva, mugore ra2029-2030 zvinoenderana nekuverenga kwataurwa pamusoro.

Zvino ngatiedzei kunzwisisa nguva iyi nemamwe mavhesi. Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora mu ‘Bhabishyata Malika’ –

Odia Sloka:

ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ସତୁରି ଯାଏ ଠୁଳ,
ଲାଗି ରହିବ ତୃତୀୟ ମହାସମର।
ପାଞ୍ଚ ବର୍ଷ କାଳ ଯୁଦ୍ଧ ହୋଇବ ଘୋର,
ଚମକ ପଡିବ ସ୍ଵର୍ଗ ମର୍ତ୍ତ୍ୟ ପାତାଳ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Pāñcha barṣha kāḷa saturi jāe ṭhuḷa,
lāgi rahiba tṛutīya mahāsamara.
pāñcha barṣha kāḷa juddha hoiba ghora,
chamaka paḍiba swarga martya pātāḷa.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Hondo Yenyika Yetatu ichagara kwemakore mashanu. Pachava nehondo inotyisa kwemakore mashanu; Denga, Pasi, nePasi Pese zvichadedera.

Achyutananda Das ji anonyora mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake zveMalika –

Odia Sloka:

ଶ୍ରୀ ହରି କହନ୍ତି ବାସବ ଶୁଣ,
ଚବିଶ ଠାରୁ ସତେଇଶ ଜାଣ।
ଏଥି ଭିତରେ ଯେ ହୋଇବ ଗୋଳ,
କାହାରି ନ ରହିବ ଥଳକୁଳ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Śhrī Hari kahanti Bāsaba śhuṇa,
chabiśha ṭhāru sataiśha jāṇa.
ethi bhitare je hoiba goḷa,
kāhāri na rahiba thaḷakuḷa.

Zvinoreva:
Muvhesi riri pamusoro, Ishe Shri Krishna, vachitaura naIndra, vanoti pachava nehondo inotyisa kubva panhamba 24 kusvika panhamba 27.

Zviri pachena kubva pamavhesi maviri akanyorwa pamusoro kuti hondo huru kana Hondo Yenyika Yetatu ichagara kwemakore anenge 4-5. Kureva kuti, ichatanga muna 2026-2027 uye ichapera muna 2029-2030.

Naizvozvo, kuparadzwa kukuru kwekupedzisira kwakataurwa naMahapurush mu ‘Kaliyuga Malika’ panhamba 27 kana gore ra2029-2030 kuri kuenderana zvakakwana nemavhesi maviri akapiwa pamusoro.

Kubva pane zvakakurukurwa pamusoro, kana mhedziso ikatorwa muchidimbu:

Gore ra2026 – 2027: Nguva yekuparadzwa ichatanga.
Gore ra2029 – 2030: Zviitiko zvakanyanya kuipa uye zvinoparadza zvichapera.
Gore ra2031 – 2032: Zviitiko zvese zvinoparadza zvichapera.

Cherechedza: Zviitiko zvikuru zvinoparadza zvinopera panosvika gore ra2029 zvinosanganisira zvese zvinowanzokurukurwa, zvakadai se – kudengenyeka kwenyika kukuru, kuparadzwa kwemvura kunotyisa, njodzi dzemhepo, njodzi dzemoto, kudonha kwemeteorites, zvirwere-denda, hondo yeIndia nenyika dzinovenga, Hondo Yenyika, hondo yeKali-Mahabharata, kuputika kweHirakud nemamwe madhamu, rima kwemazuva manomwe nehusiku hunomwe, Bhairavi Dak, nezvimwe.

13 Kutanga kweSatyayuga

Kuti upinde muSatyayuga, zvakakosha kuti munhu afambe munzira yeDharma. Anofanira kugara achitaura chokwadi uye achitevera chokwadi chete; haafaniri kutora nzira yekupenga asi nzira yerunyararo. Anofanira kuva nemanzwiro erudo, mutsa, netsitsi kune zvisikwa zvese. Haafaniri kukonzera kurwadziwa kune chero chisikwa kuburikidza nepfungwa dzake, kutaura, nemaitiro. Anofanira kuva anoshuvira zvakanaka kune zvisikwa zvese pasina chikonzero, maitiro ake anofanira kuva ekuregerera vamwe, anofanira kuva nemaitiro akareruka uye akatwasuka, haafaniri kuchengeta ruvengo kune chero munhu, uye anofanira kuziva kuchengeta pfungwa dzake dziri pasi pekutonga. Anofanira kuva mutsvene waMwari, agara achifunga nezvaMwari, achipedza nguva achiimba nziyo, achidana mazita aMwari, achipinda misangano yemweya (Satsang), achiita minamato yemazuva ese katatu (Trisandhya), achitevera magwaro matsvene, uye achiverenga Shrimad Bhagavat Mahapurana (inozivikanwa seMahapurana pakati pePuranas gumi nemasere) zuva rega rega. Anofanira kuva anoverenga kana kunzwa mitambo yaMwari. Anofanira kudya chikafu chakachena uye cheSattvic (chisina nyama). Pamwe chete nekuita mabasa ake epanyika, anofanirawo kuita Yoga, kudanidzira mazita, nezvimwe. Anofanira kugara achipa ruremekedzo kuvabereki vake nevakuru, kuratidza rudo kuvadiki, uye kugadzira manzwiro ehushamwari nevamwe vake.

Mumwe nemumwe wedu anoshuvira kupinda muSatyayuga nekupukunyuka zviitiko zvinoparadza zviri kuuya, njodzi dzinongoitika dzega, neHondo Yenyika, uye nekugamuchira nyasha dzaIshe Kalki. Asi kupinda muSatyayuga kuri nyore kudaro here? Kwete. Kupinda muSatyayuga hakusi sekugadzira mushonga mugirazi wounwa kuitira kuti ani nani anounwa nenzira iyi achawana chokwadi kupinda muSatyayuga!

Kuti upinde muSatyayuga, munhu wese anofanira kuunza shanduko huru muhupenyu hwake. Zvakakosha kuti pamwe chete nekuita mabasa epanyika nemabasa, atange kurarama hupenyu hwemweya.

Achitsanangura kuti nguva iri kuuya ichave sei kune vatsvene, Mahapurush Achyutananda Das anonyora muchinyorwa chake cheMalika ‘Chakada Madana’ –

Odia Sloka:

କଳିଯୁଗ ହଟି ଯାଇ ସତ୍ୟର ପ୍ରକାଶ,
ଭକତମାନେ ରହିବେ ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଉଲ୍ଲାସ।
ବହୁ କଷ୍ଟ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି ଭକତ ମୋହର,
ଅଳ୍ପ ଦିନରେ ଦୁଃଖ କଷ୍ଟ ଯିବ ଦୂର।
ଏ କଳି ହଟିବ ଯୁଗ ଅନନ୍ତ ହୋଇବ,
ଦୀନ ଅଚ୍ୟୁତୀ କାମନା ପୂରଣ ହୋଇବ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Kaḷiyuga haṭi jāi satyara prakāśha,
bhakatamāne rahibe atyanta ullāsa.
bahu kaṣhṭa pāichhanti bhakata mohara,
alpa dinare duḥkha kaṣhṭa jiba dūra.
e kaḷi haṭiba juga ananta hoiba,
dīna Achyutī kāmanā pūraṇa hoiba.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, vatsvene vakatambura zvakanyanya muKaliyuga, asi mushure menguva pfupi apo Kaliyuga inopera uye chiedza chenguva isingaperi (Ananta Yuga) chinoonekwa, vatsvene vachafara zvakanyanya. Ipapo marwadzo avo ese nemarwadzo zvichaenda.

Mutsvene Bhima Bhoi anonyora mune chimwe chezvinyorwa zvake zveMalika ‘Stuti Chintamani’ –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଷ୍ଟ ନିବାରିବେ ସନ୍ଥଙ୍କୁ ରଖିବେ ପାଳିବେ ଜନ ପରଜା,
କଳିଯୁଗ ଶେଷେ ସତ୍ୟ ଉପୁଜିବ ଅନାଦି ହୋଇବେ ରାଜା।
ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ ଭକତ ସକଳ ହରି ହରିବେ ଯେ ଦୁଃଖ,
ହରିବୋଲ ହୁଳହୁଳି ଶବଦରେ ପୂରିବ ଏହି ତ୍ରୈଲୋକ୍ୟ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Duṣṭa nibāribe santhaṅku rakhibe pāḷibe jana parajā,
Kaḷiyuga sheṣe satya upujiba Anādi hoibe rājā.
Haraṣa hoibe bhakata sakaḷa hari haribe je duḥkha,
Haribola huḷuhuḷi shabadare pūriba ehi traiḷokya.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, Ishe Mukuru vachaparadza vakaipa uye vachadzivirira vatsvene nevatsigiri, uye vachitarisira vanhu vari Pasi. Kaliyuga ichapera uye Satyayuga ichasvika, uye Ishe Kalki Pachavo vachava mutongi wenyika yose. Ishe vachabvisa marwadzo evatsvene vose uye vachavachengeta vachifara. Vatsvene vachafara, uye nyika dzose nhatu dzichazadzwa nekudanidzira kwezita 'Hari'.

13.1 Satyayuga ichatanga riini?

Bhabishya Malika inotaura kuti Ananta Yuga kana Adi Satyayuga ichatanga mugore ra2033. Mahapurush akanyora muchinyorwa chake cheMalika –

Odia Sloka:

ଦୁଇ ଡାହାଣରେ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ରଖ,
ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ସତ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ହୋଇବ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ଯବନ ଗଣ ବିନାଶଟି ଧ୍ରୁବ,
ଦୁଇ ଶୂନ ଏକ ତିନି ଅଙ୍କରୁ ଆରମ୍ଭ।
ଅନ୍ୟାୟ ଅସତ୍ୟ ମାନ ହଟି ଯାଉଥିବ,
ଅଧର୍ମର ନାଶେ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରଭାବୀ ହୋଇବ।
ତେତିଶିରେ ଚାରି ପାଦ ଧର୍ମ ପ୍ରକାଶିବ,
ସତ୍ୟ ଶାନ୍ତି ଦୟା କ୍ଷମା ତହୁଁ ବିକଶିବ।
ସରଳ ସଜ୍ଜନ ସାଧୁ ସନ୍ଥ ଜନ ମେଳ,
ହେବା ପାଇଁ ଏ ଧରା ରେ ଆସିଗଲା ବେଳ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Dui ḍāhāṇare shūna eka tini rakha,
sampūrṇṇa satya prakāsha hoiba pratyakṣa.
Mlechha jabana gaṇa bināshaṭi dhruba,
dui shūna eka tini aṅkaru ārambha.
Anyāya asatya māna haṭi jāuthiba,
adharmara nāshe dharma prabhābhī hoiba.
Tetishire chāri pāda dharma prakāshiba,
satya shānti dayā kṣamā tahuñ bikaśhiba.
Saraḷa sajjana sādhu santha jana meḷa,
hebā pāiñ e dharā re āsigalā beḷa.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush akanyora kuti kubva mugore ra2013, kuparadzwa kweKaliyuga uye chiedza cheChokwadi zvichatanga zvishoma nezvishoma. Kubva panguva iyoyo zvichienda mberi, kuparadzwa kwevakaipa nevatadzi kuchatanga. Simba rekunyepa, kusaruramisira, uye kusarurama richaderera, uye simba reDharma richatanga kuwedzera. Mahapurush akanyorawo kuti nenzira iyi, mugore ra2033, chiedza chembiru dzose ina dzeDharma chichaonekwa, zvinoreva kuti kusimbiswa kwakakwana kweDharma kuchaitika. Kubva panguva iyoyo zvichienda mberi, hunhu hweChokwadi, Runyararo, Mutsa, neKuregerera zvichatanga kukura zvakakwana. Pachava nemubatanidzwa wevanhu vose vakanaka, vane mutsa uye vatsvene, vatsvene, varume vakachenjera.

Mahapurush anonyora zvakare –

Odia Sloka:

ଅତ୍ୟନ୍ତ ଗୁପତୁ ଗୁପତ, ତେତିଶି ଅଙ୍କେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ଭକତମାନେ ଯେ ଜାଣିବେ, ସମସ୍ତେ ହରଷ ହୋଇବେ।
ସଂସାର ଉଶ୍ଵାସ ହୋଇବ, କାଳର ଭୟ ଯେ ଛାଡିବ।
ଅନ୍ନ ବସ୍ତ୍ରରେ ସୁଖୀ ହେବେ, ଧର୍ମ ପଥକୁ ଆଚୋରିବେ।
ଶସ୍ୟ ଯେ ବହୁତ ହୋଇବ, ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମୀ କୃପାରୁ ସବୁ ହେବ।
ସତ୍ୟରେ ସର୍ବେ ଆତଜାତ, ସତ୍ୟ କଳି ଯେ ହେବ ଖ୍ୟାତ।
ପୁରାଣ ଭାଗବତ ମାନ, କରିବେ ଗାନ ସଂକୀର୍ତ୍ତନ।
କଳି କଳ୍ମଷ ନାଶ ଯିବ, ଅଚ୍ୟୁତ ବାକ୍ୟ ନ ଟଳିବ।
କହନ୍ତି ବ୍ରହ୍ମାଣ୍ଡ କରତା, ଅନନ୍ତ ଯୁଗର ବ୍ୟବସ୍ଥା।
ମ୍ଲେଚ୍ଛ ନଥିବେ ଏକ ଜାଣ, ଭକ୍ତ ଭଗବାନ ମିଳନ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Atyanta gupatu gupata, Tetishi aṅke heba khyāta.
Bhakatamāne je jāṇibe, samaste haraṣa hoibe.
Saṃsāra ushwāsa hoiba, kāḷara bhaya je chhāḍiba.
Anna bastrare sukhī hebe, dharma pathaku āchoribe.
Shasya je bahuta hoiba, Laxmī kṛpāru sabu heba.
Satyare sarbe ātajāta, satya kaḷi je heba khyāta.
Purāṇa Bhāgabata māna, karibe gāna saṅkīrttana.
Kaḷi kalmaṣa nāsha jiba, Achyuta bākya na ṭaliba.
Kahanti Brahmāṇḍa karatā, ananta jugara byabasthā.
Mlechha nathibe eka jāṇa, bhakta Bhagavān milana.

Zvinoreva:
Mahapurush akati iyi inyaya yakavanzika zvakanyanya uye ichaitika panhamba 33 (gore ra2033). Vatsvene chete ndivo vachakwanisa kuziva izvi. Vachafara zvakanyanya vachiziva izvi. Nyika ichasunungurwa kubva muchivi; hapazovi nekutya kana kuvhiringidzika mupfungwa dzemunhu. Munhu wese achafara, munhu wese achava nechikafu chakakwana nembatya, munhu wese achatevera nzira yeDharma. Ipapo pachava nekukohwa kwakanaka kwazvo uye goho, uye izvi zvichava nekuda kwenyasha dzaAmai Lakshmi. Munhu wese achafamba munzira yeChokwadi; nguva isingaperi ichasvika. Munhu wese achaverenga Puranas neBhagavat uye achiita nziyo nekudanidzira. Kaliyuga ichaparadzwa zvachose; chirevo ichi hachizokundikani zvachose. Mahapurush akawedzera kuti iyi ichava nguva isingaperi uye iyi ichava hurongwa hwenguva isingaperi (Adya Satyayuga). Hapazovi nemunhu asina kururama, akaipa, kana mlechha Pasi. Vatsvene chete ndivo vachasara uye vose vachabatana naMwari.

Mumwe Mahapurush akazvarwa muzana ramakore rechi20 (watakakurukura kare), achitaura Ishe Mukuru, Amai Yogamaya, nevamwari nevamwarikadzi, akanyora –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତିନି ଚାରି ତିନି ଧାଡିରେ ତ ଜାଣ,
ଏହି ଦିନ ଠାରୁ ହେବ ସତ୍ୟର ନିୟମ।
ଗୁରୁବାର ଦିନ ଥିବ ଶୁଣୁଥାଅ ନର,
ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ସତ୍ୟ ଭୋଗ ତ ଆମର।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Tini tini chāri tini dhāḍire ta jāṇa,
ehi dina thāru heba satyara niyama.
Gurubāra dina thiba śhuṇuthāa nara,
sehi dinathāru satya bhoga ta amara.

Zvinoreva:
Pano Mahapurush ari kukumbira kunyora “3” kanokwana kana kutevedzana. Kureva kuti 3333. Mahapurush anoti kubva zuva iri zvichienda mberi, Satyayuga ichatanga Pasi. Pano ne3333, Mahapurush anoreva – zuva rechitatu, mwedzi wechitatu, nemakore makumi matatu nematatu, kureva, Kurume 3, 2033. Mahapurush anowedzera kunyora kuti zuva iroro richava China uye Satyayuga ichatanga kubva pazuva iroro.

Mahapurush akanyora zvakare –

Odia Sloka:

ତିନି ତାରିଖ ତ ତିନି ମାସ ବିଂଶ ଶାଳ,
ତେତିଶ ଅଙ୍କ ତ ପରେ ସତ୍ୟ ସତ୍ୟ ବୋଲ।

Kunyora kwemabhii:

Tini tārikha ta tini māsa biṃśha śhāḷa,
tetishā aṅka ta pare satya satya bola.

Zvinoreva:
Kureva kuti, mushure mezuva rechitatu remwedzi wechitatu wenhamba makumi matatu nematatu yegore rechimakumi maviri, kureva, Kurume 3, 2033, Satyayuga ichauya.

Naizvozvo, hapana mubvunzo zvino kuti nguva itsva (Ananta Yuga kana Adi Satyayuga) ichatanga mugore ra2033. Ichava nguva yevatsvene naMwari chete. Mune mamwe mazwi, vatsvene chete ndivo vachakwanisa kupinda munguva ino. Ishe vachadzivirira Pasi neChokwadi, Dharma, neKururamisira, uye vatsvene vose vachaita Chokwadi, Runyararo, Mutsa, neKuregerera muhupenyu hwavo.

14 Pfupiso

Bhabishya Malika muunganidzwa wezvinyorwa zvakawanda zvakanyorwa nePanchasakhas vakazvarwa makore anenge 600 apfuura uye vamwe vatsvene veOdia nevarume vakuru vakazvarwa muzana ramakore rechi17, rechi18, rechi19, nerechi20. Zvinyorwa zveMalika zvakanyorwa zvikuru nePanchasakhas vaIshe Shri Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Ava Panchasakhas vanozviratidza sevanofambidzana naIshe munguva dzose nemazita akasiyana, uye muKaliyugawo, makore anenge 600 apfuura, ava Panchasakhas vakawana pedyo nekuzviratidza kwaIshe, Chaitanya Mahaprabhu. Mazita ava Panchasakhas vakazvarwa muKaliyuga ndi – Achyutananda Das, Balaram Das, Jashobanta Das, Shishu Ananta Das, naJagannath Das.

Panchasakhas vanoonekwa sechikamu chekuzviratidza (Anshavatar) kwaIshe, uye makore anenge 600 apfuura, zvinyorwa zveMalika zvakanyorwa navo chete pakuraira kwaIshe. Chinangwa chega chekunyorwa kwezvinyorwa zveMalika ndicho kugara zvakanaka kwevatsvene – kumutsa pfungwa dzakarara dzevatsvene pakupera kweKaliyuga uye kuvaponesa nekuvatungamira. VaPanchasakhas vakanyora zvakajeka muzvinyorwa zvavo kuti zvinyorwa izvi hazvifaniri kuratidzwa kune chero munhu kunze kwevatsvene. VaPanchasakhas vakatsanangurawo kuti avo chete vachakwanisa kugamuchira mazwi eMalika ndivo vave vatsvene vaIshe kubva pakuzvarwa kwakapfuura nenguva.

Sezvo Malika iri izwi raMahaprabhu Pachake uye yakanyorwa nePanchasakhas chete pakuraira kwaIshe, naizvozvo shoko rega rega reMalika rakaita semutsara wakavezwa mudombo usingagoni kukanganisa uye usingagoni kushandurwa. Hapana chishamiso mune izvi kuti zviprofita zvisingaverengeki zveMalika zvakanyorwa panguva ino zvakaratidza kuva zvechokwadi 100%. Semuenzaniso – chiprofita cherusununguko rweIndia, chiprofita chekuzvarwa kwaGandhiji, chiprofita chekuumbwa kwenyika inonzi Pakistan, chiprofita chekuvakwa kwedhamu reHirakud muOdisha, nezvimwe.

Bhabishya Malika inokurukura zvakadzama nyaya dzakadai sekupera kweKaliyuga, Kalki Avatar, kuparadzwa kwevakaipa nevatadzi, kudzivirirwa kwevatsvene, kusimbiswa kweDharma, kusvika kweAnanta Yuga, nezvimwe. Malika inoti nguva yeKaliyuga, yaive makore 4,32,000, ichaderedzwa kusvika kumakore 5,000 chete nekuda kwezviito zvakasiyana-siyana zvechivi zvakaitwa muKaliyuga. Mushure mekunge makore 5,000 apfuura, Ishe Jagannath vachasiya Nilachala Dham yavo, Puri, uye vachazvarwa mumuviri wemunhu muBiraja Kshetra, Jajpur yeOdisha, uye mushure mezvo vachagara muKhandagiri, Bhubaneswar. Zvinoenderana neMalika, uku kuchava Kalki Avatar waMwari.

Malika inotaura kuti pakupera kweKaliyuga, vatsvene vachawana zviratidzo zvakawanda zvakadaro kubva patemberi yePuri Jagannath nemamwe mamonasteri netemberi dzeIndia, vachiona izvo vachanzwisisa kuti Kaliyuga yapera uye Ishe Kalki vazvarwa. Semuenzaniso, kubhururuka kure kwefuremu huru kubva patemberi yeJagannath, kudonha kunodzokororwa kwematombo kubva patemberi, Nilachakra ichikombama, kugara kunowanzoitika kwegunguo pamusoro petemberi, nezvimwe. Zvinozivikanwa kuti zviratidzo izvi zvese zvakaonekwa nevatsvene mumakore mashoma apfuura.

Malika inonongedza pakuzvarwa kwaIshe Kalki mune rimwe gore pakati pa2005-2008. Zvinoenderana neMalika, chinangwa cheKalki Avatar ndechekuparadza vakaipa, kudzivirira vatsvene, uye kusimbisazve Dharma Pasi. Malika inotaura kuti Yuga-parivartana iyi ichatanga muna 2026-2027 panguva yeMeena-Shani yoga naIshe Kalki uye ichapera zvachose muna 2031-2032. Panguva iyi, zviitiko zvese zvikuru zvinoparadza zvichaitika panosvika gore ra2029.

Malika inotaurawo kuti panguva iyo Ishe vachasimbisa Dharma Pasi, zita raMambo wePuri richava Shri Dibyasingha Deb, zvinoreva kuti mitambo yose inoparadza yaIshe ichaitika panguva yekutonga kwaMambo wePuri Dibyasingha Deb. Zvinoenderana neMalika, mitambo iyi inoparadza ichava zvikuru kuburikidza nekuparadzwa kweZvinhu Zvishanu (Panchabhuta) neHondo Yenyika.

Malika inotaura kuti mushure mekunge zviitiko zvese zvinoparadza zvapera zvachose panosvika gore ra2031-2032, Adi-Satyayuga kana Anantayuga ichatanga mugore ra2033.
